Abstract: We investigate the dissipative dynamics of multilevel quantum systems strongly coupled either to a lossy cavity mode or directly to a bosonic environment. By deriving spectral localization bounds, we establish conditions under which strong system-bath coupling gives rise to a hierarchy of population relaxation timescales. Our approach builds on the reaction-coordinate polaron-transform framework. By mapping the original strong-coupling problem onto an effective weakly dissipative model, we analyze the spectrum of the resulting Liouvillian superoperator through localization bounds. For the generalized V model, we find that strong system-bath coupling gives rise to a bright-dark structure in the effective system-bath coupling operator: a single collective mode remains strongly coupled to the environment, while the remaining modes become progressively dark. Consequently, the dynamics separate into fast and slow sectors and, at finite coupling strengths, develop a hierarchy of population relaxation timescales. Numerical simulations based on both secular and non-secular quantum master equations corroborate the emergence of timescale separation and the pronounced slowing down of dissipative dynamics at strong coupling. Our results reveal a general mechanism underlying anomalously slow relaxation in strongly coupled open quantum systems and provide a route for engineering long-lived states through system-environment interactions.

Abstract: The interface between the quantum and the classical is an intriguing and, at times, hotly contested subject of ongoing research. The quantum regime is characterized by interference, made possible by the superposition principle, while such phenomena are absent in macroscopic, everyday experience. Here, we investigate the link of this absence (or, as we will argue, unobservability) to computational complexity. We show how the assumption that quantum systems cannot solve NP-complete problems efficiently implies that certain formally valid quantum measurements on finite-dimensional systems are unperformable. We study several consequences of this restriction. First, Pauli matrices in an inconveniently transformed basis are a simple example of unobservables. Furthermore, some quantum states are not connected by any physically realizable time evolution. Finally there are quantum states whose coherence cannot be observed, i.e. superpositions of pure quantum states which are indistinguishable from mixtures. We discuss the connection of this phenomenon to the presence of superselection sectors. Our results suggest that the apparent classicality of macroscopic systems may be partly due to limitations on measurements and time evolutions imposed by computational complexity.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子计算机受环境噪声严重限制，现有错误缓解和纠错方法性能开销大，限制了实际应用。电路复制作为一种简单方法曾被忽视，但其低开销特性使其有潜力作为现有方法的补充，降低整体性能开销的同时保留错误缓解能力。

Method: 在真实噪声轮廓下，对QAOA Maxcut问题执行简单电路复制（circuit replication）实验，分析不同复制次数下推断强度（inference strength）均值和标准差的变化。实验涉及小图和大图两类规模。

Result: 小图：平均推断强度降低约21.8%，平均标准差降低约108.8%（相比6次复制）。大图：平均推断强度降低约35.4%，平均标准差降低约20.5%。减少复制次数对小图影响不大，但对大图会降低推断强度，标准差获益类似。

Conclusion: 电路复制在真实噪声环境下具有一定错误缓解效果，尤其适用于深度大、变异高的负载。其作为辅助缓解策略具有潜在实用价值。

Abstract: Current era quantum computers continue to grow in both capability and capacity. Despite these advancements, errors induced by environmental noise severely limit practical applicability. Current research into error mitigation and correction to bridge the gap between current-era quantum computers and the execution of noise-sensitive workloads. These methods have significant performance and resource overheads, thereby greatly limiting the real-world benefits of their use. Circuit replication, as a naive form of error mitigation, is not new and has largely been ignored given the resource constraints of current quantum hardware. However, its simplicity is attractive as a means to supplement modern methods, reducing the overall performance overhead while still preserving error-mitigation capabilities. In this paper, we profile the effects of simple circuit replication under real-world noise profiles to better establish replication's limits as a supplemental mitigation strategy.
  Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) for the Maxcut problem is explored for the analysis. For small graphs, we found that the average inference strength decreases by approximately 21.8% while the average standard deviation decreases by 108.8% compared to 6 replicates. For larger graphs, inference strength decreases by 35.4% while the average standard deviation decreased only 20.5%. Fewer replications did not affect smaller graphs, but degraded inference strength, with comparable benefits to standard deviation in larger graphs. These results show that replication has potential uses as a supplemental mitigation strategy for large-depth, highly variable workloads.

</details>


### [101] [Finite-Time Electrometry with a Quantum-Regime Single-Ion Phonon Laser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21068)
*Pei-Dong Li,Yuan-Zhang Dong,Zhuo-Zhu Wu,Jia-Wei Wang,Ji Li,Jian-Qi Zhang,Zhi-Jiao Deng,Liang Chen,Mang Feng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次实验实现了量子区单离子声子激光器（$\bar{n}<10$），并基于其相空间对称性破缺响应实现了弱共振电场的精密测量，揭示了开放量子系统的有限时间松弛动力学对传感性能的调控作用，达到了14.15 μV/m/√Hz的散粒噪声极限灵敏度和1.83 μV/m的最小可探测场变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离子阱中声子激光器已展示出无需边带冷却即可探测外部电信号的独特能力，而通过边带冷却进入量子区有望进一步提升其传感性能，但目前尚无实验实现。

Method: 利用囚禁$^{40}\mathrm{Ca}^+$离子实验实现了量子区单离子声子激光器($\bar{n}<10$)，通过调控声子激光参数研究有限时间Liouvillian松弛动力学对传感性能的影响，并基于相空间对称性破缺响应探测弱共振电场。

Result: 实现了散粒噪声极限灵敏度14.15 ± 0.77 μV/m/√Hz和最小可探测场变化δE_min ≈ 1.83 μV/m。发现慢速Liouvillian松弛与有限实验时间窗口的关联可有效增强动态磁化率，同时维持极限环的结构稳健性。

Conclusion: 量子声子激光器可作为先进传感的实用平台，非平衡电测量中Liouvillian动力学起核心调控作用。

Abstract: The phonon laser realized in a trapped ion, i.e., a self-sustained mechanical oscillator, has demonstrated the unique characteristics in practically detecting externally applied electric signals without the prerequisite of sideband cooling. Entering the quantum regime via sideband cooling is expected to further improve its sensing performance. Here we report the first experimental realization of a quantum-regime single-ion phonon laser ($\bar{n}<10$) using a trapped $^{40}\mathrm{Ca}^+$ ion and demonstrate electrometry based on its phase-space symmetry-breaking response to weak resonant electric fields. By tuning the phonon-laser parameters, we reveal that the sensing performance is fundamentally governed by the finite-time relaxation dynamics of the underlying open quantum system. We find that a slow Liouvillian relaxation, correlated with the finite experimental interaction window, effectively enhances the dynamic susceptibility while maintaining the structural robustness of the limit cycle. This regime, when applied to the detection of electric fields, produces a shot-noise-limited peak sensitivity of $14.15 \pm 0.77~μ\mathrm{V/m}/\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$ and a minimum detectable field variation of $δE_{\mathrm{min}} \approx 1.83~μ\mathrm{V/m}$. Our results establish quantum phonon lasers as a practical platform for advanced sensing and highlight the central role of Liouvillian dynamics in non-equilibrium electrometry.

</details>


### [102] [Schur--Horn bound on field-free molecular orientation at finite temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21164)
*Tanveer Ahmad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了热分子系综在有限转动子空间内无场取向⟨cosθ⟩的Schur-Horn型上界，该上界仅依赖温度、转动常数和最大转动量子数Jmax，并通过LiH分子验证了与解析共振协议的差距，揭示了三个温度区间内的取向性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热分子系综在有限转动子空间内可实现的最大无场取向尚未被解析地表征，缺乏一个与具体控制方案无关的、纯运动学的上限。

Method: 推导了Schur-Horn型上界，该上界是磁量子数扇区内排序后的玻尔兹曼权重与排序后的cosθ谱之和；通过LiH分子对Hong等人的N子脉冲共振协议进行了基准测试，并优化了子脉冲面积和载波相位。

Result: 该上界在零温时退化为子空间本征值，在有限温时由转动配分函数确定；对于LiH，解析协议在T≤5 K时饱和上界至约1.6%，在T≈10.8 K时失去约10%的零温取向，在10 K以上存在10-40%的间隙；优化面积和相位仅能缩小约7%的间隙，且最优相位偏移为零。

Conclusion: 该上界是控制无关的相干控制设计目标，适用于任何线性极性分子（¹Σ⁺态），并在(Jmax, T)平面上完整映射。

Abstract: The maximum field-free orientation attainable from a \emph{thermal} molecular ensemble within a finite rotational subspace has not been characterised analytically. Here we derive a Schur--Horn-type upper bound on the field-free orientation $\avg{\cosθ}$ achievable by \emph{any} $M$-conserving unitary control acting on a Boltzmann ensemble truncated to rotational levels $J\le\Jmax$. The bound is the sum, over magnetic-quantum-number sectors, of the sorted Boltzmann weights paired with the sorted spectrum of $\cosθ$; it is purely kinematic, set by temperature, the rotational constant, and $\Jmax$ alone, and interpolates exactly between the zero-temperature subspace eigenvalue and a finite-temperature ceiling fixed by the rotational partition function. Benchmarked on LiH against the analytical $N$-subpulse resonant protocol of Hong \textit{et al.} [Phys.\ Rev.\ Research \textbf{7}, L012049 (2025)], it reveals three regimes: the protocol saturates the bound to within about $1.6\%$ for $T\le\SI{5}{\kelvin}$, loses roughly $10\%$ of its zero-temperature orientation at $T=B/\kB\approx\SI{10.8}{\kelvin}$, and leaves a $10$--$40\%$ gap above $\SI{10}{\kelvin}$. Optimising the subpulse areas and carrier phases within the fixed layout closes only $\approx7\%$ of that gap, the optimal phase offsets vanishing, which localises the dominant loss in the rigidity of the analytic layout rather than in the choice of areas or phases. The bound is a control-independent target for coherent-control design, applies unchanged to any linear polar molecule in a $^1Σ^+$ state, and is mapped across the $(\Jmax,T)$ plane.

</details>


### [103] [Bounded-depth spacetime lattice surgery for resource-efficient fault-tolerant quantum computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21192)
*Kou Hamada,Hiroki Hamaguchi,Yosuke Ueno,Yasunari Suzuki,Teruo Tanimoto,Nobuyuki Yoshioka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出双切片路由（double-slice routing），一种用于基于晶格手术的容错量子计算的恒定深度时空路由方法，通过两个连续时间片实现路径规划，保证扭结奇偶校正在平面和堆叠架构下终止，在哈密顿模拟工作负载上相比单切片基线降低编译成本最高2.4倍，结合栽培兼容映射优化可达7.5倍提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有晶格手术路由方法在抑制路径冲突（通过空间分配）与利用时间方向实现高效时空路由之间存在根本性张力，且未能同时保持与内部工厂布局的兼容性和终止保证。

Method: 提出双切片路由方法，使用两个连续时间片以恒定深度进行时空路由，并保证其扭结奇偶校正在平面和堆叠两种架构下均能终止。同时结合栽培兼容的映射优化进行联合优化。

Result: 在哈密顿模拟工作负载上，双切片路由相比单切片基线降低编译成本最高2.4倍；相比允许无限时间片数的投影路由，实现了更小的电路体积且仅增加微小执行时间开销；结合映射优化后整体改进达7.5倍。

Conclusion: 双切片路由是晶格手术编译中一个实用且有价值的操作点，同时展示了映射与路由联合优化的显著收益。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing based on lattice surgery requires place-and-route compilation with low spacetime overhead. Routing, in particular, faces a basic tension between suppressing path conflicts through greater spatial allocation and exploiting the time direction to realize ancilla-efficient spacetime routing. Existing approaches do not fully resolve this trade-off while retaining compatibility with inner factory layouts and termination guarantees. Here we introduce double-slice routing, a constant-depth spacetime-routing method that uses two consecutive time slices with a guarantee that its kink-parity correction terminates under both planar and stacked architectures. We numerically benchmark the resulting compiler on Hamiltonian-simulation workloads to show that double-slice routing reduces compilation cost by up to a factor of 2.4 over a single-slice baseline. Compared to projective routing, an existing method that allows an unbounded number of time slices per path, double-slice routing achieves smaller circuit volume with only a marginal execution-time penalty. Combined with a cultivation-compatible mapping optimization, the overall improvement reaches up to 7.5-fold over a naive single-slice compilation baseline. These results identify double-slice routing as a practically useful operating point in lattice-surgery compilation and show the substantial benefit in joint optimization of mapping and routing.

</details>


### [104] [Synchronization in the quantum regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21226)
*Finn Schmolke,Eric Lutz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了量子系统中同步现象的最新理论与实验进展，探讨了经典非线性系统中普遍存在的同步行为在量子 regime 中是否能够出现，并分析了量子噪声、线性时间演化等量子特性对同步的影响，以及量子相干性和量子关联可能带来的超越经典同步的新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究经典非线性系统中广泛存在的同步现象是否也能在量子系统中涌现，并理解量子特性（如线性时间演化、量子噪声、量子相干性和量子关联）对同步行为的影响。

Method: 综述近年来的理论与实验进展，涵盖从同步振荡的表征、真正非经典形式的同步到量子网络上的多体同步。

Result: 发现量子相干性和量子关联可能为增强同步提供超越经典对应物的新机制，但同时量子噪声和线性时间演化在微观层面阻碍同步的出现。

Conclusion: 量子系统中确实可能存在同步现象，且量子特征既可能抑制也可能增强同步，综述为该领域提供了一个全面的研究图谱。

Abstract: Can synchronization -- the widespread spontaneous emergence of coordinated dynamics in classical nonlinear systems -- also occur in the quantum regime? This question has recently sparked intense research into collective behavior and temporal self-organization in quantum systems. Typical quantum features such as the linearity of time evolution and the presence of quantum noise seem to hinder the appearance of synchrony at the microscopic level. At the same time, quantum coherence and quantum correlations may provide novel mechanisms for enhancing synchronization beyond its classical counterpart. We here survey recent theoretical and experimental advances in quantum synchronization, ranging from the characterization of synchronous oscillations and genuinely nonclassical forms of synchrony to many-body synchronization on quantum networks.

</details>


### [105] [Assessing Cost Hamiltonian Reliability in Quantum Protein Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21241)
*Mathieu Roget,Cedric Damour,Frederic Cadet,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文以量子蛋白质结构预测为案例，研究了变分量子算法中成本哈密顿量与真实结构精度（RMSD）之间的对齐程度，发现对于小肽段二者相关性不足，但随着问题规模增大和相互作用壳层增多，相关性有所提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在变分量子算法中，成本哈密顿量定义了量子硬件探索的优化景观，但实际应用中该哈密顿量往往是真实任务目标的简化代理。作者旨在研究这种简化代理是否足够可靠，即成本哈密顿量与真实结构精度之间是否存在足够的相关性。

Method: 以格点量子蛋白质结构预测为案例，使用接触能量成本哈密顿量作为优化目标，以RMSD（相对于实验测定结构）作为结构精度的度量标准。通过蒙特卡洛采样估计两者之间的相关性，对小肽段进行详细分析，并对更大规模实例进行相关性评估。

Result: 对于小肽段，接触能量成本哈密顿量的能量景观与RMSD误差的相关性不够强，无法提供有意义的预测。对于更大规模的问题实例，相关性有所增加；当考虑更多相互作用壳层时，相关性也得到提升。

Conclusion: 研究证实了独立于量子算法本身来研究成本哈密顿量可靠性的重要意义，指出成本哈密顿量的设计质量直接影响了量子优化方法的预测能力。

Abstract: In variational quantum algorithms, QAOA, and quantum annealing, the cost Hamiltonian defines the optimization landscape explored by the quantum hardware; however, in many application-driven formulations, this Hamiltonian is a simplified proxy for the true task-level objective. Using lattice-based quantum protein structure prediction as a case study, we investigate whether the contact-energy cost Hamiltonian commonly used in this setting is sufficiently aligned with structural accuracy, as measured by RMSD against experimentally determined structures. Through this specific problem, we show the importance of studying the reliability of the cost Hamiltonian independently from the quantum approach used. This work shows that, for small peptides and on average, the energy landscape of the considered cost Hamiltonian is not correlated well enough to the actual error to provide meaningful predictions. Moreover, this correlation was estimated through Monte-Carlo sampling for larger instances. It shows an increase of said correlation for larger problem instances and when more interaction shells are considered. This investigation illustrates the meaningfulness of investigating cost Hamiltonian relevance independently from the quantum algorithm used.

</details>


### [106] [Machine Learning Optimal Quantum Error Correction Thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22194)
*Dominik Seip,Luis Colmenarez,Markus Schmitt,Markus Müller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子纠错中相干信息（CI）与神经网络解码器训练所用的二元交叉熵损失之间的直接联系，并开发了基于Transformer的神经网络模型来估计CI、解码和进行软后选择，在表面码上验证了其优于最小权重完美匹配解码器，证明了Transformer架构在量子纠错中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机易受噪声影响，量子纠错码的性能在超过一定噪声阈值后会崩溃，需要信息论度量来刻画量子信息保护的根本极限。现有方法缺乏对相干信息与神经网络解码器损失之间直接联系的深入理解，且需要更优的解码与后选择方案。

Method: 建立了相干信息与二元交叉熵损失之间的理论下界关系；开发了基于Transformer的神经网络模型进行最大似然解码；在表面码上针对三种噪声模型（码容量、现象学、电路级噪声）进行验证；提出了基于MLD陪集的软后选择方案并证明其最优性。

Result: 网络准确预测了相干信息，阈值估计与已知理论极限高度吻合；作为解码器时，逻辑错误率显著优于最小权重完美匹配解码器；软后选择方案在逻辑错误率和中止概率方面均表现出可扩展性。

Conclusion: Transformer架构是量子纠错的强大工具，提供了支持MLD后选择最优性和可扩展性的首个数值证据。

Abstract: As quantum computers remain susceptible to noise, QEC is essential for preserving logical information during computations. However, the performance of QEC codes breaks down beyond certain noise thresholds, revealing fundamental limits on their ability to protect quantum information. These limits can be characterized using information-theoretic measures such as the coherent information, which quantifies the maximum rate at which logical information can be reliably transmitted through a noisy quantum channel. In this work, we establish a direct connection between the CI and the binary cross-entropy loss used when training neural network decoders. Specifically, we show that the CI constitutes a sharp lower bound on the achievable loss for decoders that track logical operators across noisy channels. To this end, we develop a transformer-based neural network model based on maximum likelihood decoding. We train this network to estimate the CI and evaluate its performance on the surface code under three noise models: code capacity, phenomenological, and circuit-level noise. Our results demonstrate that the network accurately predicts CI and yields threshold estimates that closely match known theoretical limits. When used as a decoder, the network significantly outperforms the minimum weight perfect matching decoder in terms of logical error rate. We also introduce a novel soft post-selection scheme that independently treats uncertainty in both logical operators and relies on confidence-based filtering of the network's output. We prove that such post-selection strategies, based on the MLD cosets, are optimal, and demonstrate their scalability in terms of both logical error rate and abort probability. These findings establish transformer-based architectures as powerful tools for QEC and provide the first numerical evidence supporting the optimality and scalability of MLD-based post-selection.

</details>


### [107] [Entanglement-entropy analysis of critical and topological quantum phases in a frustrated spin-1/2 Heisenberg ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21242)
*Azadeh Ghannadan,Hana Vargová,Jozef Genzor,Roman Krčmár,Andrej Gendiar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过张量网络方法研究了具有各向异性耦合的自旋-1/2阻挫海森堡梯子在横向磁场中的基态相图，识别出六种不同量子相，并利用纠缠标度提取了中心电荷，确认了Tomonaga-Luttinger液体和Ising普适类行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索各向异性耦合强度α和横向磁场共同作用下阻挫自旋梯子的丰富量子相图，特别是了解从Ising极限到XY主导区间的连续演变，以及不同量子相之间的转变机制。

Method: 采用基于矩阵乘积态的密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）计算方法，分析二分纠缠熵、磁化率和自旋关联函数等物理量，并通过有限尺寸标度提取中心电荷。

Result: 识别出六种基态相：rung-singlet态、Haldane-like态、Tomonaga-Luttinger液体、canted Ising有序态、XY极化态和有序铁磁态。在无能隙区域提取到中心电荷c≈1（Tomonaga-Luttinger液体），在canted Ising与铁磁相变处观察到c≈1/2（Ising普适类）。Haldane-like行为和扩展临界TLL区域均强烈局限在各向同性点α=1附近。

Conclusion: 纠缠测度的有限尺寸标度比磁化率和关联函数能更灵敏、更统一地诊断有能隙和无能隙区域；各向异性参数α对相图有决定性影响，Haldane-like相和TLL临界区仅存在于α≈1附近。

Abstract: We investigate the ground-state phase diagram of a frustrated spin-1/2 Heisenberg ladder in the transverse magnetic field with an anisotropic inter-rung exchange coupling $α$. This bond-anisotropic parameter continuously interpolates among the Ising-like limit $α=0$, the isotropic point $α=1$, and the XY-dominated regime $α\gg 1$. Using density-matrix renormalization group calculations within a matrix-product-state framework, we analyze bipartite entanglement, magnetization, and spin correlations to characterize the emergent quantum phases. We identify six distinct ground-state phases, including rung-singlet, Haldane-like, Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid, canted Ising-ordered, XY-polarized, and ordered ferromagnetic states. While magnetization and local correlations provide a first insight into phase classifications, the finite-size scaling of the entanglement measures offers a more sensitive and unified diagnostics of both gapped and gapless regimes. In the gapless regime, we extract the central charge $c \simeq 1$, confirming Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid behavior, while at the transition between the canted Ising and ferromagnetic phases, we observe $c \simeq 1/2$, consistent with the Ising universality. Finally, we find that both the Haldane-like behavior and the extended critical Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid regime are strongly confined to the vicinity of the isotropic point $α= 1$.

</details>


### [108] [Reducing measurement error with adaptivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21283)
*James Byrne,Noah Linden,Paul Skrzypczyk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明自适应（前馈）测量方案相比并行（非自适应）方案能更有效地降低噪声测量设备的误差，且这种优势在仅使用三次测量时即可出现，并随测量次数增加而无限增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自适应测量协议能否超越非自适应并行测量协议，在多次使用噪声测量设备时进一步降低测量误差。

Method: 理论分析比较自适应与非自适应测量电路的最优性能；针对两类两输出量子比特测量开发最优测量电路求解方法；推广到一般维度的量子系统（qudit）。

Result: 自适应测量电路在相同测量次数下普遍优于非自适应电路；对一大类噪声两输出量子比特测量，仅需三次测量即可展现自适应优势；该优势随测量次数增加而无界增长。

Conclusion: 自适应（前馈）是降低测量误差的强大资源，能在有限测量次数下提供显著且可扩展的优势，为量子测量误差缓解提供了新的最优协议方向。

Abstract: We show that adaptivity, also called feed-forward, is a powerful resource in reducing the error of measurement circuits that combine multiple uses of a noisy measuring device. In previous work, it has been shown that error in measurements can be mitigated by using the measuring device multiple times. So far, the most effective protocols have been parallel measurement schemes, where all measurements are simultaneous. We extend this idea to adaptive measurement schemes, where the results of previous measurements can influence our choice of processing further down the circuit. We show that adaptive measurement circuits can in general reduce overall measurement errors further than any non-adaptive measurement circuit could with the same number of measurements. In particular, we show that for a large class of noisy two-outcome qubit measurements, such an adaptive advantage can exist when as few as three measurements are used. We also show that the adaptive advantage is unbounded across the class of noisy two-outcome qubit measurements, as the number of uses of the device increases. As part of this work, we devise and use methods for finding optimal measurement circuits in both the non-adaptive and adaptive cases. In addition, we prove general results about the limits of such circuits, both in measuring a qubit, and more generally, a qudit.

</details>


### [109] [Time-Bin BB84 QKD System Using Indium Phosphide and Silicon Nitride Photonic Integrated Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21308)
*Denis Fatkhiev,Alexander Grebenchukov,João dos Reis Frazão,Gleb Nazarikov,Chigo Okonkwo,Idelfonso Tafur Monroy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种双芯片InP-SiN光子QKD系统，集成了片上脉冲生成和可重构解码功能，在150-250公里光纤上实现了低于4%的QBER和kbps级的密钥速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现集成化、高性能的量子密钥分发系统，满足远距离光纤通信中安全密钥生成的需求。

Method: 采用双芯片InP-SiN光子集成方案，实现片上脉冲生成和可重构解码，基于时间比特BB84协议，并采用有限密钥安全分析抵抗相干攻击。

Result: 系统在150-250公里光纤上维持QBER低于4%，并实现kbps级别的安全密钥速率。

Conclusion: 该双芯片集成光子方案在远距离光纤QKD中实现了低误码率和实用化的密钥速率，验证了InP-SiN异构集成的可行性。

Abstract: We demonstrate a dual-chip InP-SiN photonic QKD system with on-chip pulse generation and reconfigurable decoding, implementing time-bin BB84 with finite-key security against coherent attacks. The system sustains a QBER below 4% and delivers secret keys at kbps rates over 150-250 km of fiber.

</details>


### [110] [Temporal processing of quantum states with hybrid quantum-classical reservoirs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21327)
*Mateu Coll-Comas,Gian Luca Giorgi,Roberta Zambrini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子-经典混合储层计算架构（HRC），将量子储层与经典回声状态网络结合，克服了量子储层计算（QRC）对单输入态输出线性的限制，能够计算纯度、熵等非线性泛函。在完整层析和部分信息（单轴测量）两种信息机制下，混合系统在线性和非线性任务中均优于其单独组件。基于此，作者应用了在线监测协议，考虑了测量反作用和有限测量系综，在实验条件下评估了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子储层计算（QRC）对单量子输入态的输出本质上是线性的，无法自然计算纯度、熵等非线性泛函，这限制了其在量子机器学习中的应用。

Method: 提出量子-经典混合储层计算（HRC）架构，将量子比特储层与经典回声状态网络（ESN）结合。在两种信息机制（完整层析和部分信息/单轴测量）下进行系统研究，并应用在线监测协议处理测量反作用和有限测量系综问题。

Result: 在部分信息（单轴测量）条件下，混合系统在线性和非线性任务中均优于其单独组件（纯量子储层或纯经典ESN），这归因于量子储层增强了信息检索能力。

Conclusion: 混合量子-经典储层计算（HRC）架构是在近程量子硬件上实现增强量子机器学习的实用且可扩展的路线。

Abstract: A distinctive feature of Quantum Reservoir Computing (QRC) is the ability to directly embed quantum input states into the reservoir dynamics. However, the resulting output is fundamentally linear for a single input state, preventing QRC from naturally computing nonlinear functionals such as purity or entropy. We overcome this limitation with a quantum-classical hybrid architecture combining a qubit quantum reservoir with a classical echo state network (ESN), allowing both nonlinear functional approximation and effective temporal processing. We systematically study performance under two information regimes: full-tomography and partial information (single-axis measurements), with the latter demonstrating that the hybrid system outperforms its standalone components in both linear and nonlinear tasks due to the enhanced information retrieval provided by the quantum reservoir. Building on these results, we apply an online monitoring protocol that explicitly accounts for measurement back-action and finite measurement ensembles, enabling a realistic assessment of performance under experimental conditions. These results establish hybrid quantum-classical reservoir computing (HRC) architectures as a practical and scalable route for enhanced quantum machine learning on near-term qubit hardware.

</details>


### [111] [Efficient Verification of Entangled Measurements with Local States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21355)
*Kun Wang,Masahito Hayashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅利用局域态制备的量子测量验证（QMV）框架，证明了对于局域传递且不可约的投影测量，对称性可将局域约束下的QMV简化为单基矢的量子态验证，从而将协议设计转化为齐次验证算子的优化。作者将该框架应用于广义贝尔测量、两量子比特单参数测量、优雅联合测量及稳定子态诱导测量，得到了显式局域协议、闭式验证算子、成功概率和样本复杂度。进一步证明了齐次QMV协议可直接从观测通过频率估计测量保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子测量验证方法通常需要全局纠缠态制备或复杂的实验设置，本文旨在开发一种仅依赖局域态制备的QMV框架，降低实验实现难度，并利用对称性简化协议设计。

Method: 利用局域态制备和对称性分析，将局域约束下的QMV问题转化为单基矢的量子态验证问题，通过齐次验证算子优化来设计协议。

Result: 得到了多种测量（广义贝尔测量、两量子比特单参数测量、优雅联合测量、稳定子态诱导测量）的显式局域协议、闭式验证算子、成功概率和样本复杂度，并证明了齐次QMV协议可直接从通过频率估计测量保真度。

Conclusion: 该框架为局域约束下的量子测量验证提供了通用且高效的方法，显著降低了实验资源需求，并可直接估计测量保真度。

Abstract: We develop a framework for quantum measurement verification (QMV) using only local state preparations. For locally transitive and irreducible projective measurements, we prove that symmetry reduces locality constrained QMV to quantum state verification of a single basis state, thereby reducing protocol design to the optimization of homogeneous verification operators. We apply the framework to generalized Bell measurements, single-parameter measurements on two qubits, elegant joint measurements, and stabilizer state induced measurements, and derive explicit local protocols together with closed form verification operators, success probabilities, and sample complexities. We further show that homogeneous QMV protocols can estimate measurement fidelity directly from observed passing frequencies.

</details>


### [112] [Quantum Beam-Splitter Cooling and Thermometry in Large Trapped-Ion Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21357)
*Kirthik Rajakumar,Ansh Das,Abhinay Pandey,Athreya Shankar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并表征了一种用于大型囚禁离子晶体质心模式快速近基态冷却的协议——量子分束器冷却（QBSC），该协议利用红边带驱动实现离子晶体的集体自旋与质心模式之间的分束器型SWAP操作，随后通过自旋重置去除熵，从而实现近基态冷却。同时，本文还展示了基于该方案的近最优量子分束器测温法（QBST）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现大型囚禁离子晶体质心模式的快速近基态冷却，克服传统冷却方法在离子数量增大时的局限性，并探索利用多体效应开辟激光冷却和测温的新途径。

Method: 当初始平均热占据数远小于离子数时，利用红边带驱动实现质心模式与N个离子集体自旋（等效量子谐振子）间的分束器型SWAP操作，随后对自旋进行重置以去除熵；同时分析了有限离子数、离共振载波和蓝边带贡献、旁观者模式边带驱动等实际不完善因素的影响，并提出了消除载波驱动的策略；在测温方面，通过在SWAP操作结束时测量离子布居统计量实现近最优量子分束器测温。

Result: QBSC协议可实现大型囚禁离子晶体质心模式的快速近基态冷却；QBST方案的经典Fisher信息可接近热态的量子Fisher信息，实现近最优测温。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了利用多体效应为大型囚禁离子晶体中的激光冷却和测温开辟新途径的可行性，并与连续边带冷却方案及基于快速绝热通过测温方案进行了对比。

Abstract: We propose and characterize a protocol for rapid near-ground state cooling of the center-of-mass (c.m.) mode of a large trapped ion crystal. When the initial mean thermal occupation of the mode $\bar{n}_i$ is small compared to the number of ions $N$, a red sideband drive implements a beam-splitter type SWAP operation between the mode and the collective spin of the $N$ ions, with the latter effectively serving as a quantum harmonic oscillator. Subsequently, a reset of the spins removes the entropy, leading to near-ground state cooling of the c.m. mode. We term this protocol as quantum beam-splitter cooling (QBSC). We analyze the impact of several practical imperfections on the final temperature achievable under QBSC, including finite ion number, off-resonant carrier and blue-sideband contributions, and the impact of the sideband drives arising from spectator modes. In addition, we outline practical strategies to eliminate the carrier drive. Furthermore, we show that measuring the population statistics of the ions at the end of the SWAP operation can enable near-optimal quantum beam-splitter thermometry (QBST), with the classical Fisher information approaching the quantum Fisher information of a thermal state. We discuss the connection of QBSC with continuous sideband cooling and compare QBST with a recently proposed rapid adiabatic passage-based thermometry scheme. Our work constitutes an example of harnessing many-body effects to open new routes to laser cooling and thermometry in large trapped ion crystals.

</details>


### [113] [Bell inequalities tailored to optimal global randomness certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21362)
*Ignacio Perito,Raffaele D'Avino,Michał Jung,Piotr Mironowicz,Antonio Acín,Remigiusz Augusiak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了两族新的二分贝尔不等式，旨在为任意输出维度d实现最优全局随机性认证。第一族通过d×d最大纠缠态实现量子违背（推测最优），数值验证了对d=3,4的最优随机性；第二族给出了任意d的最优量子违背和实现，并在d=3时自检验证了该实现，认证了两个完全随机三进制数（trits）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的贝尔不等式在认证全局随机性方面存在局限性，尤其是对于高维输出系统（d>2），缺乏能够达到最优2 log d比特随机性认证的通用不等式族。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用对称性论证构建两个贝尔不等式族；第一族通过d×d最大纠缠态构造量子违背（推测最优），并用数值方法验证d=3,4时认证最优随机性；第二族给出任意d的最优量子违背和量子实现（使用d×d最大纠缠态和至少两个无偏基的投影测量），并对d=3进行自检验证。

Result: 第一族不等式在d=3,4时数值认证了最优全局随机性（2 log d比特）；第二族不等式在任意d下达到最优量子违背，并在d=3时自检验证了该实现，认证了两个完全随机trits。

Conclusion: 本文成功构建了两族适用于任意输出维度d的贝尔不等式，能够实现最优的全局随机性认证（2 log d比特），为高维量子随机性认证提供了理论基础和实验可行方案。

Abstract: We present two novel families of bipartite Bell inequalities designed to achieve optimal global randomness certification for an arbitrary number of outputs $d$. We first use symmetry arguments to argue that their maximal quantum violations certify $2\log d$ random bits. For the first family, we construct a quantum realization using $d\times d$ maximally entangled states which provides a quantum violation that we conjecture to be optimal for any $d$. It is then numerically shown that the obtained quantum violation certifies optimal global randomness, up to numerical precision, for $d=3,4$. For the second family, we provide the optimal quantum violation and its quantum realization for any $d$, again using $d\times d$ maximally entangled states and projective measurements over at least two unbiased bases on one of the parties. We self-test this realization for $d=3$, which implies the optimal certification of two fully random trits.

</details>


### [114] [Maximal global device-independent randomness from projective measurements in every dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21369)
*Máté Farkas,Piotr Mironowicz,Remigiusz Augusiak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明使用投影测量和互无偏基，可以从局部维度为d的双边系统中认证2log(d)比特的设备无关随机数，达到了理论最大速率，并且协议对实验噪声具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备无关量子随机数生成（DIQRNG）是最安全的随机数生成方式，但实现成本高，需要高质量纠缠系统。因此需要充分利用可用资源，在现有实验条件下达到理论最大随机数生成速率。

Method: 使用投影测量（最易于实验实现）和互无偏基（MUBs）设计显式协议，从局部维度为d的双边系统中认证2log(d)比特的设备无关随机数。同时通过数值计算分析协议在非完美实现（有噪声）情况下的性能。

Result: 对于每个d≥2，可以实现2log(d)比特的设备无关随机数，达到理论上最大可能速率。数值结果显示了协议对实验噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 使用投影测量即可实现DIQRNG的理论最大速率，且协议对噪声具有鲁棒性，为实际低成本实现最优DIQRNG提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Device-independent random number generation (DIQRNG) is the most secure form of generating private randomness using quantum physical processes. Its strength lies in producing numbers that are impossible to predict by any eavesdropper restricted by the laws of quantum theory. Moreover, security is proven solely from observed measurement statistics, without the need to characterise or trust the devices used in random number generation. Implementing DIQRNG is, however, costly, as it requires high-quality entangled systems. It is therefore important to make the best use of available resources. In this work, we show that using projective measurements -- which are most readily implementable experimentally -- one can certify $2\log(d)$ bits of device-independent randomness from a bipartite system of local dimension $d$ for every $d \ge 2$, thus reaching the theoretically maximum possible rate of DIQRNG. We provide explicit protocols reaching $2\log(d)$ bits based on mutually unbiased bases. Furthermore, we compute numerical bounds on the rate for the case of imperfect implementations, showing that our protocols are robust to experimental noise.

</details>


### [115] [Noise robustness of three outcome Bell certified quantum randomness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21371)
*Raffaele D'Avino,Ignacio Perito,Piotr Mironowicz,Antonio Acín,Remigiusz Augusiak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于三方三输出贝尔不等式违背的设备无关全局随机性认证，发现多输出测量能超越二元输出场景的随机性极限，大量结构化贝尔表达式可认证显著的最小熵，并展现出良好的抗噪鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多输出测量是否能在现实场景中超越二元输出所能实现的随机性量，并系统性地评估基于贝尔不等式违背的设备无关全局随机性认证能力。

Method: 分析已知贝尔表达式并评估其抗噪鲁棒性；提出一种在结构化族内系统生成新贝尔表达式的方法；开展大规模数值研究。

Result: 大量不等式可认证显著的最小熵；部分简单不等式在减少测量设置数量的同时实现了接近最大的全局随机性；绝大多数非平凡证书在现实噪声下仍能维持正认证随机性。

Conclusion: 强设备无关随机性扩展在多输出场景中并不局限于精心设计的不等式，而是在适当构建的贝尔表达式族中具有普遍性。

Abstract: We investigate device-independent certification of global randomness based on Bell inequality violations in bipartite scenarios with three outcomes per party. Our goal is to determine whether multi-outcome measurements allow one to surpass the amount of randomness achievable with binary outputs in realistic scenarios. We begin by analyzing several known Bell expressions and evaluating their robustness against noise for randomness certification. We then introduce a systematic method for generating new Bell expressions within structured families and perform a large-scale numerical study. We find that a substantial number of inequalities certify significant amounts of min-entropy. In particular, we identify simple inequalities that achieve near-maximal global randomness while involving a reduced number of measurement settings, thus improving the balance between certified randomness and number of inputs. Moreover, the vast majority of nontrivial certificates exhibit robustness against realistic noise, maintaining positive certified randomness away from the ideal regime. These results demonstrate that strong device-independent randomness expansion in multi-outcome scenarios is not restricted to carefully engineered inequalities, but arises generically within suitably constructed families of Bell expressions.

</details>


### [116] [Collective enhancement in sideband cooling of ion crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21374)
*Ivan Vybornyi,Artem Zhdanov,Matthias Bock,Leo Walz,Nele Griesbach,Klemens Hammerer,Christian F. Roos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过理论和实验研究，证明了在多离子边带冷却中，强耦合条件下自旋与运动子系统会发生相干态交换，使残余声子数随离子数N按1/N²比例降低，并利用91离子平面库仑晶体实现了平均声子数低于2×10⁻⁴的极低熵运动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离子库仑晶体的低熵运动态对量子应用至关重要，通常通过激光冷却制备。随着更大晶体进入量子区操作，离子数增加是否有利于冷却尚不清楚且近期存在争议，本文旨在解决这一开放问题。

Method: 采用理论分析与实验验证相结合的方法，研究多离子边带冷却在不同自旋-运动耦合机制下的集体效应。在弱耦合与强耦合两种条件下分别分析，并在强耦合条件下利用相干态交换机制，通过适当定时的激光脉冲实现冷却。实验采用含多达91个离子的平面库仑晶体。

Result: 弱耦合条件下多体效应不显著；强耦合条件下，自旋与运动子系统发生相干态交换，残余平均声子数随离子数N按1/N²标度律降低。通过迭代脉冲，测得平均声子数低于2×10⁻⁴。此外，大晶体在相干区中自旋-运动动力学对初始声子统计几乎不敏感，且可通过自旋测量利用态交换机制探测声子分布。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在强耦合多离子边带冷却中，离子数增加不仅不阻碍冷却，反而能显著提升冷却效果（1/N²标度律），为大规模离子晶体的量子态制备提供了有效方案，同时揭示了态交换机制作为声子分布探测工具的潜力。

Abstract: Low-entropy motional states of ion Coulomb crystals are an essential prerequisite for a plethora of applications and are typically prepared by laser cooling. As larger crystals are operated in the quantum regime, it remains unclear, and has recently become debated, whether increasing the ion number can be beneficial for cooling. Here, we investigate theoretically and experimentally many-ion sideband cooling and the role of collective effects in different spin-motion coupling regimes. For weak coupling, the many-body effects are insignificant. In the strong-coupling regime, however, the spin and motional subsystems undergo a coherent state swap, enabling cooling by a suitably timed laser pulse. Using planar Coulomb crystals with up to 91 ions, we demonstrate that the residual mean phonon occupation after one such pulse scales as $1/N^2$ with the number of ions. By iterating the pulses, we measure mean phonon occupations $<2\cdot10^{-4}$. For large crystals in the coherent regime, we further show that the spin-motion dynamics becomes largely independent of the initial phonon statistics. Through spin measurements, the state-swap mechanism can be utilized to probe the phonon distribution in the mode.

</details>


### [117] [Hierarchy of mixed symmetry protected topological states in extended cluster states under subsystem decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22507)
*Yoshihito Kuno,Takahiro Orito*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了子系统退相干对扩展团簇态（一种对称保护拓扑相）的影响，发现退相干并非单纯破坏性过程，而是能诱导并组织一系列非平庸混合态：从分层混合态SPT相（由Rényi-2弦序表征）最终演变为Z₂强到弱自发对称破缺（SWSSB）态及玻璃态GHZ长程纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索退相干在量子多体系统中的作用，尤其是子系统退相干如何影响对称保护拓扑（SPT）相，揭示退相干可能诱导而非仅仅破坏非平庸量子相。

Method: 从具有多个子系统Z₂对称性的扩展团簇态出发，逐步引入子系统退相干，分析混合态中的对称性结构、Rényi-2弦序以及纠缠特性。

Result: 子系统退相干诱导局域电荷涨落，产生不受影响子系统上的混合SPT态。逐步退相干产生分层混合态SPT相层次，最终在剩余子系统上终止于Z₂ SWSSB态及玻璃态GHZ长程纠缠态。

Conclusion: 退相干可以系统性地将混合态SPT序转化为SWSSB及玻璃态GHZ型长程纠缠，为理解退相干作为量子相变和有序化过程提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study the effect of subsystem decoherence to an extended cluster state which is a symmetry protected topological (SPT) phase. The model includes many subsystem $Z_2$ symmetries. We report that subsystem decoherence induces local charge fluctuations, leading to a mixed SPT state in the unaffected subsystems. If we start from the extended cluster state, hierarchical mixed-state SPT phases emerge in response to step-by-step subsystem decoherences. These mixed-state SPT phases keep strong symmetries the symmetry of which is protecting symmetries for the initial cluster SPT. Moreover, these SPTs can be characterized by Rényi-2 string orders. Then, as the subsystems are progressively decohered, the hierarchy of mixed-state SPT phases terminates in a $Z_2$ strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SWSSB) state on the final remaining subsystem, where a long-range entangled state appears, namely a glassy Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) state. Our work demonstrates that decoherence is not merely a destructive process, but can induce and organize series of nontrivial mixed-states. This reveals a systematic route from mixed-state SPT order to SWSSB with the glassy GHZ-type long-range entanglement.

</details>


### [118] [Entanglement engineering in magnomechanical system via cross-Kerr interaction and mechanical parametric amplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21388)
*E. Kongkui Berinyuy,P. Djorwé,A. N. Al-Ahmadi,H. Ardah,A. -H. Abdel-Aty*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出在腔磁力系统中，通过交叉Kerr非线性和机械参量放大(MPA)来生成并增强量子纠缠。研究表明，即使交叉Kerr耦合较弱，引入声子跳跃和MPA也能实现纠缠生成，且纠缠态纯度较高，为量子信息处理等应用提供了可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠在腔磁力系统中有广泛的应用前景，但传统方案需要较强的交叉Kerr耦合才能产生纠缠，限制了系统的实用性和可调性。本文旨在探索如何通过额外的机制（声子跳跃和机械参量放大）在弱耦合条件下也能生成和增强量子纠缠。

Method: 构建了一个包含磁振子模式、声子模式和质心运动(CMM)模式的钇铁石榴石(YIG)球体系统。磁振子同时驱动声子模式和CMM模式，两者通过声子跳跃率$J_m$弱耦合。磁振子与声子通过交叉Kerr相互作用耦合，声子模式额外受到MPA驱动。通过调节声子跳跃和MPA参数，研究纠缠生成条件。

Result: 在没有机械耦合($J_m=0$)和MPA时，需要较强的有效交叉Kerr耦合才能产生纠缠。引入声子跳跃和MPA后，即使在弱交叉Kerr耦合下也能生成量子纠缠，且纠缠态纯度在相同参数区间内保持较高水平，说明纠缠生成不会显著增加态的混合程度。

Conclusion: 声子跳跃和机械参量放大在磁力系统中工程化量子关联中起关键作用，实现了鲁棒且稳定的量子纠缠，可用于量子信息处理、量子通信和量子计算等现代量子应用。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement in cavity magnomechanical system has a wide range of applications in modern quantum technologies. In this work, we propose a theoretical scheme to generate and enhance quantum entanglement through cross-Kerr nonlinearity and mechanical parametric amplification (MPA) in a magnomechanical system. Our system is made of a magnonic mode that is simultaneously driving the acoustic phononic and the center-of-mass motion (CMM) phonon in a yttrium iron garnet sphere. The acoustic mode and the center-of-mass mechanical (CMM) mode are weakly coupled via the phonon hopping rate $J_m$. Moreover, the magnonic and phononic modes interact through cross-Kerr interaction, while the phononic mode is additionally driven via a Mechanical Parametric Amplification (MPA). Without the mechanical coupling ($J_m = 0$) and the MPA, the generation of entanglement among the subsystems requires a relatively strong effective cross-Kerr coupling. However, when phonon hopping and MPA are accounted, quantum entanglement can be generated even for weak values of the cross-Kerr coupling strength, revealing the key role of these interactions in the engineering of quantum correlations in our proposal. Furthermore, the related purity of the generated entangled states remains high for the same parameter's regime, revealing that the generated quantum entanglement is established without significantly increasing the mixing of the involved states in the system. Our work suggests how robust and stable quantum correlations can be engineered in magnomechanical structures based on nonlinear interactions. These results are useful for modern quantum applications including quantum information processing, quantum communication, and quantum computational tasks.

</details>


### [119] [A single-electron double quantum dot with Rashba spin-orbit interaction as a working substance for heat machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22733)
*Ivan Romualdo de Oliveira,Vinícius N. A. Lula-Rocha,Moises Rojas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了以双量子点中单电子为工质、受外磁场和Rashba自旋-轨道耦合调控的量子奥托热机，分析了准静态循环中的热交换、功输出和效率，发现Rashba相互作用可作为切换热机、制冷机、加热器和加速器四类工作模式的有效控制参数，并揭示了效率与功输出之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Rashba自旋-轨道耦合对量子热机热力学性能的影响机制，研究其在量子奥托循环中切换不同工作模式（热机、制冷机等）的可行性，并寻找最优效率与最大功输出的操作参数空间。

Method: 以双量子点中单电子为工质，引入外磁场（Zeeman分裂）、阱间隧穿振幅和Rashba自旋-轨道耦合（诱导自旋翻转隧穿）调控哈密顿量，在准静态奥托循环框架下分析热交换、功输出和效率，并对哈密顿参数和储热器温度进行全局数值优化。

Result: Rashba相互作用可作为有效控制参数在热机、制冷机、加热器和加速器四种模式间切换；最高效率接近卡诺极限，出现在最大温度梯度附近；最大功输出位于参数空间的不同区域，两者存在明确权衡关系，该权衡受Zeeman分裂、隧穿振幅和Rashba耦合引起的能谱变形调控。

Conclusion: Rashba自旋-轨道耦合为量子热机提供了额外的调控自由度，可在多种热力学模式间灵活切换，但效率与功输出之间存在固有权衡，需根据实际需求选择优化目标。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic performance of a quantum Otto machine whose working substance is a single electron confined in a double quantum dot under an external magnetic field and Rashba spin-orbit interaction. The Hamiltonian is controlled by the Zeeman splitting, the interdot tunneling amplitude, and the Rashba coupling, which induces spin-flip tunneling between localized orbital states. Within a quasistatic Otto cycle, we analyze the heat exchanged with the reservoirs, the extracted work, and the efficiency as functions of the Hamiltonian parameters and reservoir temperatures. We show that the Rashba interaction acts as an effective control parameter for switching among heat-engine, refrigerator, heater, and accelerator regimes. A global numerical analysis over the Hamiltonian parameters and reservoir temperatures identifies the optimal operating points for efficiency and work output in the heat-engine regime. The highest efficiencies occur near the maximum temperature gradient explored and approach the Carnot bound, whereas the largest work output appears in a different region of parameter space. The results reveal a clear trade-off between maximum efficiency and maximum extracted work, governed by the spectral deformation induced by the Zeeman splitting, tunneling amplitude, and Rashba coupling.

</details>


### [120] [Optimal GHZ-State Distribution in LOSR Quantum Networks via Local Decoding from Information Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21500)
*Leonardo Oleynik,Shehbaz Tariq,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，在仅允许局域操作和共享随机性（LOSR）的网络中，多部分纠缠源可以替代实时经典通信，实现任意大小的正则均匀网络中(N)方GHZ态的高保真度制备，保真度为(d^{m-M})，并证明了该值在局域酉策略下的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展量子网络需要分布多部分纠缠，但基于LOCC的网络需要实时经典通信，带来量子存储和延迟开销。LOSR网络虽避免了这些成本，但仅使用二分源时无法制备有用的GHZ态。作者试图证明多部分源可以克服这一限制。

Method: 将网络的超边与线性码的坐标对应，利用码的局部译码器构造一组固定的局域酉变换。当每个节点关联的边构成一个信息集时，该变换将源态转化为(N)方GHZ态，保真度为(d^{m-M})。其中(m)为每个节点关联的边数，(M)为网络总边数。

Result: 对于完全((N-1))-一致超图(K_N^{(N-1)})，保真度降至(1/d)；证明该值在所有局域酉策略中是最优的，且超过了仅使用二分源所能达到的最佳保真度（四节点情况下1/2对比1/8）。

Conclusion: 多部分源结合共享随机性可以在LOSR网络中替代实时经典通信，用于高效的多部分纠缠分布。

Abstract: Distributing multipartite entanglement is a prerequisite for scalable quantum networks. Networks restricted to local operations and shared randomness (LOSR) avoid the quantum-memory and latency costs associated with the real-time classical communication required by LOCC-based networks. However, when only bipartite sources are available, LOSR networks cannot prepare useful GHZ states. In earlier work, we conjectured that multipartite sources overcome this limitation and supported this claim with a single numerical example. In this work, we prove the conjecture for regular and uniform networks of arbitrary size. By identifying the hyperedges of the network with the coordinates of a linear code, we show that whenever the edges incident to each node form an information set, a fixed collection of local unitaries, namely the local decoders of the code, transforms the source state into an (N)-party GHZ state with fidelity (d^{m-M}), without requiring any classical communication. Here, (m) denotes the number of edges incident to each node and (M) the total number of edges in the network. For the complete ((N-1))-uniform hypergraph (K_N^{(N-1)}), this fidelity reduces to (1/d). We further prove that this value is optimal among all local-unitary strategies and exceeds the best fidelity achievable using only bipartite sources. For example, in the four-node case, the optimal fidelity is (1/2), compared with the bipartite bound of (1/8). These results demonstrate that multipartite sources, together with shared randomness, can replace real-time classical communication for entanglement distribution.

</details>


### [121] [A Correlation Aware Quantum Feature Map for Variational Quantum Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21570)
*Murat Kurt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种相关性感知量子特征映射(CAQFM)，将特征依赖关系(通过Pearson、Spearman、Kendall Tau、互信息和距离相关性度量)编码进量子电路，在三个基准数据集上提升了变分量子分类器的分类性能，其中Spearman和Kendall Tau变体表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子特征映射采用固定编码策略，未能显式考虑数据集中特征间的关系，限制了量子表示对数据结构的表达能力。

Method: 利用五种相关性度量(Pearson, Spearman, Kendall Tau, Mutual Information, Distance Correlation)识别特征依赖，将超过预设阈值的依赖通过受控量子门嵌入量子电路中，构建CAQFM。

Result: 在乳腺癌诊断、信用违约预测和学生就业分类三个基准数据集上，基于相关性编码的量子特征映射优于传统编码策略，其中Spearman和Kendall Tau变体在预测性能上持续超越标准量子特征映射。

Conclusion: 将经典数据的依赖信息融入量子特征映射有助于生成更具判别力的量子表示，从而提升变分量子分类器的有效性。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning has emerged as a promising research area for learning complex data patterns. However, most existing quantum feature maps employ fixed encoding strategies that do not explicitly consider the relationships among features within a dataset. In this study, we propose a Correlation Aware Quantum Feature Map (CAQFM) which integrates feature dependencies into the quantum encoding process. The proposed approach utilizes Pearson, Spearman, Kendall Tau, Mutual Information, and Distance Correlation measures to identify relationships among features. Dependencies exceeding a predefined threshold are incorporated into the quantum circuit through controlled quantum gates, enabling the construction of richer quantum representations that better reflect the underlying structure of the data.
  The proposed method is evaluated using a Variational Quantum Classifier (VQC) on three benchmark datasets, namely breast cancer diagnosis, credit default prediction, and student placement classification. Simulation results demonstrate that correlation based quantum encoding can improve classification performance compared to conventional encoding strategies. In particular, the Spearman and Kendall Tau based CAQFM variants achieved the highest predictive performance and consistently outperformed standard quantum feature maps. The findings indicate that incorporating dependency information from classical data into quantum feature maps facilitates the generation of more discriminative quantum representations and enhances the effectiveness of variational quantum classifiers.

</details>


### [122] [Systematic derivation of Tsirelson bounds in arbitrary dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21626)
*Lorenzo Coccia,Matteo Padovan,Giuseppe Vallone*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于平方和分解的系统方法，用于推导二分系统的Tsirelson界限和局域界限，并发现了高维量子态的新界限，同时恢复了关于最大纠缠态（qubits和qudits）的已知结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子非局域性和Tsirelson界限是量子信息科学的核心问题，但高维量子态和测量的紧致量子界限的确定仍是一项重大挑战。

Method: 提出了一种基于平方和（sum-of-squares）分解的系统性方法，用于推导二分量子系统的Tsirelson界限和局域界限。

Result: 发现了新的量子关联界限，并恢复了关于最大纠缠态（qubits和qudits）的已有结果。

Conclusion: 该平方和分解方法为系统性地推导高维量子系统的Tsirelson界限提供了一条有效途径，有助于更深入地理解量子理论的界限。

Abstract: The study of Bell nonlocality and the bounds of quantum correlations, the so-called Tsirelson bounds, is fundamental to quantum information science and the exploration of the limits of quantum theory. While quantum bounds for qubit systems have been extensively characterized, determining tight quantum bounds for correlations attainable with high-dimensional quantum states and measurements remains a significant challenge. In this work, we propose a systematic derivation of bipartite Tsirelson and local bounds written in terms of sum-of-squares decompositions. Using this method, we discover novel bounds and recover established results for maximally entangled states of qubits and qu$d$its.

</details>


### [123] [No-signaling values of quantum games--an operator algebra perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21664)
*Roy Araiza,Marius Junge,Carlos Palazuelos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从算子代数和算子空间角度研究双证明者量子游戏（量子输入输出游戏），通过算子空间范畴中的张量范数刻画游戏的各种值概念，重点给出了无信号值的精确算子空间表述，并建立了与算子代数问题的紧密联系，同时改进了无信号值与量子值之间差距的上界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从算子代数和算子空间的角度系统地研究双证明者量子游戏，特别是为这些游戏的各种值概念提供严格的数学刻画，并建立量子信息理论与算子代数之间的深层联系。

Method: 利用算子空间范畴中的张量范数来形式化描述双证明者量子游戏的值概念；通过算子空间理论建立无信号值的精确表述；借助量子信息论结果重新解释算子空间中Grothendieck定理的反例。

Result: 给出了无信号值的精确算子空间表述；证明了算子空间中Grothendieck定理的反例可视为量子信息论结果的直接推论；获得了无信号值与量子值之间差距的新上界，改进了此前已知的最佳估计。

Conclusion: 算子空间理论为双证明者量子游戏提供了自然的数学框架，无信号值在该框架下具有精确表述；量子信息论与算子代数之间存在深刻的相互联系，量子信息论结果可用于解决算子代数中的问题。

Abstract: The aim of this work is to study two-prover quantum games (i.e., games with quantum inputs and outputs) from an operator-algebraic and operator-space point of view. We characterize several notions of the value of such games by formulating them in terms of tensor norms in the category of operator spaces. The main results of the paper concern the description of the so-called no-signalling value of these games, for which we not only provide a precise operator-space formulation, but also establish close connections between this study and some problems in operator algebras. In particular, we show how the recent counterexample to Grothendieck's theorem for operator spaces given in \cite{Ara} can be understood as a direct consequence of results in quantum information theory. We also obtain new upper bounds on the gap between the no-signalling value and the quantum value of two-prover quantum games, improving the best previously known estimates.

</details>


### [124] [Exceptional Points revealed by the integrated imaginary scattering eigenphase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21684)
*J. Colín-Gálvez,V. Domínguez-Rocha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明并展示了散射矩阵的本征相位可以直接且相位灵敏地标记开放PT对称系统中的奇异点。通过一维PT对称量子二聚体模型，作者发现散射本征相位的虚部在奇异点附近形成符号相反的局域结构，并引入集成虚部散射本征相位G(γ)作为单一实验可测量标量。G(γ)在PT精确相中极小，在奇异点附近经历尖锐转变（拐点后饱和为平台）。拐点出现在γ_EP之前，与透射共振最大幅度一致，揭示了开放系统中增益/损耗不对称性与本征态合并由独立条件控制。该分析完全基于S矩阵，适用于光子、声学和微波等波动系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对开放PT对称系统中奇异点的探测主要关注透射/反射谱的幅度特征，缺乏直接利用散射矩阵本征相位作为相位灵敏探头的系统性理论框架。作者旨在填补这一空白，提出一种基于S矩阵本征相位的通用方法，以更精确地识别和表征奇异点，并揭示开放系统中增益/损耗不对称性与本征态合并之间的独立动力学。

Method: 构建一维PT对称量子二聚体（平衡增益与损耗）并与连续统导耦合；通过解析计算散射矩阵的本征相位（复数值），追踪其在PT精确相到PT破缺相转变过程中的演化；定义集成虚部散射本征相位G(γ)作为标量诊断量，分析其在奇异点附近的临界行为；比较G(γ)的拐点与透射共振最大幅度的位置关系。

Result: 散射本征相位的虚部在奇异点附近呈现符号相反的局域峰结构，标志增益/损耗不对称性在散射本征模中的涌现；G(γ)在PT精确相中几乎为零，在接近奇异点时经历一个尖锐转变：先是明显拐点，随后饱和为平台值；该拐点精确对应透射共振幅度最大值，但出现在奇异点γ_EP之前，表明开放系统中增益/损耗不对称性达到峰值与本征态合并并非同时发生。

Conclusion: 散射矩阵本征相位是探测开放PT对称系统奇异点的有效相位灵敏工具；G(γ)提供了一个简洁、实验可行的标量观测量；开放系统中本征态合并（奇异点）与增益/损耗不对称性的最显著表现（透射共振峰值）由不同条件控制，这种分离是系统与连续统有限耦合的直接指纹；该S矩阵层面的分析框架不依赖具体物理实现，具有跨波动系统的普适性。

Abstract: We propose and analytically demonstrate that the eigenphases of the scattering matrix provide direct, phase-sensitive signatures of exceptional points in open PT-symmetric systems. Using a one-dimensional PT-symmetric quantum dimer with balanced gain and loss, coupled to continuum leads, we track the evolution of the scattering eigenphases across the PT-exact to PT-broken transition. The imaginary parts of the eigenphases develop localized structures of opposite sign as the exceptional point is approached, reflecting the emergence of gain/loss asymmetry in the scattering eigenmodes. We introduce the integrated imaginary scattering eigenphase G(gamma), which condenses this information into a single experimentally accessible scalar. G(gamma) is negligibly small in the PT-exact phase and undergoes a sharp transition -- a pronounced inflection followed by saturation into a plateau -- near the exceptional point. The inflection point of G(gamma) precedes gamma_{EP} and coincides with the maximum amplitude of the transmission resonances, revealing that peak gain/loss asymmetry and eigenstate coalescence are governed by independent conditions in open systems -- a direct fingerprint of their finite coupling to the continuum. Because the analysis is formulated entirely at the level of the S-matrix -- without reference to any specific physical realization -- the results are universally applicable across wave systems, including photonic, acoustic, and microwave platforms where phase-resolved measurements are available.

</details>


### [125] [Finite-shot operating windows for probabilistic error cancellation and Clifford data regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21686)
*Vicenzo Scavino*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了概率误差消除(PEC)与Clifford数据回归(CDR)在有限样本下的均方误差边界，发现两者间存在CDR占优的工作窗口，其上限与校准失配度的平方成反比。同时证明了响应盲化CDR仅当目标噪声响应为理想值的仿射函数时才能消除一阶偏差，否则存在不可约的校准基底误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子误差缓解在噪声设备上不仅受残余偏差限制，还受缓解过程本身引入的散粒噪声和校准误差限制。现有方法（PEC和CDR）的误差特性尚缺乏系统的有限样本理论分析，尤其是两者之间的权衡关系与适用边界尚不明确。

Method: 推导了PEC、CDR和无缓解情况下Pauli可观测量估计的有限样本均方误差边界；证明了目标-响应投影定理；通过封闭形式的两量子比特计算和QAOA仿真验证理论预测。

Result: 精确PEC通过准概率方差开销消除目标偏差；CDR虽具有更小的目标-样本方差，但存在校准基底误差。两者竞争形成CDR占优窗口，其上限B∝1/(δ₁²p)。响应盲化CDR仅当目标噪声响应为仿射函数时才消除一阶偏差。

Conclusion: 建立了无缓解、CDR占优和PEC占优三种区域的完整相图，理论分析可扩展至二阶校准、对易Pauli哈密顿量、有限CDR训练样本和残余PEC模型偏差等情形。

Abstract: Quantum error mitigation on noisy devices is limited not only by residual bias but also by the shot noise and calibration errors introduced by the mitigation procedure itself. We derive finite-shot mean-square-error boundaries for probabilistic error cancellation (PEC), Clifford data regression (CDR), and no mitigation for noisy Pauli-observable estimates. Exact PEC removes the target bias under an exact noise inverse at the price of a quasi-probability variance overhead, whereas population linear CDR can have smaller target-shot variance but retains a calibration floor when the training and target noise responses do not match. This competition yields a finite CDR-dominant operating window whose upper endpoint scales as $B_{\mathrm{PEC}=\mathrm{CDR}}(p)\propto 1/(δ_1^2p)$, where $δ_1$ is the first-order CDR calibration mismatch. We further prove a target-response projection theorem showing that response-blind affine CDR removes the first-order bias only when the target noise response is affine in the ideal target value; otherwise a nonzero projection error gives an irreducible local calibration floor. The same mean-square-error formulation extends to second-order calibration, commuting Pauli Hamiltonians, finite CDR training shots, and residual PEC model bias. A closed-form two-qubit calculation and QAOA simulations support the predicted no-mitigation, CDR-dominant, and PEC-dominant regimes.

</details>


### [126] [Continuous-variable model for arbitrary image propagation via Dirac-comb expansion in Four-Wave Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21692)
*Fabián Ramírez-Pacheco,Andrea Basilio-Zárate,Hans Marin Florez,Pablo Solano,Carla Hermann-Avigliano*

Main category: quant-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四波混频模型缺乏统一的理论框架来描述同时编码在亮强度和非局域空间互相关中的图像信息传输，且对任意泵浦和种子横向轮廓的解析解尚未完善。

Method: 建立宏观连续变量模型，采用狄拉克梳展开泵浦场，推导出平均强度、方差和协方差的闭式表达式，适用于任意横向轮廓到所有非线性相互作用阶数。

Result: 平面波极限下恢复相位不敏感放大器结果；种子角谱传递到孪生光束强度，泵浦平方场角谱传递到探针-共轭空间互相关；刻画了由种子-泵浦束腰比和相互作用强度控制的高保真图像传输参数窗口。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了一个统一的解析框架，能从理论上解释在亮强度或空间互相关中编码信息的实验，揭示了四波混频中信息路由的物理机制。

Abstract: We develop a macroscopic continuous-variable model of stimulated four-wave mixing (4WM) yielding closed-form expressions for the mean intensity, variance, and covariance of bright probe and conjugate beams, valid for arbitrary transverse profiles of the pump and seed to all orders in the nonlinear interaction strength. The model uses a Dirac-comb expansion of the pump field, making the photon statistics computable pixel-by-pixel from one set of expressions. In the plane-wave limit, we recover the phase-insensitive amplifier results. Beyond this limit, the formalism explicitly demonstrates the spatial routing of information observed in earlier experiments: the seed angular spectrum is transferred to the twin-beam intensities, whereas the angular spectrum of the squared pump field is transferred to the spatial cross-correlation between the probe and conjugate. We characterize the parameter window of high-fidelity image transfer in the covariance, which is controlled by the seed-to-pump waist ratio and the interaction strength. Our model provides, in one analytical framework, a theoretical account of experiments encoding information in the bright intensities or non-locally in the spatial cross-correlations.

</details>


### [127] [Evaluation of Variational Quantum Classifiers (VQC) for Cyberattack Detection in the NISQ Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21715)
*Angelos Thomos,Theodore Andronikos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了变分量子分类器(VQC)在NISQ时代检测网络异常的有效性和结构限制。基于NSL-KDD数据集，一个4量子比特、24可训练参数的分类器使用振幅编码嵌入16个主成分，在测试集上达到88%的二分类准确率。与经典Tiny MLP（97%准确率）对比显示表达力差距。在多类分类任务中，VQC仅达9%准确率并出现模式坍缩。作者将此归因于量子概率空间中的过度压缩导致线性可分离性丧失，并提出基于16量子比特角度编码的替代方案以扩展希尔伯特空间表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究VQC在NISQ设备上检测网络异常的可行性，并揭示其性能瓶颈的根本原因——是优化问题还是表达力限制。

Method: 4量子比特VQC，24参数可训练ansatz，振幅编码嵌入16个主成分（从NSL-KDD的20%研究子集提取）。使用COBYLA和SPSA两种优化器，并与4节点Tiny MLP经典网络进行架构级对比。在多类设置中使用22个原始攻击类别的类平衡集进行容量探测。

Result: 二分类准确率88%（VQC） vs 97%（Tiny MLP）；多类分类准确率仅9%，出现模式坍缩（模型仅预测少数类别）。两种优化器性能相当，排除局部极小值问题。

Conclusion: VQC的性能瓶颈源于振幅编码导致的过度数据压缩，在量子概率空间中破坏了线性可分离性（符合Cover定理），而非优化器收敛问题。建议采用16量子比特角度编码方案以扩展希尔伯特空间，避免激进的经典降维。

Abstract: This paper investigates the effectiveness and structural limits of Variational Quantum Classifiers (VQC) for detecting network anomalies in the era of Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) systems. Using the official 20\% research subset of the NSL-KDD dataset, a 4-qubit classifier featuring a 24-parameter trainable ansatz was developed, utilizing amplitude encoding to embed 16 principal components. On a held-out partition of this subset, the model achieved a consistent binary-classification accuracy of 88\%. A comparative evaluation with two fundamentally different optimizers, COBYLA and SPSA, found that the observed performance plateau is not attributable to convergence to local minima, a result we interpret as consistent with an encoding-related expressibility limit rather than an optimization artifact. An architectural parity comparison with a classical neural network (a Tiny MLP with four nodes, achieving 97\% accuracy) highlighted the expressiveness gap associated with data overcompression into restricted quantum states. The model was trained on a class-balanced set spanning the 22 raw NSL-KDD attack categories and evaluated in-sample as a probe of representational capacity: under this configuration the VQC reached only 9\% accuracy and exhibited a degenerate mode collapse onto a small subset of classes. We interpret this behavior as consistent with the loss of linear separability induced by excessive compression in the quantum probability space, while explicitly noting that our experiments do not isolate the encoding from the ansatz depth, optimizer budget, and measurement-decoding scheme (see Limitations). Motivated by these observations and by Cover's theorem, we outline an alternative paradigm: a 16-qubit VQC with angle encoding that expands the Hilbert-space representation rather than relying on aggressive classical dimensionality reduction.

</details>


### [128] [On a Central Limit Theorem and Sanov's principle for quantum neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21721)
*Anderson Melchor Hernandez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子神经网络通过梯度流训练的混合专家模型（MoE）在专家数量趋于无穷时的波动性，建立了中心极限定理和大偏差原理，并证明其波动满足线性输运方程，同时MoE收敛到由神经正切核驱动的演化方程的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子神经网络中混合专家模型在专家数量发散时的统计波动行为及其收敛性质。

Method: 利用中心极限定理和大偏差原理（Sanov定理）分析MoE参数经验测度的波动，并结合线性输运方程描述其动态行为；通过神经正切核建立MoE极限函数的演化方程。

Result: 建立了MoE的CLT和Sanov原理；证明了参数经验测度的波动在极限下服从线性输运方程；MoE极限函数由量子神经网络对应的神经正切核驱动演化。

Conclusion: MoE在专家数趋于无穷时表现出可刻画的统计波动规律，其极限行为由线性输运方程和神经正切核共同决定。

Abstract: In this work, we study the fluctuations of a Mixture of Experts (MoE) generated by a quantum neural network trained via gradient flow on supervised learning problems. Our main results establish the Central Limit Theorem (CLT), and Sanov's principle for an MoE as the number of experts diverges. We demonstrate that the fluctuations of the empirical measure of its parameters close to its corresponding limit probability measure solve a linear transport equation. As a byproduct, we show that the MoE converges to a limit function which solves an evolution equation governed by the neural tangent kernel associated with the quantum neural network.

</details>


### [129] [Feedback-Controlled Magnon-Atom Entanglement and Photon Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21758)
*Yiping Lu,Yulong Hu,Zhuzheng Han,Shirong Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次将磁振子引入反馈控制量子系统，利用LSTM网络高效求解动力学问题，实现腔态的稳定化，并发现反馈显著增强光子关联与纠缠的周期性调控能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统存在精确控制难题，复杂混合平台的薛定谔方程难以求解，现有方法在高效模拟和稳定控制方面存在局限，亟需结合人工智能实现可扩展的量子态工程。

Method: 将磁振子引入反馈控制量子系统；采用长短期记忆（LSTM）网络数值传播概率振幅至稳态；应用相干反馈稳定内腔态。

Result: 光子-光子关联函数和并发度（磁振子-原子纠缠度量）随腔镜距离呈现周期性振荡；反馈在失谐变化时显著增强反聚束和聚束效应。

Conclusion: 证明了人工智能在量子动力学模拟中的有效性，为混合磁振子系统中的按需量子态工程开辟了新路径，有望应用于量子网络和量子信息处理。

Abstract: Quantum systems face inherent challenges in achieving precise control, and solving the Schrödinger equation is often intractable for complex hybrid platforms. Here, for the first time, we introduce a magnon into a feedback-controlled quantum system. To solve the dynamics numerically and efficiently, we employ a Long Short-Term Memory network, a machine learning approach, to propagate the probability amplitudes to the steady state. By applying coherent feedback, we effectively stabilize the intracavity state. Our results reveal that the photon-photon correlation function and the concurrence, a measure of magnon-atom entanglement, exhibit periodic oscillations with the cavity-mirror distance, and that feedback significantly enhances both antibunching and bunching when the detuning is varied. These findings not only demonstrate the power of artificial intelligence in quantum dynamics simulation, but also open a promising route for on-demand quantum state engineering in hybrid magnonic systems, with potential applications in quantum networks and quantum information processing.

</details>


### [130] [Dynamical Decoupling using Universal Optimal Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21762)
*Amit Devra,Emanuel Malvetti,Niklas J. Glaser,Abhishek Agarwal,Ivan Rungger,Santana Lujan,Max Werninghaus,Stefan Filipp,Leo Van Damme,Steffen J. Glaser*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种最优跟踪（optimal tracking）方法，将原始跟踪概念扩展到完全与状态无关的框架，用于设计动态解耦（DD）序列。该方法通过监控量子比特在预定义航点（waypoints）的演化，在保持规则重聚焦的同时动态补偿残余误差。超导量子比特实验验证了该方法在静态控制缺陷下抑制误差积累的能力，数值模拟进一步证明其在含时噪声下的稳健性。该方法是一种实用、硬件无关的短脉冲强鲁棒DD序列设计途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动态解耦依赖周期性重复短脉冲块来重聚焦量子比特状态，但脉冲块的不完善会导致残余误差积累，最终在长空闲时间下降低状态恢复保真度。需要一种新方法在保持规则重聚焦的同时动态补偿残余误差。

Method: 提出最优跟踪方法，将原始跟踪概念扩展到完全与状态无关的框架。通过在优化过程中监控量子比特在预定义航点处的演化，动态补偿残余误差，同时保持规则的周期重聚焦。

Result: 超导量子比特平台实验证实该方法在静态控制缺陷下能抑制误差积累，与数值预测一致。补充模拟表明基于最优跟踪的序列在含时噪声下仍保持强性能。

Conclusion: 最优跟踪是一种实用且硬件无关的方法，适用于在噪声量子设备上设计短脉冲、高鲁棒性的DD序列。

Abstract: Dynamical decoupling (DD) is a widely used and resource-efficient technique for error suppression, but conventional DD relies on periodically repeating a short pulse block to refocus the qubit state during idle periods. Imperfections in this block cause residual errors to accumulate, ultimately degrading state recovery over long idle times. Here, we introduce a universal optimal tracking approach that extends the original tracking concept to a fully state-independent setting for designing DD sequences. By monitoring the qubit's evolution at predefined waypoints during optimization, the method dynamically compensates residual errors while preserving regular refocusing. Experimental demonstrations on a superconducting-qubit platform confirm the suppression of error accumulation under static control imperfections, in agreement with numerical predictions. Complementary simulations further show that optimal-tracking-based sequences maintain strong performance under time-dependent noise. These results establish optimal tracking as a practical and hardware-agnostic approach to designing short, robust DD sequences suitable for noisy quantum devices.

</details>


### [131] [Common causes for quantum identical particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21768)
*A. Hovhannisyan,S. Weigert,A. E. Allahverdyan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在交换测量下，两个全同量子粒子的联合概率是否存在对称的共同原因。研究表明，要么对称的共同原因不存在（即粒子可被隐藏区分），要么存在但平凡（即单一共同原因无法解释所有单次测量相关性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bell不等式违背表明非交换测量下非同粒子的联合概率不存在单一共同原因。但交换测量下非同粒子的联合概率存在非平凡共同原因。本文进一步考虑全同粒子情形，探究其对称共同原因的存在性问题。

Method: 考虑两个全同量子粒子，其密度矩阵和可观测量（厄米算符）必然具有置换对称性。文章从同一测量数据出发，考察多种定义联合概率的方式，分析对称共同原因的存在条件。

Result: 得出两种可能结论：（1）对称共同原因不存在，即粒子可以被隐藏区分；（2）对称筛选变量存在但是平凡的，即单一共同原因无法解释所有单次测量相关性。

Conclusion: 全同粒子在交换测量下的共同原因问题存在本质困难：要么破坏全同性（可区分性），要么共同原因的解释力不足（平凡性）。

Abstract: Violations of Bell's inequalities imply that joint probabilities generated by non-commutative measurements on two (non-identical) quantum particles do not have a single common cause. But joint probabilities generated for such non-identical particles via commutative measurements do have non-trivial common cause variables. We focus on commutative measurements and consider two identical quantum particles, whose density matrices and observables (hermitian operators) are necessarily permutation-symmetric. It is natural to demand that the common cause describing joint probabilities is also permutation symmetric, i.e., it acts symmetrically on both particles. Looking at various ways of defining joint probabilities from the same measurement data, we conclude that either symmetric common causes need not exist (i.e., that the particles can be hiddenly distinguishable), or that symmetric screening variables exist, but they are trivial, i.e., no single common cause can explain all single-measurement correlations.

</details>


### [132] [First-Quantized Relativistic Quantum Simulation with Periodic and Dirichlet Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21794)
*Jeongho Bang,Timothy P. Spiller,Kyunghyun Baek,Kyoungho Cho,Jaewoo Joo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在一维有限域上使用周期边界条件和狄利克雷边界条件进行第一量子化相对论量子模拟的方法论，通过构造弱相对论晶格哈密顿量并利用平移测量和边界项重构动量矩，在多种基准势场中验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子模拟方法在处理相对论性粒子时面临边界条件处理的挑战，尤其是在一维有限域上需要同时保证边界一致性和离散化动量矩的重构精度。

Method: 从正能相对论动能算符出发，构造弱相对论晶格哈密顿量，其主导修正项需边界一致的离散化动量矩〈P̂²〉和〈P̂⁴〉。PBC下通过幺正循环平移重构动量矩，DBC下使用开链有限差分，并在量子比特寄存器中通过循环平移估计器加边界项消除非物理环绕链接。能量估计流程结合平移测量、端点/近端点重叠概率和位置基采样。

Result: 在无势场、余弦势（PBC）、无限深方势阱和谐振子势（DBC）的基准测试中，有限次采样实验显示估计器重构与直接矩阵评估具有良好一致性，同时能够分离有限网格离散化误差、弱相对论截断误差和测量误差。

Conclusion: 该框架为第一量子化相对论量子模拟提供了边界条件通用的方法论基础，验证了在典型势场下的准确性和误差可分离性。

Abstract: In this work, we present a methodology for first-quantized relativistic quantum simulation on one-dimensional finite domains under the two boundary conditions most commonly used in lattice models: periodic boundary conditions (PBC) and Dirichlet boundary conditions (DBC). Starting from the positive-energy relativistic kinetic operator, we construct weakly relativistic lattice Hamiltonians whose leading correction requires the boundary-consistent discretized momentum moments $\langle \hat{P}^{2}\rangle$ and $\langle \hat{P}^{4}\rangle$. These moments are reconstructed in the PBC Hamiltonian from moments of a unitary cyclic translation while the DBC Hamiltonian uses the open-chain finite-difference. In a qubit-register implementation, it can be evaluated as the corresponding cyclic translation estimator plus boundary-local terms that remove the unphysical wrap-around link. The resulting energy-estimation workflow uses translation measurements for the kinetic terms, a small number of endpoints and near-endpoints overlap probabilities for DBC, and position-basis sampling for diagonal potentials. We valdate the framework of the relativistic quantum simulation in various benchmark potentials such as no potential and a cosine potential for PBC as well as an infinite square well and a harmonic potential for DBC, with finite-shot sampling tests. These benchmarks show good agreement between the estimator reconstruction and direct matrix evaluation while separating the finite-grid discretization, weak-relativistic truncation, and measurement errors.

</details>


### [133] [Dynamical Tipping in a Quantum Limit Cycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21849)
*Ya-Xin Xiang,Zhengyang Bai,Yu-Qiang Ma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过工程化一个量子极限环，展示了非平衡系统如何通过一阶吸收态相变与反馈机制之间的耦合，在强相互作用的驱动-耗散开放量子系统中维持时空有序性。该极限环展现出由乘法量子噪声驱动的动力学关联，并通过周期性穿越临界点实现状态转换的早期预警信号，伴随能量耗散率和动力学熵的阶梯式增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 演示非平衡系统通过能量耗散和熵产生维持时空有序性的原理，具体通过工程化量子极限环来实现。

Method: 将一阶吸收态相变与反馈机制耦合，使序参量和控制参数相互调节，形成量子极限环。量子极限环受乘法量子噪声驱动，周期性穿越临界点（tipping points）。

Result: 量子极限环展现出：(1) 由乘法量子噪声驱动的动力学关联；(2) 周期性穿越临界点作为状态转换的鲁棒早期预警信号；(3) 每个临界事件附近动力学磁化率增强、长程关联瞬态上升，远离后消失；(4) 临界转变伴随能量耗散率急剧增加，导致动力学熵产生随时间阶梯式累积。

Conclusion: 该结果为在强相互作用驱动-耗散开放量子系统中实现动力学涨落和长程关联提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Nonequilibrium systems maintain spatiotemporal order through energy dissipation and entropy production. Here, we demonstrate this principle by engineering a quantum limit cycle that emerges from the interplay between a first-order absorbing-state phase transition and feedback mechanism coupling order and control parameters. The quantum limit cycle manifests dynamical correlations driven by multiplicative quantum noise and periodic traverses through tipping points, which act as a robust early-warning signal for state transitions. This periodic crossing results in the enhanced dynamical susceptibility and transient rise of long-range correlations near each tipping event and their subsequent disappearance away from it. These critical transitions are accompanied by a sharp increase in the energy dissipation rate, leading to a stepwise accumulation of dynamical entropy production over time. Our results provide a way for realizing dynamical fluctuations and long-range correlations in strongly interacting driven-dissipative open quantum systems.

</details>


### [134] [Demonstration of a Monolithic and Fully Telecom-Fiber-Compatible Tunable Source of Polarization Entangled Photon Pairs Based on a van der Waals Material](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21874)
*Benjamin Laudert,Fatemeh Abtahi,Duk Choi,Dragomir Neshev,Falk Eilenberger*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并展示了一种基于单模光纤、可调谐的偏振纠缠光子对源，工作于近红外电信波段，可直接部署于标准基础设施。该源利用3R-MoS₂（一种过渡金属二硫族化合物）亚微米薄膜中的自发参量下转换（SPDC）产生光子对，借助晶体二阶非线性磁化率张量的本征对称性，通过改变泵浦光偏振即可控制生成的双光子偏振态，可产生四种最大纠缠Bell态中的两种以及完全共偏振的光子对。在低泵浦功率下观察到背景光致发光信号的非线性下降，实现了(8.3±1.8)×10³的符合-偶然比（CAR），这是迄今范德华材料中SPDC的最高记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光纤集成纠缠光子源需要额外的光学元件、精确对准或复杂的设计参数工程，缺乏简便性和可调谐性。作者旨在开发一种更简单、可调谐且可直接部署于标准光纤基础设施的纠缠光子源。

Method: 在单模光纤连接器之间放置3R-MoS₂亚微米薄膜，利用该材料的二阶非线性磁化率张量对称性，通过调控泵浦光偏振来控制产生的双光子偏振纠缠态。

Result: 实现了两种最大纠缠Bell态和完全共偏振光子对的产生；在低泵浦功率下观察到背景光致发光非线性下降；CAR值达到(8.3±1.8)×10³，为范德华材料中SPDC的最高记录。

Conclusion: 该混合方法在可调谐性和简洁性上显著优于传统光纤集成纠缠光子源，是迄今范德华材料中SPDC性能最佳的方案，适合部署于实际光纤通信基础设施。

Abstract: We present a tunable, single-mode-optical-fiber-based source of polarization entangled photon pairs for the near-infrared telecommunication band that is deployable in standard infrastructure. The photon pairs are generated via spontaneous parametric down-conversion (SPDC) in a submicron-scale thin film of the inversion-broken rhombohedral polytype of the transition metal dichalcogenide molybdenum disulfide (3R-MoS$_2$), located between two fiber connectors. By exploiting the intrinsic symmetries of the second-order nonlinear susceptibility tensor of 3R-MoS$_2$, this hybrid approach offers control over the generated two-photon polarization state through the incident pump polarization. Most notably, two of the four maximally entangled Bell states, as well as fully co-polarized pairs can be produced. This represents a substantial improvement in terms of tunability and simplicity over established fiber-integrated sources, which require additional optical elements, precise alignment, or careful engineering of design parameters. Additionally, a nonlinear drop in the background photoluminescence signal of 3R-MoS$_2$ is observed at low pump powers, allowing us to reach a coincidences-to-accidentals ratio (CAR) of $(8.3\pm1.8)\times10^{3}$, the highest value recorded for SPDC in van der Waals materials to date.

</details>


### [135] [Shuttling in Bidimensional Segmented Ion-Trap Quantum Processors with T-Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21899)
*J. Durandau,C. A. Brunet,F. Schmidt-Kaler,U. Poschinger,F. Mailhot,Y. Bérubé-Lauzière*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于穿梭操作的离子阱量子处理器中，一维线性架构与二维量子CCD架构在编译量子算法典型构建模块时的性能差异。通过引入线性穿梭和结穿梭的成本函数，比较了QFT、Carry、Adder、Shift、Comparator等电路的总成本及随量子比特数量的扩展特性，发现二维架构在相同穿梭成本下优于一维架构，并讨论了基于单元阵列的处理器设计以实现穿梭高效的协同优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在优化量子处理器架构，通过协同设计匹配特定任务并以穿梭高效的方式进行扩展升级。

Method: 分别引入线性穿梭和结穿梭各自操作的成本函数，比较两类架构下量子电路构建模块（QFT、Carry、Adder、Shift、Comparator）的总成本及其随量子比特数的扩展特性。

Result: 在相同穿梭成本下，二维架构性能优于一维线性架构，且优势随离子数量增加而扩大。

Conclusion: 二维量子CCD架构相较一维线性架构具有更好的扩展性和效率，基于单元阵列的处理器设计能够实现穿梭高效的协同优化。

Abstract: Shuttle-based trapped ion quantum processors typically employ a one-dimensional (1D) linear architecture to transport ion-qubits between one ore more laser interaction zones where the quantum gates are implemented, along with several qubit register storage segments. The two-dimensional (2D) quantum CCD architecture employs also T- or X-junctions for an improved scaling and efficiency. Here, we explore the shuttling layer in the compilation of quantum algorithm typical building blocks in such architecture. To weight the effort of linear shuttle and junction shuttle, we introduce individual cost functions for each operation. This allows comparing the total cost for quantum circuit building blocks such as the QFT, Carry, Adder, Shift, and Comparator circuits. We study their scaling properties with increased qubit numbers. At equivalent transport cost for junction and linear shuttling, we show that 2D architectures outperform the 1D linear trap with the ratio improving as the number of ions increases. Finally, we discuss the use of cells, such that the entire processor is constructed from a 2D array of such interconnected cells. The work aims to optimize quantum processor architectures, implementing a co-design that fits to the specific task and scaling up in a shuttle-efficient way.

</details>


### [136] [Quantifying and Probing Multipartite Entanglement via Minimum Entanglement Drop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21950)
*Dong-Dong Dong,Xue-Ke Song,Liu Ye,Dong Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最小纠缠降（tracing out单个粒子后全局纠缠的减少）的多体纠缠单调量，利用tangle和negativity构建计算高效版本，证明其LOCC下的单调性，并建立了纠缠降作为结构探针的操作框架，可区分图拓扑、实验估计和动态追踪纠缠网络，给出了n比特W态在环境噪声下的精确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化真正的多体纠缠仍然是一个重大挑战，现有度量难以同时满足计算可行性和对W类态非零响应等要求。

Method: 定义最小纠缠降作为多体纠缠单调量；利用tangle和negativity构造计算高效变体，确保对W类态非零；严格证明其在LOCC下的单调性；建立纠缠降作为结构探针的操作框架，结合经典阴影实现实验估计和动态追踪；推导n比特W态在环境噪声下的精确解析解。

Result: 三体情况下最小tangle降等价于最小pairwise concurrence；纠缠降可识别不可分离团簇、提取区分图拓扑的连通性指纹；对5比特纠错码等高度鲁棒态诊断灵敏度严格为零。

Conclusion: 提出的纠缠降单调量兼具理论有效性和实验可行性，为多体纠缠结构和动力学研究提供了新工具，但也存在对某些鲁棒态诊断失效的局限性。

Abstract: Quantifying genuine multipartite entanglement remains a significant challenge. We propose a multipartite entanglement monotone defined by the minimum entanglement drop -- the reduction in global one-to-group entanglement upon tracing out a single particle. We formulate a computationally efficient variant using tangle and negativity to ensure non-vanishing values for W-class states, and rigorously prove it is a valid monotone under local operations and classical communication. In the tripartite regime, the minimum tangle drop is physically equivalent to the minimum pairwise concurrence. We establish an operational framework where the entanglement drop acts as a structural probe: by assessing sensitivity to qubit loss, it identifies inseparable clusters, extracting connectivity fingerprints that uniquely differentiate graph topologies within the same local Clifford equivalence class. Integrating this mapping with classical shadows enables efficient experimental estimation and dynamic tracking of entanglement network evolution. We derive exact analytical solutions for n-qubit W states under environmental noise, revealing robust scaling behaviors. Finally, we acknowledge limitations, noting that diagnostic sensitivity strictly vanishes for highly robust states such as the 5-qubit error-correcting code.

</details>


### [137] [Acceleration of Free Electrons by Photonic Time-Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21953)
*Lior Bar-Hillel,Alexey Gorlach,Ido Kaminer,Mordechai Segev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了时变介质中自由电子与光子的量子相互作用，发现周期性调制会在动量间隙内指数级放大电子-光子耦合，实现任意大的动量转移。通过将光制备为双模压缩真空态，电子动量增长速度超过其光谱展宽，从而确立了时间调制光子介质作为加速自由电子和塑造其量子态的平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索时变介质中自由电子与光子的量子相互作用，寻求突破传统电子-光子耦合强度限制的新机制，以实现更大的动量转移和电子加速。

Method: 量子电动力学分析：研究时变介质中自由电子与光子的相互作用；利用周期性调制在动量间隙内放大耦合；采用双模压缩真空态制备光场。

Result: 周期性调制在动量间隙内指数级放大电子-光子耦合，实现任意大的动量转移；在双模压缩真空态下，电子动量增长速度超过光谱展宽。

Conclusion: 时间调制光子介质可作为加速自由电子和塑造其量子态的有效平台。

Abstract: We study the quantum interaction between free electrons and photons in a time-varying media, and find that periodic modulation exponentially amplifies electron-photon coupling within momentum gaps, enabling arbitrarily large momentum transfer. By preparing the light in a two-mode squeezed vacuum state, the electron momentum grows faster than its spectral spreading, establishing time-modulated photonic media as a platform for accelerating free-electrons and shaping their quantum state.

</details>
