### [138] [Quantum Error Suppression via Symmetry-Averaged Virtual Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21962)
*Canyu He,Chunhua Zeng,Ruyu Yang,Yongdan Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出对称平均虚拟蒸馏(SAVD)协议，通过在虚拟蒸馏之前施加对称平均，在密度矩阵层面同时抑制算法近似和硬件噪声，数值实验证明其在各向异性海森堡链上优于单独使用任一方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期的量子模拟同时受算法近似和硬件噪声影响，现有误差抑制策略通常分别处理这两类误差源，缺乏统一框架。

Method: SAVD先通过对称标记实现构建对称平均输出系综，保留对称不变的目标贡献同时平均掉对称相关分支的残余成分，再对平均后的系综施加虚拟蒸馏以放大主本征分量。

Result: 在存在相干算法误差和硬件噪声的各向同性海森堡链上，SAVD展示了改进的精度。

Conclusion: SAVD提供了一种基于对称性的通用架构来提升量子模拟性能，对称平均充当虚拟蒸馏的状态预处理层。

Abstract: Reliable quantum simulation is limited by both algorithmic approximations and hardware noise, which usually coexist in the output of near-term and early fault-tolerant quantum devices. Existing error-suppression strategies often target these error sources separately. Here, we introduce symmetry-averaged virtual distillation (SAVD), an error-suppression protocol that applies symmetry averaging before virtual distillation and treats both imperfections at the density-matrix level. The protocol constructs a symmetry-averaged output ensemble from symmetry-labeled implementations, leaving the symmetry-invariant target contribution unchanged while averaging residual components over symmetry-related branches. Virtual distillation (VD) is then applied to this averaged ensemble, rather than to the raw output state, to amplify its dominant eigencomponent. We analyze the resulting spectral suppression mechanism and identify the role of symmetry averaging as a state-preconditioning layer for VD. Numerical demonstrations on an isotropic Heisenberg chain show improved accuracy in the presence of both coherent algorithmic errors and hardware noise. Our results provide a general symmetry-based architecture for enhancing quantum simulations.

</details>


### [139] [Fine-Tuning Large Language Models for Quantum Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21974)
*Katherine Ip,Casey R. Myers,Udaya Parampalli,James Quach,Peiyong Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何通过微调流水线使大语言模型(LLM)具备真正的量子推理能力，而非任务特定的模式匹配。以量子电路模拟为训练目标，提出了监督微调(SFT)和两阶段SFT+GRPO两种方法。结果表明，SFT在分布内和外推任务上接近完美，SFT+GRPO则牺牲部分分布内精度换取对更大量子比特系统的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何开发能够赋予LLM真正的量子推理能力（而非任务特定模式匹配）的微调流水线，从而将LLM应用于量子计算这一高度专业化的领域。

Method: 以量子电路模拟为训练目标（预测量子门操作序列的测量概率分布），提出两种微调流水线：(1) 在逐门状态向量模拟轨迹上进行监督微调(SFT)；(2) 两阶段SFT+GRPO方法，先SFT再通过组相对策略优化(GRPO)结合可验证奖励进行强化学习。

Result: SFT在分布内和门数外推任务上达到接近完美的精度，显著优于基模型和GPT-OSS-120B基线。SFT+GRPO虽牺牲部分分布内精度，但能泛化到SFT无法处理的更大量子比特系统。两种方法均显著优于基线。

Conclusion: 针对显式推理轨迹进行目标微调是提升LLM量子推理能力的有效策略，SFT+GRPO的两阶段方法在精度与泛化之间取得了有益的权衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit abilities beyond natural language modelling and text generation. Recent advances in their reasoning capabilities have spurred interest in applying LLMs to complex scientific tasks requiring deep domain expertise and sophisticated reasoning. Quantum computing, as a highly specialised field with significant knowledge barriers and hardware constraints, could greatly benefit from such advancements. However, a key open question that first must be answered is: How can we develop fine-tuning pipelines that instil genuine quantum reasoning in LLMs, rather than task-specific pattern matching? We study this question through quantum circuit simulation as a training objective, where the model must predict the measurement probability distribution resulting from a sequence of quantum gate operations. We propose and compare two fine-tuning pipelines: (1) Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) on explicit gate-by-gate state-vector simulation traces, and (2) a two-stage SFT+Group Relative Policy Optimisation (GRPO) approach that sequentially applies SFT followed by GRPO with verifiable rewards. Our findings show that SFT achieves near-perfect in-distribution and gate-count extrapolation accuracy, significantly outperforming both the base model and the GPT-OSS-120B baseline. SFT+GRPO trades some in-distribution precision for better generalisation to larger qubit systems that SFT alone cannot handle. Both pipelines significantly outperform the baselines, demonstrating that targeted fine-tuning on explicit reasoning traces is an effective strategy for advancing quantum reasoning in LLMs.

</details>


### [140] [Detector-Grade Germanium as a Low-Disorder Host for Indium-Acceptor Spin Qubits: A Five-Qubit Materials-to-Architecture Design Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22067)
*D. -M. Mei,K. -M. Dong,S. A. Panamaldeniya,N. Budhathoki,S. Chhetri,A. Prem,S. Bhattarai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于探测器级高纯锗中故意掺入铟受主的受主束缚空穴自旋量子比特平台，通过理论引导的材料与器件设计，展示了其在量子信息处理中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锗中受主束缚空穴自旋为半导体量子信息处理提供了有前景但尚未充分探索的路径，亟需理论指导下的材料与器件设计研究。

Method: 结合超纯Ge（残余杂质~10^10 cm^{-3}）与目标In密度~2×10^14 cm^{-3}（受主间距~170 nm），设计1 μm长有源通道（含约5个受主），分析物理基础、器件架构、应变与无序限制、耦合层次、建模流程、制备路径和扩展前景。

Result: 探测器级Ge可将无序控制在受主-空穴量子比特可接受水平；自旋-轨道耦合能带支持全电操控及偶极或声子介导耦合；直接交换仅适用于近邻或栅极增强耦合；声子晶体工程可作为第二阶段增强手段。

Conclusion: 探测器级Ge In受主量子比特是介于施主杂质量子比特与全门控Ge空穴自旋硬件之间的可信中间架构。剩余挑战包括统计受主分布、界面无序、电荷噪声、读出集成及实验验证。

Abstract: Acceptor-bound hole spins in germanium (Ge) offer a promising but underexplored route to semiconductor quantum information processing. We present a theory-guided design study of a detector-grade Ge acceptor-spin platform based on intentionally incorporated indium (In) acceptors in ultra-high-purity Ge. The proposed materials strategy combines a residual impurity background near $10^{10} \mathrm{cm^{-3}}$ with a target In density of approximately $2\times10^{14} \mathrm{cm^{-3}}$, corresponding to an acceptor spacing of about 170 nanometer. A 1 $μ$m-long active channel with a suitable transverse mode volume can contain about five acceptors on average, enabling a statistically selected post-fabrication register rather than a deterministically placed chain. We analyze the physical basis, device architecture, strain and disorder limits, coupling hierarchy, modeling workflow, fabrication pathway, and scaling prospects. Our results indicate that detector-grade Ge can suppress uncontrolled bulk electrostatic and strain disorder to levels compatible with acceptor-hole qubits, while the spin--orbit-active valence-band manifold supports all-electrical control and dipolar or phonon-mediated coupling. Direct exchange is treated as a close-pair or gate-enhanced interaction rather than the generic mean-spacing coupling. Phononic crystal engineering is identified as a second-stage enhancement for suppressing unwanted acoustic modes and enabling selected cavity-mediated interactions after baseline control, readout, and nearest-neighbor coupling are validated. Remaining challenges include statistical acceptor placement, interface disorder, charge noise, readout integration, and experimental validation. This work identifies detector-grade Ge In-acceptor qubits as a credible intermediate architecture between donor-based impurity qubits and fully gate-defined Ge hole-spin hardware.

</details>


### [141] [Iterative quantum phase estimation with cQED encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22071)
*Changchun Zhong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于电路量子电动力学（circuit QED）的量子相位估计新协议，通过二进制阈值测试序列替代逆量子傅里叶变换，利用玻色子模作为量子存储器将相位二进制数字编码为相空间旋转方向，并通过零差测量顺序读出，在海森堡极限精度下实现指数级降低的失败概率，显著简化硬件复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子相位估计算法依赖于深度受控幺正操作和逆量子傅里叶变换，导致电路深度大、硬件开销高，难以在当前量子平台上高效实现。

Method: 提出基于电路QED的替代方案：通过一系列二进制阈值测试提取相位，用玻色子模作为量子存储器将相位的二进制数字编码为相空间旋转方向，再通过高保真度零差测量顺序读取这些数字。

Result: 该协议实现了海森堡尺度的估计精度（Heisenberg scaling），同时提供指数级抑制的失败概率，显著降低了硬件复杂度。

Conclusion: 该方案用玻色子自由度替代辅助电路，为在电路QED平台上实现高精度量子相位估计提供了一条实用且硬件高效的路径。

Abstract: Quantum phase estimation is a cornerstone algorithm for determining eigenvalues of unitary operators or Hamiltonians with Heisenberg-limited precision. Conventional implementations rely on deep controlled-unitary operations together with an inverse quantum Fourier transform, resulting in substantial circuit depth and hardware overhead. Here, we propose a conceptually simple and experimentally feasible alternative that exploits the toolbox of circuit quantum electrodynamics. The protocol extracts the phase through a sequence of binary threshold tests, eliminating the need for an inverse quantum Fourier transform. A bosonic mode serves as an efficient quantum memory in which the binary digits of the phase are encoded into the direction of phase-space rotations. These digits are then read out sequentially via high-fidelity homodyne measurements. We show that the protocol achieves Heisenberg scaling in estimation precision while simultaneously providing exponentially suppressed failure probability. By replacing the ancillary circuit with a bosonic degree of freedom, the scheme significantly reduces hardware complexity and offers a practical route toward implementing high-precision quantum phase estimation on circuit quantum electrodynamics platforms.

</details>


### [142] [Optimizing Pump Conditions of Parametric Amplifiers for Fast Multiplexed Readout of Superconducting Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22080)
*Jeongwon Kim,Wei Dai,Omrie Ovdat,Akiva Feintuch,Nir Alfasi,Yonuk Chong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对快速多路复用读取的参量放大器泵浦条件优化策略，通过最大化限制性量子比特在读取频率处的信噪比提升，来最小化总多路复用读取时间。实验在五量子比特系统中验证了该方法，将读取时间缩短了320纳秒，且不降低任何量子比特的目标信噪比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参量放大器的性能高度依赖于泵浦条件的选择，而传统优化方法（如优化所有量子比特的平均信噪比提升）并非针对快速多路复用读取场景的最优方案。作者旨在提出一种能够最小化总多路复用读取时间的泵浦校准策略。

Method: 识别多路复用读取链中的"限制性量子比特"——即达到目标信噪比所需读取时间最长的量子比特。将放大器泵浦条件设置为最大化该量子比特在读取频率处的信噪比提升，而非优化所有量子比特的平均信噪比提升。

Result: 在五量子比特多路复用读取链（使用行波参量放大器）上的实验表明，与优化所有量子比特的平均信噪比提升相比，该策略将多路复用读取时间缩短了320纳秒，且未降低任何量子比特的目标信噪比。

Conclusion: 所提出的泵浦校准策略能有效减少多路复用读取时间，特别适用于需要快速、高保真度量子比特读取的大规模量子计算系统。

Abstract: Low-noise parametric amplifiers are widely used as the first-stage amplifier in qubit readout chains. The performance of parametric amplifiers depends sensitively on the choice of the pump condition. We propose a strategy for determining the pump condition that is tailored for fast multiplexed readout. Choosing the amplifier pump to maximize the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) improvement at the readout frequency of the limiting qubit--the qubit that requires the longest readout time to reach a target SNR--minimizes the total multiplexed readout time. We demonstrate our pump calibration strategy experimentally on a five-qubit multiplexed readout chain with a traveling-wave parametric amplifier. Using our strategy, we reduce the multiplexed readout time by 320 ns compared to optimizing the average SNR improvement on all qubits, without degrading the target SNR for any qubit.

</details>


### [143] [Transient Information Partition in Coherent Exciton-Phonon-Photon Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22099)
*Kunio Ishida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过子系统分辨的量子互信息（QMI）研究相干激子-声子-光子体系中的瞬态信息划分，发现光学活性不能单独决定光诱导瞬态态的特征，并识别出从极化激元到极化子的宽泛交叉区域以及一个隐藏的瞬态关联结构（边界相邻重分布脊），该脊既非静态相界也非光学吸收增强，而是与有限能量极化子激发相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在相干激子-声子-光子耦合体系中，传统光学吸收和能谱分析难以全面刻画光诱导瞬态态的量子关联特征，需要一种基于信息论的方法来区分不同自由度之间的信息共享模式。

Method: 利用子系统分辨的量子互信息（QMI）分析激子-声子-光子模型在J-ν参数平面上的动力学，结合时间平均QMI图、吸收光子数对比、以及变分强度函数分析。

Result: 发现激子中心的信息划分识别出极化激元到极化子的宽泛交叉区域；声子中心的信息划分揭示出一个边界相邻重分布脊，该脊在基态相图和光子吸收图中均不存在，且与有限能量极化子激发相关。

Conclusion: QMI信息划分为表征光与物质自由度联合参与的相干光诱导瞬态态提供了基于关联的理论框架，能揭示传统光学测量无法捕捉的隐藏瞬态关联结构。

Abstract: We study transient information partition in a coherent exciton-phonon-photon system using subsystem-resolved quantum mutual information (QMI). By employing a model with excitonic, phononic, and photonic degrees of freedom, we analyze the dynamics in the $J$-$ν$ plane, where $J$ characterizes excitonic delocalization and $ν$ denotes the exciton-phonon coupling strength. By comparing time-averaged QMI maps with the absorbed photon number, we show that optical activity alone does not determine the character of the light-induced transient state. The exciton-centered information partition identifies a broad crossover between polariton-like and polaron-like transient responses, depending on whether excitonic information is mainly shared with the photon or phonon subsystem. In contrast, the phonon-centered partition reveals a sharper boundary-adjacent redistribution ridge near the boundary between the zero- and one-exciton ground-state sectors. This ridge is absent from both the ground-state sector map and the photon-absorption map, indicating that it is neither a static sector boundary nor an enhancement of optical absorption. A variational strength-function analysis connects the ridge to a region-II-like finite-energy polaronic excitation whose dominant spectral weight lies near the one-phonon energy, and thus the ridge represents a hidden transient correlation structure in which a limited amount of phonon-related information is preferentially shared with the photon subsystem before being predominantly allocated to exciton-phonon dressing. These results show that QMI-based information partition provides a correlation-based framework for characterizing coherent light-induced transient states in which optical and material degrees of freedom jointly participate quantum mechanically.

</details>


### [144] [Spectral and thermodynamic properties of supersymmetric quantum systems with self-adjoint deformed momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22107)
*J. A. Oke,F. A. Dossa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过GEMO formalism构建了严格的几何变形量子系统框架，揭示了su(1,1)对称性，并首次完整表征了线性变形（热容发散峰）和二次变形（C/kB→0.6饱和）的热力学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖于边界条件厄米性，缺乏内在自伴性保证，且未系统研究几何变形系统的热力学性质。

Method: 基于广义扩展动量算符（GEMO）形式构建严格自伴的变形动量算符，采用非厄米超对称分解方案，利用Euler-Maclaurin近似解析计算配分函数。

Result: 获得精确解析能谱；发现线性变形导致态密度在最大能量处累积并产生发散热容峰；二次变形使热容饱和于C/kB→0.6，低于Dulong-Petit极限。

Conclusion: 几何变形可作为可调参数用于调控弯曲纳米结构中的量子热力学响应。

Abstract: We establish a rigorous framework for quantum systems with geometric deformations by constructing a strictly self-adjoint deformed momentum operator through the generalized extended momentum operator (GEMO) formalism. Unlike previous approaches relying on boundary-condition hermiticity, our method ensures intrinsic self-adjointness for both linear ($μ(x)=αx$) and quadratic ($μ(x)=αx^{2}$) deformations within a unified non-Hermitian supersymmetric factorization scheme. This yields exact analytical spectra while revealing hidden $\mathfrak{su}(1,1)$ symmetry structures. Crucially, we provide the first complete thermodynamic characterization of such systems by analytically evaluating the partition function via the Euler--Maclaurin approximation. Geometric deformation fundamentally reshapes the density of states $ρ(E)$, producing distinct thermal signatures: a divergent heat capacity peak for linear deformation due to state accumulation near a maximal energy, and a saturation $C/k_{\mathrm{B}}\to 0.6$ (below the Dulong--Petit limit) for quadratic deformation. These results establish geometric deformation as a tunable parameter for engineering quantum thermodynamic responses in curved nanostructures.

</details>


### [145] [Emergence of Boolean Facts from Markovian Coarse-Graining in Relational Quantum Causal Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22127)
*Yipeng Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种算子代数机制，通过完全正量子操作的粗粒化极限，从局部量子动力学中涌现出精确的布尔记录（经典事实），无需预先植入微观结构。核心结果是：在满足渐近阿贝尔性的条件下，粗粒化通道的Cesàro均值收敛到交换的von Neumann代数，其投影格构成完备布尔代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决量子理论中经典事实（布尔记录）如何从纯量子动力学中涌现的问题，避免人为插入"分裂记录实验室"等微观结构假设，建立从局部完全正量子操作到经典布尔代数的严格数学机制。

Method: 1）定义影响代数：从源干预的预对偶响应出发，结合背景策略类构造影响代数，将其中心投影定义为精确事件；2）引入状态保持的范数单位完全正粗粒化通道；3）证明若该通道的Cesàro均值收敛到Choi-Effros红外范围，且该范围在GNS半范数下渐近阿贝尔，则红外代数为交换von Neumann代数；4）给出有限扇区块本原判据（基于局域性和量子混沌/扰码性质），保证指数级抑制的渐近阿贝尔性。

Result: 建立了从局部量子操作到经典布尔代数的涌现机制：经典事实不是任意Type-III局域代数的投影，而是由渐近阿贝尔的完全正红外极限选出的中心投影。有限扇区块本原判据保证了该机制的物理可实现性，并给出指数级抑制的定量控制。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个保守的数学表述：经典事实是渐近阿贝尔红外极限选出的中心投影，而非任意投影。这为量子-经典过渡、量子测量问题和量子引力中的涌现经典时空提供了严格的算子代数框架。

Abstract: We formulate an operator-algebraic mechanism by which exact Boolean records can arise from local completely positive quantum operations without being imposed as microscopic structure. The kinematic input is an algebraic process functional assigning probabilities to local normal completely positive operations in a finite operational context. From the predual response of a target algebra to source interventions, relative to a background strategy class, we define an influence algebra; exact events are then, by definition, the projections in its center. The dynamical question is whether nontrivial centers can be generated by coarse-graining rather than inserted through split-record laboratories. We address this question using state-preserving normal unital completely positive coarse-graining channels. If the Cesaro means of such a channel converge to a Choi-Effros infrared range and the range is asymptotically abelian in the GNS seminorm, then the represented infrared algebra is a commutative von Neumann algebra. Its projection lattice is therefore a complete Boolean algebra. We also give a finite-sector block-primitive criterion, motivated by locality and scrambling, which implies this asymptotic abelianness with exponential suppression of off-sector coherences and intra-sector fluctuations. The result is a conservative mathematical statement: classical facts are not identified with arbitrary projections of a Type-III local algebra, but with central projections selected by an asymptotically abelian completely positive infrared limit.

</details>


### [146] [StableShots: Online Shot Stopping for Quantum Circuit Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22170)
*Giuseppe Bisicchia,Alessandro Bocci,Ernesto Pimentel,Antonio Brogi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: StableShots是一种用于静态量子电路的在线停止规则，通过分批执行、监测累积经验分布的TV距离，在达到局部稳定性后自动停止。在180个QSimBench轨迹上评估，使用中位数7,650次测量即可达到TVD≤0.05，而固定预算方案要么失败率更高，要么消耗更多测量次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子电路执行采用固定的shot预算，这种静态选择存在缺陷：低预算导致分布欠采样，高预算则浪费测量资源。需要一种自适应停止规则来动态决定测量次数。

Method: StableShots采用黑盒在线停止规则：将电路执行分为小批次，持续监测累积累积经验分布之间的总变差距离（TVD），当累积分布反复表现出局部稳定性时停止执行。仅需验证集校准，无需训练数据。

Result: 在180个QSimBench轨迹（6种电路族、4-14量子比特6种规模、5种IBM模拟后端）上，100次重复后端-保留集划分验证，所选配置在所有保留集测试评估上达到TVD≤0.05，中位数仅需7,650 shots，优于固定shot基线。

Conclusion: StableShots有效解决了量子电路执行中shot预算的静态分配问题，通过自适应停止策略在保证输出分布精度的同时显著降低了测量成本。

Abstract: Quantum circuit execution estimates output distributions by repeated measurements, yet developers commonly choose a fixed shot budget before execution. This static choice is brittle: low budgets can under-sample the distribution, while high budgets waste measurements. In this paper, we present StableShots, a black-box online stopping rule for static quantum circuits. The method executes a fixed circuit in small batches, monitors the total-variation distance between cumulative empirical distributions, and stops after repeated evidence of local stability. We evaluate StableShots on 180 QSimBench traces spanning six circuit families, six sizes from 4 to 14 qubits, and five noisy IBM simulated backends. With validation-only calibration and 100 repeated backend-holdout splits, the selected configuration reaches TVD <= 0.05 on all held-out test evaluations with median 7,650 shots, whereas fixed-shot baselines either fail more often or spend substantially more shots.

</details>


### [147] [Fundamental Irreversibility from Discrete Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22187)
*M. W. AlMasri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Gol'fand在1964年提出的离散时间量子力学，将其严格重构为CPTP量子信道并导出连续极限下的Lindblad主方程。研究表明离散时间步长τ会引发内禀退相干和正熵产率，为时间箭头提供微观基础，并利用光晶格钟、物质波干涉和中微子振荡数据将τ约束在10^{-26}秒量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gol'fand的离散时间量子力学思想具有历史意义，但缺乏现代开放量子系统框架的处理。本文旨在严格重构该形式，揭示其与量子信道、Lindblad方程的联系，并为时间箭头方向和量子引力现象学提供可检验的预言。

Method: 将离散演化方程重构为CPTP量子信道，推导粗粒化连续极限得到Lindblad主方程；利用Spohn熵产形式分析热力学；通过保真度退化和纯度损失量化量子相干性损失；结合光晶格钟、物质波干涉和中微子振荡实验数据设定τ的上限。

Result: 离散时间动力学收敛到具有特征时间尺度τ的Lindblad主方程，在能量基和算子基W中诱导退相干。离散时间步长产生严格正的熵产率，为时间箭头提供独立于环境耦合的微观基础。离散时间晶体存在内禀寿命极限。实验数据将τ约束在τ≲10^{-26}s，显著优于此前极限。

Conclusion: Gol'fand离散时间动力学可统一在开放量子系统框架下，其内禀退相干机制为时间箭头提供了新解释，同时为量子引力现象学提供了实验可检验的严格约束。

Abstract: In 1964, Yu. A. Gol'fand proposed an extension of quantum mechanics to discrete time, predicting intrinsic non-unitarity and entropy increase. While historically significant, this formalism predates the modern theory of open quantum systems. In this work, we rigorously recast Gol'fand's discrete evolution equation as a Completely Positive Trace-Preserving (CPTP) quantum channel and derive its continuous-time coarse-grained limit. We demonstrate that the dynamics converge to a specific Lindblad master equation characterized by a fundamental time scale $τ$, which induces decoherence in both the energy basis and a fundamental operator basis $W$. We analyze the thermodynamic implications using Spohn's entropy production formalism, proving that the discrete time step induces a strictly positive entropy production rate driven by the decay of quantum coherences, thereby providing a microscopic foundation for the arrow of time independent of environmental coupling. Furthermore, we quantify the loss of quantum coherence via fidelity decay and purity loss, establishing exact constraints for fault-tolerant quantum computing. We further investigate the impact of this intrinsic decoherence on Discrete Time Crystals (DTCs), showing that Gol'fand dynamics impose a fundamental lifetime limit on time-translation symmetry breaking phases. Finally, we utilize precision data from optical lattice clocks, matter-wave interferometry, and neutrino oscillations to place stringent upper bounds on $τ$. Our results constrain the fundamental time discretization to $τ\lesssim 10^{-26}$ s, significantly tightening previous limits and offering a testable framework for quantum gravity phenomenology.

</details>


### [148] [Probing the Weak-Driving Quantum Speed Limit via Drift-Aware Shooting Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22212)
*Denis Janković,Saba Taherpour,Paul-Louis Etienney,Paul-Antoine Hervieux,Christoph Wolf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出MAGICARP方法，一种基于庞特里亚金最小原理的射击法，用于生成光滑、节能的量子控制脉冲。该方法从少量参数生成完整脉冲，在封闭系统中（如外磁场中两个交换耦合自旋）实现了最低能量消耗、最鲁棒的控制，并揭示了弱驱动量子速度极限及其能量发散规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子最优控制的目标是实现高保真度和低能量控制脉冲。传统方法独立优化每个时间点上的脉冲值，虽能获得高保真度，但产生宽带且耗能的波形。需要一种能够生成光滑、节能脉冲的控制方法。

Method: 方法分为三个阶段：(1) 利用漂移哈密顿量的缀饰态结构对目标进行结构化处理；(2) 在旋转波框架下进行初始射击优化（基于庞特里亚金最小原理）；(3) 在实验室框架下进行精确细化。MAGICARP从少量参数生成完整脉冲，保证光滑性和节能性。

Result: 与Krotov和GRAPE方法在相同门保真度下对比，MAGICARP始终实现最低能量和守恒的脉冲面积，频谱集中在门相关跃迁上，对交换耦合波动最鲁棒。Krotov方法能耗高一个数量级。研究还揭示了双交换耦合电子自旋的弱驱动量子速度极限：低于临界门时间T*时，低幅值的两比特量子傅里叶变换不再存在，控制能量在接近该极限时发散，服从二参数面积-极点律。

Conclusion: MAGICARP方法在能量效率和鲁棒性方面优于传统方法；发现了交换耦合自旋系统的弱驱动量子速度极限及能量发散规律，为量子控制脉冲设计提供了新的理论指导。

Abstract: A central goal of quantum optimal control is to achieve high-fidelity and low-energy control pulses. When quantum optimal control methods optimize every point of a pulse discretized over small time steps independently this can yield high fidelity control but also results in broadband and energy-hungry waveforms. We extend MAGICARP -- a shooting method inspired by Pontryagin's maximum principle on energy that generates an entire pulse from a small set of parameters, making it smooth and energy-efficient by construction -- from driftless systems to closed systems with the constant drift Hamiltonian of two exchange-coupled spins in an external magnetic field. The optimization proceeds in stages: the dressed states of the drift Hamiltonian structure the target, an initial shooting optimization is performed in the rotating-wave frame, and an exact laboratory-frame refinement follows. Benchmarked against Krotov and GRAPE at matched gate infidelity, MAGICARP consistently achieves the lowest energy and a conserved pulse area, concentrates its spectral weight on the gate-relevant transitions, and is the most robust to fluctuations in the exchange coupling; GRAPE independently converges to essentially the same pulse, while Krotov's method pays an order-of-magnitude energy premium. Moreover, a large statistical survey of unselected optimization runs resolves a weak-driving quantum speed limit for two exchange-coupled electron spins: low-amplitude realizations of the two-qubit quantum Fourier transform cease to exist below a critical gate time $T^*$ set by the drift's interaction rate, and the minimum control energy diverges on approach to this limit. The divergence obeys a simple two-parameter area-pole law, $E_2^{\mathrm{law}}(T)=A/T+B/(T-T^*)$, whose first term is the time-optimal area cost and whose second term is a pole at the speed limit.

</details>


### [149] [Collective Mpemba-Type Relaxation in Degenerate Bosonic Modes Coupled to a Common Thermal Reservoir](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22273)
*I. M. de Araújo,H. Sanchez,L. F. Alves da Silva,L. F. Alves da Silva,Norton G. de Almeida,M. H. Y. Moussa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了简并玻色子模式库在共同热浴中的集体Mpemba型弛豫现象，通过平均场约化推导了三种主方程（弱耦合马尔可夫、强耦合Caldeira-Leggett、弱耦合非马尔可夫），揭示了集体相干性、正交动力学和储库记忆对弛豫模式及Mpemba交叉时间的调控机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多模玻色系统中集体效应如何影响Mpemba效应（即系统冷却速度反常快于加热速度的现象），以及不同耦合机制和记忆效应对弛豫通道竞争的调控作用。

Method: 从全对称M模描述出发，采用平均场约化，分别推导弱耦合马尔可夫、强耦合Caldeira-Leggett和弱耦合非马尔可夫三种 regime 的有效主方程，并用能量和Kullback-Leibler散度作为观测指标进行数值模拟。

Result: 弱耦合马尔可夫极限下弛豫分解为热通道（衰减率γ）和集体相干通道（衰减率Mγ），得到显式Mpemba交叉时间；强耦合下正交动力学导致过阻尼区交叉延迟、欠阻尼区产生多次交叉；非马尔可夫 regime 中储库记忆通过时变衰减率和Lamb移位产生延迟和簇状交叉窗口。数值结果展示了Mpemba/反Mpemba行为、判据依赖性和多次瞬态重排序。

Conclusion: 集体相干性、正交动力学和储库记忆是调控多模玻色系统Mpemba弛豫行为的关键因素，不同耦合机制和判据选择可导致截然不同的瞬态重排序现象。

Abstract: We investigate collective Mpemba-type relaxation in a degenerate family of bosonic modes coupled to a common thermal reservoir. Starting from a fully symmetric M-mode description and employing a representative mean-field reduction, we derive effective master equations for weak-coupling Markovian, strong-coupling Caldeira-Leggett, and weak-coupling non-Markovian regimes. In the weak-coupling Markovian limit, relaxation separates into an incoherent thermal channel decaying at rate gamma and a collective coherent channel decaying at rate M gamma, yielding an explicit Mpemba crossing time determined by the initial-state preparation. In the strong-coupling Caldeira-Leggett regime, transient quadrature dynamics enriches the relaxation pattern, delaying crossings in the overdamped sector and generating multiple crossings in the underdamped sector. In the non-Markovian regime, reservoir memory reshapes the same channel-competition mechanism through time-dependent decay rates and a Lamb shift, producing delayed and clustered crossing windows. Numerical results based on the energy and the Kullback-Leibler divergence reveal Mpemba and anti-Mpemba behavior, criterion dependence, and multiple transient reorderings induced by collective coherence, quadrature dynamics, and reservoir memory.

</details>


### [150] [Probing Single-Particle Spatial Extent With Helical Neutron Wavefronts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22292)
*D. Sarenac,O. Lailey,C. W. Clark,D. G. Cory,H. Ekinci,D. V. Garrad,M. G. Huber,L. Matthews,N. Shentevski,P. R. Vadnere,D. A. Pushin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用螺旋中子波前区分横向相干长度与单粒子波包扩展。实验测量显示中子波包空间扩展（≥2微米）比横向相干长度（~180纳米）大一个数量级以上，证明两者是不同物理量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横向相干长度和单粒子波包扩展在传统实验中均表现为强度分布的空间展宽，难以区分，导致中子文献中长期存在混淆。

Method: 利用螺旋中子波前产生环形强度分布，其中峰值半径取决于横向波包扩展，而相干长度仅贡献于剖面展宽，从而实现两者的分离测量。

Result: 实验测得束流发散度~1.1 mrad（横向相干长度~180 nm），同时测得单中子波包空间扩展下限≥2微米，后者比前者大一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 横向相干长度和单粒子波包扩展是截然不同的物理量，螺旋波前方法为这一长期存在的混淆提供了直接的实验证据。

Abstract: Distinguishing transverse coherence length from single-particle wavepacket extent is fundamentally challenging, as both manifest through spatial broadening of observed intensity profiles in conventional experiments. Here we introduce a method based on helical neutron wavefronts that enables this separation. Helical neutron states produce annular intensity profiles whose peak radius depends on the transverse wavepacket extent, while coherence length only contributes to profile broadening. In our experimental geometry we measure a beam divergence of ~1.1 mrad, corresponding to a transverse coherence length of ~180 nm. In contrast, the same measurement places a lower bound of >= 2 um on the spatial extent of the individual neutron wavepackets, more than an order of magnitude larger than the coherence length. These results provide direct experimental evidence that transverse coherence length and single-particle wavepacket extent are distinct physical quantities, resolving a longstanding source of confusion in the neutron literature.

</details>


### [151] [The Physics Behind Symmetrization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22298)
*R. E. Kastner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文挑战了量子力学中“同种粒子态必须对称化”的传统观点，指出粒子散射实验存在两个物理上不同的通道，这一现象直接反驳了“标记冗余”假设。作者认为，对称化并非普遍必要，且现有文献处理存在物理内容缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑量子力学中同种粒子态必须对称化的传统教条，指出“标记冗余”假设（即标签排列不反映物理区别）在散射实验中面临直接反例。

Method: 以电子散射为例，分析两个物理上不同的散射通道，批判当前文献中忽略关键物理内容的处理方式，并结合粒子制备与实验结果进行论证。

Result: 同种粒子对称化的普遍强制要求缺乏实际计算支持，也与基于制备和结果的粒子态描述不一致。

Conclusion: 对称化不应被普遍强加于同种粒子态，现有处理方式需要重新审视。

Abstract: It is often asserted that quantum states for same-type particles must be symmetrized due to ``label redundancy,'' i.e. the assumption that the permutations of labels in direct-product states do not reflect any real physical distinction and thus their permutations constitute an ``exchange degeneracy''. This assumption is directly challenged by the case of scattering of same-type particles such as electrons, which involves two physically distinct scattering channels effectively corresponding to permutation of the labels. I discuss this counterexample with critical attention to an extant portrayal in the literature that omits pertinent physical content. I further note ways in which the assumption that symmetrization must be universally imposed is not supported by actual calculations of particle interactions, nor by seemingly viable particle states based on preparations and outcomes.

</details>


### [152] [Making Quantum Networks Work: Routing, Calibration, and Programmable Quantum Repeaters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22316)
*Vinay Kumar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本论文研究了量子互联网在现实约束下的架构、路由和运行问题，提出了面向异构节点的灰盒路由方法、最优校准调度策略以及基于NV中心的可编程量子中继节点指令集架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典网络的抽象和协议不适用于量子网络，因其受限于不可克隆定理、概率性纠缠生成、退相干和硬件漂移等量子特有约束，需要新的架构、协议和优化方法。

Method: 在网络层，针对异构中继效率提出了基于部分节点质量信息的选路策略，进一步引入了仅依赖拓扑和端到端估计的灰盒路由方法。在链路层，提出了区分激活和校准阶段的校准感知模型，推导了线性中继链的最优校准调度，并扩展到通用拓扑的贪婪编排启发式算法。最后，基于NV中心设计了可编程量子中继节点的指令集架构。

Result: 灰盒路由在无需详细链路信息的情况下实现了鲁棒性和公平性；最优校准调度平衡了操作时间和校准开销；指令集架构实现了物理操作与高层协议的有效衔接。

Conclusion: 量子网络需要在各层重新设计协议和算法以应对量子特有的物理约束，本文提出的方法在端到端保真度、吞吐量、可扩展性和公平性方面均取得了有效提升。

Abstract: The quantum internet enables distribution of quantum states across distant nodes, supporting secure communication, distributed computing, and quantum sensing. Unlike classical networks, it is constrained by the no cloning theorem, probabilistic entanglement generation, decoherence, and hardware drift, making classical abstractions inadequate. Scalable quantum networking therefore requires new architectures, protocols, and optimisation methods that explicitly account for these limitations.
  This thesis studies the architecture, routing, and operation of quantum networks under realistic constraints, focusing on bipartite entanglement distribution over quantum repeater networks. Key metrics include end to end fidelity, throughput, scalability, and fairness. At the network layer, routing strategies are developed beyond assumptions of homogeneous nodes and full network knowledge. Routing under heterogeneous repeater efficiencies shows how partial knowledge of node quality improves fidelity and reduces path blocking. A grey box routing approach is then introduced, where path selection relies only on topology and end to end estimates, achieving robustness and fairness without detailed link information.
  At the link layer, calibration and hardware drift are addressed through a calibration aware model separating activation and calibration phases. For linear repeater chains, an optimal calibration schedule is derived to balance operation time and calibration overhead. This is extended to general topologies with shared links, where a greedy orchestration heuristic is proposed.
  Finally, the thesis connects network protocols with hardware via an instruction set architecture for programmable quantum repeater nodes based on NV centers, enabling coherent programmability and linking physical operations to higher layer protocols.

</details>


### [153] [No Reference-Free Generalization in Quantum Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22331)
*Jeongho Bang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子机器学习中，当训练数据缺乏外部量子参考系时，所有与训练状态张成空间正交的纯态必须得到相同预测，这一限制源于缺失参考信息而非状态区分或计算能力。学习多量子比特系统上的通用无结构概念需要指数级数量的独立定向训练方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习通常以指数级大的希尔伯特空间作为动机，但这留下了一个基本的泛化问题：当训练数据不提供任何优选基、测量框架或其他定向结构时，学习器如何能为未见的量子方向赋予不同含义？

Method: 通过在没有外部量子参考系的情况下制定监督学习，要求预测不能依赖于希尔伯特空间坐标的任意选择，迫使学习到的分类器保留训练数据未破坏的所有酉对称性。

Result: 当训练状态未能张成完整希尔伯特空间时，所有与其张成空间正交的纯态必须获得相同预测，即使这些态相互正交且在提供适当测量时可完美区分。该限制并非由状态区分、优化或计算能力引起，而是源于缺失参考信息。在弱对称性破缺下建立了鲁棒版本，并证明学习多量子比特系统上的通用无结构概念需要指数级数量的独立定向训练方向。

Conclusion: 特征映射、测量基、哈密顿量、局域性、对称性先验、架构和足够多样化的训练状态是泛化的操作性资源。核心启示是希尔伯特空间维度本身不是一个可学习的特征空间：成功的QML必须指定赋予未见量子方向语义意义的物理结构。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning is often motivated by the exponentially large state space of quantum systems, but this promise leaves a basic generalization problem unresolved: how can a learner assign different meanings to unseen quantum directions when the training data provide no preferred basis, measurement frame, or other orienting structure? We address this identifiability problem by formulating supervised learning without an external quantum reference frame, so that predictions cannot depend on an arbitrary choice of Hilbert-space coordinates. This requirement forces the learned classifier to preserve every unitary symmetry left unbroken by the training data. We prove that whenever the training states fail to span the full Hilbert space, all pure states orthogonal to their span must receive the same prediction -- even when those states are mutually orthogonal and perfectly distinguishable once an appropriate measurement is supplied. The limitation is therefore not caused by state discrimination, optimization, or computational power, but by missing reference information. We further establish a robust version under weak symmetry breaking and show that learning generic unstructured concepts on multiqubit systems requires exponentially many independently oriented training directions. Numerical illustrations visualize the resulting prediction collapse and its controlled relaxation. Our results identify feature maps, measurement bases, Hamiltonians, locality, symmetry priors, architectures, and sufficiently diverse training states as operational resources for generalization. The central implication is that Hilbert-space dimension alone is not a learnable feature space: successful QML must specify the physical structure that gives unseen quantum directions semantic meaning.

</details>


### [154] [Lattice-quantile estimation of π and convex-region integrals from coined two-dimensional quantum walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22334)
*Jen-Yu Chang,En-Jui Kuo,Chih-Yu Chen,Tsung-Wei Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于二维离散时间量子游走的数值积分新框架，通过将量子游走的弹道扩散与Hardy-Huxley格点渐近结合，构造出主导误差由确定性的数论残差而非统计波动控制的估计器，从而绕过了Cramér-Rao下界对蒙特卡洛积分M^{-1/2}的速率限制，并支持单批次测量覆盖整族积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒙特卡洛积分受限于Cramér-Rao下界给出的M^{-1/2}收敛速率，而离散时间量子游走的弹道扩散（方差T^2）尚未被利用于数值积分。本文旨在利用这种更快扩散来突破经典采样-均值估计器的统计极限。

Method: 将2D离散时间量子游走的弹道缩放与Hardy-Huxley高斯圆格点计数渐近结合，用格点计数与径向位置分位数平方之比N(R̂)/R̂²替代传统样本均值，使主导误差变为由游走深度T控制的确定性数论残差。通过Kraetzel格点渐近处理凸光滑区域，通过Cavalieri原理处理凸或环形上水平集的光滑积分，并给出无参数偏置底噪恒等式。

Result: 在固定精度下与经典随机游走进行测量计数对比，所有深度下偏置底噪恒等式的验证误差在1.5倍以内。实验隔离了量子贡献，框架无需量子幅度估计中的预言机（无需受控酉编码被积函数），与QAE和Szegedy游走方法结构不同。

Conclusion: 该框架通过数论方法替代统计平均，从根本上改变了误差来源，有望超越经典蒙特卡洛的收敛极限，且具有无需量子预言机、单批次覆盖多积分的实用优势。

Abstract: Monte Carlo integration is fundamentally limited by the M^(-1/2) rate that the Cramer-Rao bound imposes on any sample-mean estimator of an expectation value, regardless of how the samples are drawn. Coined discrete-time quantum walks (DTQWs) are known to spread ballistically - their position variance scales as T^2 against the diffusive T of classical random walks - yet this faster spreading has not been exploited for numerical integration. We show that coupling the ballistic scaling of a 2D DTQW to the Hardy-Huxley asymptotic for Gauss circle lattice counts produces estimators whose dominant error is a deterministic number-theoretic residual controlled by walk depth T, not a statistical fluctuation controlled by sample count M. The construction replaces the empirical mean of a sample-mean estimator with the ratio N(R-hat)/R-hat^2 of a lattice count to the square of a radial position quantile, a structural change that sidesteps the Cramer-Rao barrier. A single batch of measurements then propagates through classically precomputed multipliers to cover an entire family of integrals simultaneously. We develop the framework for convex smooth domains via Kraetzel's lattice asymptotic and for smooth integrals with convex or annular super-level sets via Cavalieri's principle, and provide a parameter-free identity for the bias floor (validated to within 1.5x across all tested depths). Every experiment is benchmarked against the classical random walk with the identical estimator to isolate the quantum contribution; the framework is oracle-free in the QAE sense (no controlled unitary encoding the integrand is required) and structurally distinct from quantum amplitude estimation and Szegedy-walk approaches. These ratios compare measurement counts at fixed precision and do not include quantum circuit execution cost.

</details>


### [155] [Probabilistic Storage and Retrieval of Quantum Superchannels for "Retrospective'' Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22343)
*Wataru Yokojima,Jisho Miyazaki,Mio Murao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子信道的概率存储与检索（pSAR）问题的高阶版本——确定因果酉超信道的概率存储与检索，提出了两种协议：部分遥传协议（小查询次数最优）和阶梯回针协议（查询次数增加时成功概率渐近趋于1），并推导了酉超信道的通用反转协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服量子计算无编程定理的限制，实现将未知量子计算存储在量子态中并在后续任意时间点精确检索，特别是针对具有因果结构的酉超信道（序列化酉信道间可插入任意干预信道）的存储与检索问题。

Method: 提出了两种协议：1) 部分遥传协议（partial teleportation），在小查询次数下最优；2) 阶梯回针协议（staircase backstitch），随查询次数增加成功概率渐近趋于1。同时推导了酉超信道的通用反转协议。

Result: 部分遥传协议在小存储查询次数下达到最优成功概率；阶梯回针协议在查询次数增加时成功概率渐近趋于1；推导了酉超信道的通用反转协议。

Conclusion: 实现了对确定性因果酉超信道的高效概率存储与检索，为高阶量子信息处理协议的设计提供了新途径。

Abstract: Storing an unknown quantum computation in a quantum state and retrieving it at a desired later time is a challenging task, hindered by the no-programming theorem of quantum computations. In the previous studies on the task of probabilistic storage-and-retrieval (pSAR) of quantum channels, the maximum probability of exactly retrieving a single unknown unitary channel from a quantum state in which the unknown unitary has been encoded via multiple calls to the unknown unitary channel is derived. In this work, we consider a higher-order version of pSAR, the probabilistic storage-and-retrieval of definite-causal unitary superchannels, which are physically modeled by sequences of unitary channels with open slots where arbitrary channels can be inserted between the unitary channels for intervention. This task requires activating the ``retrospective'' intervention functionality on the superchannel, beyond its normal intervention functionality. We propose two protocols: partial teleportation, which is optimal for a small number of storage queries, and staircase backstitch, which achieves unit success probability asymptotically as the number of queries increases. We also derive a universal inversion protocol for unitary superchannels.

</details>


### [156] [Vibe Calibration: Autonomous Bring-up of a 112-Qubit Superconducting Quantum Processor by a Skill-Orchestrating Language Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22376)
*Huikai Xu,Jiaxiu Han,Shigang Ou,Cheng Ye,Zisong Shen,Jing Gao,Yijia Wang,Tianrui Che,Yu Song,Weiyang Liu,Lei Wang,Lin-Feng Zhang,Pan Zhang,Hai-Feng Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Vibe Calibration，一种由大语言模型智能体驱动的自主校准系统，通过将专家隐性知识蒸馏为可复用的Skill（决策树），在112比特超导量子处理器上实现4-5倍速度提升，并首次展示了百比特级量子处理器的全自主校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子计算在向容错量子计算扩展过程中，传统脚本校准方法对异常信号脆弱，且专家判断受限于认知带宽和串行操作时间，无法跟上系统规模的增长。

Method: 通过三阶段人机协同蒸馏流程将专家隐性知识转化为可复用Skill（参数化测量命令、定量接受标准、审计记录的决策树），并基于验证轨迹微调大语言模型，构建自主校准系统Vibe Calibration。

Result: 在112比特频率可调transmon处理器上，系统4.7小时内自主完成108/112个量子比特校准，速度提升4-5倍；与专家手动校准的16比特子集交叉验证显示14/16一致；校准工作流可跨设备迁移。

Conclusion: 首次实现了百比特级超导处理器的全自主、可复用、可审计的校准，消除了可扩展量子硬件运行的关键障碍。

Abstract: Superconducting quantum computing is one of the most mature solid-state platforms for quantum computation, with processors exceeding one hundred qubits. Yet further scaling toward fault-tolerant quantum computing is increasingly constrained by calibration complexity. Conventional scripts are brittle to anomalous signals, and expert judgment is bounded by cognitive bandwidth and serial operation time, failing to keep pace with system scale. Here we report Vibe Calibration, an autonomous calibration system orchestrated by large language model agents, which distills expert tacit knowledge into reusable Skills. Each Skill is organized as a decision tree that packages parameterized measurement commands, quantitative acceptance criteria, and audit records, enabling autonomous execution and self-healing. We capture this knowledge through a three-phase human-in-the-loop distillation process and fine-tune a large language model on validated trajectories. On a 112-qubit processor with frequency-tunable transmons, the system autonomously completes calibration of 108 out of 112 qubits in 4.7 hours, achieving a 4--5$\times$ speedup over manual calibration of the full 112 qubits. A cross-validated comparison with expert manual calibration on a 16-qubit subset shows agreement on 14 out of 16 qubits. More importantly, the model demonstrates transferable calibration workflows across devices. While low-level control scripts require minor interface adaptation for different hardware platforms, the core decision logic and task orchestration generalize to new processors, demonstrating a reusable laboratory interface rather than a memorized script.This work demonstrates, for the first time, fully autonomous calibration of a hundred-qubit superconducting processor through reusable and auditable Skills, removing a critical barrier to scalable quantum hardware operation.

</details>


### [157] [Quantum Otto engine with decoupled idle levels in a non-Hermitian XY model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22405)
*Maimaitiyiming Tusun,Fang Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在双量子比特非厄米XY模型（含交错虚部磁场）中实现了量子奥托循环。该系统能谱自然分为依赖于外场的工作能级和完全独立于外场的空闲能级，首次在物理自旋模型中具体实现了de Oliveira和Jonathan提出的空闲能级量子热机架构。通过调节非厄米参数η₀，系统从耗散区（净功为负、从热库吸热）连续过渡到真正的热机模式，同时提升输出功和效率。在PT未破缺相内，η₀增大会显著提高效率，接近卡诺极限。该效应源于空闲能级间隙压缩，改变了热、冷平衡态的能级占据权重，从而调节吸收热量。净功表达式分子与η₀无关，但分母通过双曲余弦函数间接依赖η₀——这是空闲能级控制机制的数学根源。本文还分析了参数变化的鲁棒性、与厄米极限的对比，并提出了离子阱量子模拟器的实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现空闲能级量子热机架构的微观物理模型，探索非厄米性作为控制量子热机工作模式和性能的新工具。

Method: 在双量子比特非厄米XY模型（交错虚部磁场）中构造量子奥托循环，利用非厄米参数η₀调控能谱结构，分析热力学性能指标。

Result: 1) 实现了从耗散区到热机模式的连续过渡；2) η₀增大同时提升输出功和效率，效率可达卡诺极限的显著比例；3) 揭示了空闲能级间隙压缩的调控机制；4) 净功分子与η₀无关，分母通过双曲余弦函数间接依赖η₀。

Conclusion: 非厄米性是引导量子热机工作模式和性能的不可或缺的工具，为量子热力学提供了新的控制维度。

Abstract: We study a quantum Otto cycle in a two-qubit non-Hermitian XY model with a staggered imaginary magnetic field. The energy spectrum of this system naturally decouples into a pair of working levels that depend on the external field and a pair of idle levels that are completely independent of it, thereby providing the first concrete microscopic realization of the idle-level quantum heat engine architecture proposed by de~Oliveira and Jonathan [Phys. Rev. E 104, 044133 (2021)] in a physical spin model. Tuning the non-Hermitian parameter eta_0 drives a continuous transition from a dissipative regime with negative net work and net heat absorption from the hot reservoir into a genuine heat engine mode, while simultaneously enhancing both output work and efficiency. As eta_0 increases within the stable PT-unbroken phase, the engine efficiency rises significantly, reaching a substantial fraction of the Carnot limit. This effect originates from the compression of the idle-level gap, which redistributes the level occupation weights in the hot and cold equilibrium states and thereby modulates the absorbed heat. The numerator of the net work expression is independent of eta_0, but the denominator depends on eta_0 indirectly through hyperbolic cosine functions -- this is the mathematical root of the idle-level control mechanism. We provide a detailed analysis of the robustness of these findings against parameter variations, a critical comparison of the non-Hermitian control with the Hermitian limit, and a concrete experimental proposal for trapped-ion quantum simulators. Our results demonstrate that non-Hermiticity serves as an indispensable tool for steering both the operation mode and the performance of a quantum engine.

</details>


### [158] [A Three-Layer Architecture for Fault-Tolerant Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22418)
*Zhirao Wang,Zhou You,Yiming Huang,Tianyi Li,Ying Li,Xiao Yuan,Yuan Yao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个硬件无关的三层高层架构框架，用于通用容错量子计算。该框架包含应用层、容错层和硬件层，其中容错层作为逻辑程序与硬件执行之间的桥梁，系统描述了其内部五个组件及接口、数据流和执行路径。通过端到端示例展示了全栈操作流水线，为模块化、异构和跨层容错量子系统提供了统一的基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错性是构建大规模通用量子计算机不可或缺的前提条件，但目前缺乏一个硬件无关的通用抽象标准来指导容错量子计算的架构设计。受经典计算机体系结构哲学启发，作者旨在提出一个不依赖于特定物理量子比特平台和量子纠错码的高层架构框架。

Method: 提出了一个三层高层架构框架：应用层（逻辑程序）、容错层（架构桥梁）和硬件层（物理执行）。容错层包含五个内部组件，并定义了组件间的接口、数据交换方式，以及执行路径、校正路径和自适应路径三大运行路径，分别支持逻辑综合、容错资源管理、解码和运行时容错控制。

Result: 给出了一个端到端示例，展示了在该框架下容错量子算法的全栈操作流水线。该架构为模块化、异构和跨层容错量子系统提供了统一的基础组织模型。

Conclusion: 随着对模块化、异构和跨层容错量子系统关注度的提升，该架构提供了一个统一的通用模型来组织此类设计，可作为通用抽象标准而非特定平台实现方案。

Abstract: Fault tolerance is an indispensable prerequisite for constructing large-scale universal quantum computers. Drawing philosophies from classical computer architecture, this paper presents a hardware-agnostic three-layer high-level architectural framework for generic fault-tolerant quantum computation. Guided by the real execution workflows of fault-tolerant quantum algorithms, the proposed model is decoupled from specific physical qubit hardware platforms and quantum error correction codes, serving as a universal abstract standard rather than a platform-specific implementation scheme. Special attention is devoted to the intermediate Fault-Tolerance Layer, which serves as the architectural bridge between application-level logical programs and hardware-level execution. We systematically characterize its five internal components, the interfaces and data exchanged among them, and the execution, correction, and adaptation paths that together enable logical synthesis, fault-tolerant resources management, decoding, and runtime fault-tolerant control. An end-to-end example is further provided to illustrate the full-stack operating pipeline of fault-tolerant quantum algorithms under this framework. Given the increasing emphasis on modular, heterogeneous, and cross-layer fault-tolerant quantum systems, our architecture provides a unified foundational model for organizing such designs.

</details>


### [159] [Geometric Response in Open Quantum Systems: Coherence, Curvature, and Susceptibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22517)
*Eric R. Bittner,Carlos Silva-Acuña*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为开放量子系统建立了一个响应几何框架，将稳态响应张量分解为对称度规部分和反对称曲率部分，后者对应非互易响应和几何功，建立了非平衡稳态下推广的热力学涨落-响应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将平衡态热力学的几何结构（如热力学度规）推广到非平衡稳态，揭示开放量子系统中互易与非互易响应共存的几何结构。

Method: 将稳态响应张量分解为对称（度规型）和反对称（曲率型）两部分；通过驱动耗散量子比特在纯退相干下的例子验证；与Bures度规比较。

Result: 对称部分定义局域磁化率的度规；反对称部分定义非互易响应和几何功的曲率；平衡时反对称部分消失恢复互易性；非平衡时曲率来源于哈密顿本征基与退相干指针基的错位。

Conclusion: 开放量子系统中响应几何与信息几何刻画稳态流形的不同性质；几何功可作为非互易响应的可测量标志。

Abstract: We develop a response-geometric framework for open quantum systems by decomposing the stationary-state response tensor into symmetric and antisymmetric sectors. The symmetric sector defines a metric-like response tensor governing local susceptibility, while the antisymmetric sector defines a curvature two-form associated with nonreciprocal response and geometric work. We show that the work curvature is precisely the antisymmetric component of the response tensor, establishing a fluctuation--response relation that extends the geometric structure of equilibrium thermodynamics to nonequilibrium steady states, and reveals a response geometry with both metric and symplectic sectors. In equilibrium, the antisymmetric sector vanishes by reciprocity, recovering the familiar metric geometry of thermodynamic response. Open quantum systems admit a broader structure in which reciprocal and nonreciprocal responses coexist on the same control manifold. We illustrate this with a driven dissipative qubit under pure dephasing, where finite curvature emerges from the misalignment between the Hamiltonian eigenbasis and the pointer basis selected by the environment, without requiring strong driving or engineered reservoirs. Comparison with the Bures metric shows that response geometry and information geometry characterize distinct properties of the stationary-state manifold: the former governs susceptibility and geometric work, the latter quantifies state distinguishability. Geometric work thus emerges as a measurable signature of nonreciprocal response in open quantum systems.

</details>


### [160] [Liouvillian Geometry of Multidimensional Spectra: Pathway Transport and Observational Holonomy in Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22530)
*Eric R. Bittner,Carlos Silva-Acuña,Hao Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何框架，将开放量子系统中非线性光谱的Liouville路径视为在自由演化区间内连续传输振幅的动态对象，而非固定不变的实体。环境与观测基的不兼容性导致路径间传输，产生可观测的谱学特征（如光谱畸变、峰位偏移及禁阻路径贡献），这些特征被解释为Liouville空间流形的几何签名（曲率与holonomy），而非唯象的展宽修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将Liouville路径视为光学相互作用之间独立演化的固定对象，但在开放量子系统中，环境相互作用会在自由演化区间内持续重新分配路径间的振幅，这一图景是不完整的。作者旨在建立一个几何框架，以描述这种路径传输（transport）及其可观测的光谱学特征。

Method: 利用Liouville联络（Liouvillian connection）及其曲率（curvature）和观测和乐（observational holonomy）来刻画路径传输。采用Duhamel展开处理Liouville传播子，推导出重构程序以识别导致路径权重重新分布的传输算子，适用于任意基失准程度。

Result: 建立了基失准（环境指针基与观测基不兼容）导致路径间传输的机制；导出了可重构传输算子的重建程序；表明光谱畸变、峰位偏移及禁阻路径贡献是弯曲Liouville空间流形的几何签名。

Conclusion: 非线性光谱的谱学特征不仅取决于哪些路径存在，更取决于振幅如何在路径间传输。路径几何（pathway geometry）是组织非线性光谱的互补性层级，将传统上归因于唯象展宽的现象重新解释为几何效应。

Abstract: Liouville pathways provide the conceptual foundation for interpreting multidimensional spectroscopies, yet are typically treated as fixed objects that evolve independently between optical interactions. In open quantum systems, this picture is incomplete. Environmental interactions continuously redistribute amplitude among pathways during every free-evolution interval, generating transport that leaves measurable signatures in the nonlinear spectroscopic response. We develop a geometric framework in which pathway transport is governed by a Liouvillian connection, its associated curvature, and the resulting observational holonomy. The framework applies to open quantum systems in which the environment selects a pointer basis distinct from the observational basis used to construct the spectroscopic response. This basis incompatibility induces transport among Liouville pathways, generating characteristic spectral distortions and a nontrivial Liouvillian curvature. Using a Duhamel expansion of the Liouvillian propagator, we derive a reconstruction procedure that identifies the transport operators responsible for the observed redistribution of pathway weight, accurate throughout the full range of basis misalignment. This perspective reframes spectral features as determined not only by which pathways exist but by how amplitude is transported among them. Spectral distortions, peak shifts, and otherwise-forbidden pathway contributions become geometric signatures of a curved Liouville-space manifold rather than phenomenological broadening corrections, identifying pathway geometry as a complementary layer of organization in nonlinear spectroscopy.

</details>


### [161] [The Quantum Hamming Bound in Arbitrary Local Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22538)
*Yu-Xuan Zhang,Jing-Ling Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意局部维度下有限长量子汉明界成立：对于q≥3的非二进制情形，通过两中心汉明球交集不等式和Lloyd响应归一化证明；q=4的二进制端点由独立几何处理；量子三能级系统(q=3)通过二次滤波Lloyd平方和Stein切线正性论证完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子汉明界是精确量子纠错码的有限长球堆积计数，但简并性可能导致不同物理错误在码子空间上重合，使得球堆积计数过高。核心问题是这种过度计数是否可能使汉明不等式失效，此前线性规划、渐近和结构结果均未解决任意精确子空间码的点态有限长问题。

Method: 将问题简化为两中心汉明球交集不等式，用Lloyd响应归一化；对q≥4通过Lloyd平方线性规划在字母表Q≥16和临界长度n=4t+1下的均匀半间隙证明；对q=3采用二次滤波Lloyd平方、精确系数证书约简和Stein切线正性论证。

Result: 对于所有q≥3的非二进制精确子空间码，量子汉明界成立；q=4的二进制端点由独立定理处理。因此任意局部维度下有限长量子汉明界得证。

Conclusion: 简并性可以合并错误扇区，但不足以击败汉明计数。本文完成了非二进制部分，结合二进制端点定理，得到了任意局部维度的完整结果。

Abstract: The quantum Hamming bound is the finite-length sphere-packing count for exact quantum error correction: the code dimension times the number of correctable local error patterns must fit inside the ambient Hilbert space. For nondegenerate codes this follows from disjoint error spheres. Degeneracy is the only obstruction, because distinct physical errors can coincide on the code subspace and turn sphere packing into an overcount. The central finite-length question has been whether this overcount can ever invalidate the Hamming inequality. Earlier linear-programming, asymptotic, and structural results left a pointwise finite-length problem for arbitrary exact subspace codes. Writing $Q=q^2$ for the Hamming-scheme alphabet, the nonbinary range begins at $Q=9$; here we prove the bound for every $q\ge3$, while the binary endpoint is governed by a distinct $Q=4$ charging geometry. For every nontrivial exact subspace code in this range, any possible violation reduces to a two-center Hamming-ball intersection inequality normalized by the Lloyd response. For $q\ge4$, the Lloyd-square linear program has a uniform half-gap after reduction to alphabet $Q\ge16$ and critical length $n=4t+1$. Qutrits form the boundary: the half-gap disappears, but the bridge is handled by a quadratic-filtered Lloyd square, an exact coefficient-certificate reduction, and a Stein-tangent positivity argument. Thus degeneracy may merge error sectors, but not enough to beat the Hamming count. This proves the nonbinary part of the finite-length quantum Hamming bound; together with the independent binary endpoint theorem, it gives the result in arbitrary local dimension.

</details>


### [162] [Hierarchical Logical Processor on the Rotated Surface Code with Shuttle Buses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22594)
*Zi-Han Chen,Ming-Cheng Chen,Chao-Yang Lu,Jian-Wei Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出层次化逻辑处理器(HLP)，通过将高码率量子CSS码与旋转表面码(RSC)级联，在保持超越RSC编码效率的同时，将长程耦合需求从每轮综合征提取降低到每Θ(d₀)轮。HLP引入"穿梭总线"(shuttle bus)概念，利用横向混合单元CNOT门实现高效并行操作。在物理错误率10⁻³下，基于[[256,194,4]]码的HLP相比标准RSC实现3-4倍量子比特效率提升，相比同级别yoked surface code降低每个逻辑量子比特100-200物理量子比特的空间开销，并缩短纠错周期20-30倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子平台具备超越平面拓扑的连接能力，为容错量子计算提供新机遇。但qLDPC码直接实现需要每轮综合征提取中引入非局域耦合，增加了物理错误和实现复杂度。因此，需要一种能减少非局域耦合频率、同时保持高编码效率的量子纠错方案。

Method: 提出层次化逻辑处理器(HLP)，将高码率量子CSS码与旋转表面码(RSC)级联。引入elongated RSC patches称为穿梭总线(shuttle bus)，利用横向混合单元CNOT门实现单个穿梭总线同时耦合多个标准RSC patch，支持高效的level-1综合征提取和高并行度的逻辑Pauli测量。HLP仅需每Θ(d₀)轮level-0纠错执行一次长程连接。

Result: 电路级仿真表明：物理错误率10⁻³下，基于[[256,194,4]]码的HLP比标准RSC实现3-4倍量子比特效率提升；相比同级别yoked surface code，每个逻辑量子比特的空间开销减少100-200物理量子比特，逻辑纠错周期时间缩短20-30倍。

Conclusion: HLP通过层次化级联设计，在不牺牲编码效率的前提下显著降低了长程耦合频率，实现了空间开销和纠错时间的双重优化，为超越平面拓扑的量子平台提供了一条实用的容错量子计算路径。

Abstract: Quantum platforms with beyond-planar connectivity provide new opportunities for fault-tolerant quantum computation (FTQC). While quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes offer high encoding efficiency, their direct implementation requires non-local couplings in every round of syndrome extraction, incurring additional physical error and implementation complexity. To reduce the frequency of such couplings, we propose the Hierarchical Logical Processor (HLP), which concatenates a high-rate quantum CSS code with the rotated surface code (RSC). HLPs can achieve beyond-RSC encoding efficiency while requiring long-range connectivity only once every $Θ(d_0)$ rounds of level-0 error correction, where $d_0$ denotes the base-code distance, substantially reducing the frequency of non-local couplings relative to direct implementations of qLDPC codes. HLPs introduce elongated RSC patches called shuttle buses. Using transversal hybrid-unit CNOT gates, a single shuttle bus can simultaneously couple to multiple standard RSC patches. This capability enables efficient level-1 syndrome extraction with suppressed level-1 error correlations and supports highly parallel logical Pauli measurements. We perform circuit-level simulations of several concrete HLP constructions and benchmark both logical memory and logical Pauli measurement performance. At a physical error rate of $10^{-3}$, an HLP based on the [[256,194,4]] code achieves 3-4 times higher qubit efficiency than the standard RSC. Compared with the yoked surface code on the same level-1 code, this HLP reduces the space overhead per logical qubit by 100-200 physical qubits and shortens the logical error-correction cycle time by a factor of 20-30.

</details>


### [163] [Generalization of catability for parity-less cat states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22641)
*Michal Matulík,Jan Provazník,Petr Marek,Šimon Bräuer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于零化子的可验证性框架，从奇偶性定义的相干猫态扩展到无奇偶性的Kerr猫态，使用位移-奇偶算符替代原奇偶分量，实现了无需完整态层析的有限光子数测量认证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将可验证性框架从奇偶性定义的相干猫态扩展到无奇偶性的Kerr猫态，解决Kerr猫态无法通过单一奇偶性区分的问题。

Method: 提出位移-奇偶算符替代原始零化子中的奇偶分量，捕获Kerr猫态的特征干涉结构；同时识别出基于复共轭的精确反线性零化子，提供理想的代数描述。

Result: 该方法能忠实认证Kerr猫态特征，产生理想Kerr猫态的精确近似，且对光学损耗的鲁棒性优于基于保真度的认证方法。

Conclusion: 拓宽了可验证性框架的适用范围，为Kerr猫态的表征提供了一种实验上实用的方法。

Abstract: We extend the nullifier-based certification framework of catability from parity-defined coherent cat states to parity-less Kerr cat states. Since Kerr cats cannot be distinguished by parity alone, we replace the parity component of the original nullifier with a displaced-parity operator that captures their characteristic interference structure. The resulting generalized catability remains directly observable and can be evaluated from a finite number of photon-number measurements without full state tomography. Numerical benchmarks show that the method faithfully certifies Kerr-cat features, produces accurate approximations of ideal Kerr cat states, and is more resilient to optical loss than fidelity-based certification. We also identify an exact anti-linear nullifier based on complex conjugation, providing an ideal algebraic description of Kerr cat states. Our results broaden the scope of catability and provide an experimentally practical approach to the characterization of Kerr cat states.

</details>


### [164] [A Measurement-Like Test of Continuous Spontaneous Localization with a Reversible Nanoparticle Pointer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22707)
*Peter Renkel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于悬浮纳米颗粒的干涉式连续自发局域化（CSL）测试方案。纳米颗粒作为可逆介观指针，通过微弱力将其与微观系统的路径信息耦合，经一个弱阱周期后指针位置和动量重新重合，量子力学预测微观相干性恢复，而CSL模型预测不可逆的可见度损失。对10⁻¹⁸ kg纳米颗粒、10⁻²¹ N差分力、0.4 K温度下，测得最小CSL坍缩率λ_min≈1.4×10⁻¹⁰ s⁻¹（r_c=100 nm, 10⁵次测量），更低频点达λ_min≈8.7×10⁻¹² s⁻¹，灵敏度较直接物质波CSL界限提升约7×10³和10⁵倍，但低于最强非干涉界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CSL直接干涉测试灵敏度有限，需要更强灵敏度的方法来检验自发坍缩模型。本文旨在设计一种测量式干涉测试，通过可逆介观指针放大小尺度量子相干性的退相干信号。

Method: 采用悬浮纳米颗粒作为可逆介观指针，利用微观两分支系统施加相反微弱力（10⁻²¹ N），使指针产生分支条件依赖的位移。经过一个弱阱周期后，指针位置和动量重新重合，量子力学预测微观相干性完全恢复，而CSL模型预测因质量分布分离导致的不可逆可见度损失。

Result: 对10⁻¹⁸ kg纳米颗粒，在T=0.4 K、差分力10⁻²¹ N、热呼吸和常规损失预算Λ_loss≤0.1条件下，测得最小CSL坍缩率λ_min≈1.4×10⁻¹⁰ s⁻¹（r_c=100 nm, 10⁵次测量），更低频点达λ_min≈8.7×10⁻¹² s⁻¹。灵敏度较直接物质波CSL界限提升约7×10³和10⁵倍。

Conclusion: 该方案旨在提供一种较现有直接干涉测试强得多的CSL可见度损失检验，实现可逆测量式坍缩灵敏度验证，但其灵敏度低于最强的非干涉CSL界限。

Abstract: We propose a measurement-like interferometric test of Continuous Spontaneous Localization (CSL), in which a trapped nanoparticle acts as a reversible mesoscopic pointer rather than as the initially prepared microscopic superposition. A microscopic two-branch system applies opposite weak forces to the nanoparticle, temporarily amplifying which-branch information into branch-conditioned pointer displacements. After one weak-trap period the pointer positions and momenta recombine, so ordinary quantum mechanics predicts recovery of the microscopic coherence, whereas CSL predicts an irreversible visibility loss accumulated while the pointer mass distributions were separated.
  For a $10^{-18},{\rm kg}$ nanoparticle driven by a $10^{-21},{\rm N}$ differential force at $T=0.4,{\rm K}$, including thermal breathing and an ordinary loss budget $Λ_{\rm loss}\lesssim 0.1$, we find $λ_{\min}\simeq 1.4\times 10^{-10},{\rm s}^{-1}$ at $r_c=100,{\rm nm}$ with $10^5$ shots. A more aggressive lower-frequency point reaches $λ_{\min}\simeq 8.7\times 10^{-12},{\rm s}^{-1}$.
  These sensitivities would improve over digitized direct matter-wave CSL bounds by about $7\times 10^3$ and $10^5$, respectively, while remaining above the strongest non-interferometric bounds. The proposal is therefore aimed at a substantially stronger direct visibility-loss test of CSL, and at a reversible measurement-like realization of collapse sensitivity, rather than at the strongest overall exclusion curve.

</details>


### [165] [Lewis-Ermakov approach for the time-dependent two-level system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22709)
*M. Huerta-Sandoval,I. Ramos-Prieto,H. M. Moya-Cessa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过su(2)和su(1,1)代数与时间依赖谐振子的代数对应，构建了显式的Lewis-Ermakov型动力学不变量，从而得到时间依赖二能级系统的闭式演化算子和精确传播子，并应用于Landau-Zener跃迁、非厄米耗散过程和绝热快速通道，以及逆工程设计捷径到绝热性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为时间依赖二能级系统构造显式动力学不变量，以统一处理任意时间依赖耦合和失谐下的系统演化问题。

Method: 利用su(2)和su(1,1)代数与时间依赖谐振子之间的代数对应关系（共享相同的复化sl(2,C)代数），构建Lewis-Ermakov型动力学不变量，推导出闭式演化算子和精确传播子。

Result: 得到了适用于任意时间依赖耦合a(t)和失谐b(t)的闭式演化算子和精确传播子；在辅助标度函数为实数的条件下，获得了完全显式控制场的逆工程捷径到绝热性方案。

Conclusion: 该方法可统一处理Landau-Zener跃迁、非厄米耗散过程和绝热快速通道等多种代表性场景，为时间依赖二能级系统的精确求解和量子控制提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We construct an explicit Lewis-Ermakov-type dynamical invariant for time-dependent two-level systems by exploiting their algebraic correspondence with the time-dependent harmonic oscillator through the $su(2)$ and $su(1,1)$ algebras, which share the common complexification $sl(2,\mathbb{C})$. This invariant provides a closed-form evolution operator and an exact propagator for arbitrary time-dependent coupling $a(t)$ and detuning $b(t)$. We illustrate the method on representative scenarios, including Landau-Zener transitions, non-Hermitian dissipative processes, and adiabatic rapid passage, and we obtain inverse-engineered shortcuts to adiabaticity with fully explicit control fields when the auxiliary scaling function is taken to be real.

</details>


### [166] [An interpretable closed form for entanglement entropy from bitstrings, guided by a graph neural network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22713)
*Anas Saleh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于比特串可导物理标量的六项线性闭式公式来估计Rydberg原子阵列中的二分冯·诺依曼熵，该公式在可解释性与泛化性上取得突破，并揭示了其在大尺度下的标度行为和重校准规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验上Rydberg原子阵列中最易观测的是比特串分布，但其约束的二分冯·诺依曼熵却极难获取。作者旨在构建一个可读、无需重新训练的闭式熵估计器，同时理解其精度、可移植性、标度行为及校准成本。

Method: 利用图神经网络（GNN）定位熵预测的关键特征——二分边界上的两点关联函数；基于此特征集，通过穷举真实标签搜索得到一个六项线性闭式公式；用密度矩阵重正化群（DMRG）扩展到100原子（远超精确对角化的20原子规模）研究尺寸外推问题；分析各系数随系统尺寸的变化规律，并拟合反比尺寸定律以实现免标签部署。

Result: 闭式公式在分布内达到0.024 nats的平均绝对误差（约为GNN的6.4倍），但在6个分布外数据池中有5个误差低于原GNN，第6个持平。DMRG研究表明：小尺寸冻结系数在大尺度下失效，但失效模式有结构可循。按尺寸重新拟合后误差维持在25–50 mnat（交叉验证）；六个系数中有两个遵循干净的反比尺寸定律、一个呈向下弯曲增长、其余无趋势。基于拟合定律可在大约40–80 mnat误差下免标签部署。大尺寸下只需几十个标签即可重校准闭式公式以匹配微调后的分布内集成模型，而非线性ML模型只有在大量标记数据下才能超越。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个兼具可解释性、可迁移性和低校准成本的熵估计方案，明确了不同方法（闭式公式 vs. ML模型）在不同标签预算下的适用边界，为量子模拟中熵的实用测量提供了理论指导。

Abstract: The empirical bitstring distribution is the most accessible observable on Rydberg-atom arrays, but the bipartite von~Neumann entropy it constrains is far costlier to obtain. We present a six-term linear closed form for the entropy, built on bitstring-derivable physics scalars, and characterize its accuracy, portability, scaling behaviour, and calibration cost. The feature set is selected with guidance from a trained graph neural network: probing the network localizes its entropy prediction to the two-point correlators on the bipartition boundary, and an exhaustive ground-truth search restricted to those boundary correlators isolates the form. It reaches $0.024$~nats mean absolute error in distribution: $6.4$ times the network's error, but in a form a human can read and apply without retraining. Fit once and applied unchanged, it has lower error than the base network on five of six out-of-distribution pools and ties the sixth. An independent density-matrix renormalization-group study to one hundred atoms -- five times the reach of exact diagonalization -- settles the size-extrapolation question: coefficients frozen at small size fail at scale, but the failure is structured. Refit per size the form holds to $25$--$50$~mnat (cross-validated); two of its six slopes follow clean inverse-size laws, one a downward curving growth, and the others are trendless; the fitted laws deploy the form label-free at roughly $40$--$80$~mnat. The result fixes a label-budget rule: at large sizes, a few dozen labels recalibrate the closed form to match a fine-tuned in-distribution ensemble on the same features, while nonlinear ML models pull ahead only given large labelled datasets.

</details>


### [167] [Emergence of Gaussian entanglement and non-Gaussianity in high-harmonic generation driven by bright squeezed light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22714)
*J. Rivera-Dean,M. Even-Tzur,M. F. Ciappina,C. Granados,O. Cohen,P. Stammer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究以明亮压缩真空（BSV）光驱动高次谐波产生（HHG）时，谐波场的量子统计特性。发现在双色驱动（强相干光ω + 弱BSV光2ω）下，偶次谐波响应近似线性于BSV正交分量，产生非经典多模高斯纠缠态，可描述为偶次谐波流形上的分布式集体压缩模，并通过协方差矩阵、纠缠结构和量子隐形传态保真度表征其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HHG在经典高斯态驱动下产生的谐波仍保持经典高斯统计，而使用明亮压缩真空（BSV）光驱动可以打破这一范式。本文旨在研究BSV驱动HHG产生非经典高斯态的条件，并识别高斯描述失效的区间，探索在极紫外波段工程化高斯和非高斯光态的潜力。

Method: 采用双色驱动方案：强相干光（频率ω）与微扰BSV光（频率2ω）共同驱动HHG。分析偶次谐波响应对BSV正交分量的线性依赖关系，利用协方差矩阵、纠缠结构分析和量子隐形传态保真度等工具表征产生的量子态。

Result: 偶次谐波响应近似线性于BSV正交分量，产生非经典多模高斯纠缠态；该态可描述为偶次谐波流形上的分布式集体压缩模；成功表征了协方差矩阵、纠缠结构和量子隐形传态保真度。

Conclusion: 非经典驱动的HHG可作为在极紫外波段工程化高斯和非高斯光态的可行平台，为产生极端紫外波段的量子光态提供了新途径。

Abstract: High harmonic generation (HHG) is a highly nonlinear optical process in which radiation from a strong driving field is up-converted into its high-order harmonics. In atomic systems, this nonlinearity manifests itself through the intensity scaling of the emitted harmonics with the driving field strength. Despite the highly nonlinear nature of HHG, when the driving field is prepared in a classical Gaussian state and atomic depletion remains negligible, the quantum statistical properties of the generated harmonics retains classical Gaussian quantum statistics. Driving HHG with bright squeezed vacuum (BSV) light challenges this paradigm, as its enhanced field fluctuations can modify the statistical properties of the generated harmonics. In this work, we investigate the conditions under which BSV-driven HHG gives rise to non-classical Gaussian states, and identify the regimes where this Gaussian description breaks down. For bichromatic driving by a strong coherent field at frequency $ω$ and a perturbative BSV field at $2ω$, the even-harmonic response is approximately linear in the BSV quadrature, leading to non-classical multimode Gaussian entanglement in the harmonic field. We show that this state can be described as a distributed collective squeezed mode over the even-harmonic manifold, and characterize its covariance matrix, entanglement structure, and quantum teleportation fidelity as an operational benchmark. Our results highlight the potential of non-classically driven HHG as a platform for engineering Gaussian and non-Gaussian states of light in the extreme ultraviolet regime.

</details>


### [168] [Protecting Qubits from Purcell Decay via Permanent Dipoles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22732)
*Alex Krasnok*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究利用量子比特的固有永久电偶极矩来抑制Purcell衰减，通过位移腔场使基态和激发态的波函数重叠减少，从而削弱横向耦合。在简化模型中，交换作用在η=√2时消失；完整模型中仍有强抑制。基准测试显示归一化寿命从κT₁=11.1提升至47.3，单次读取错误率从0.21降至0.07。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 读取量子比特通常需要将其与谐振器耦合，但谐振器会为量子比特提供额外的衰减路径（Purcell衰减）。作者希望找到一种方法来减少这种损耗，从而提升量子比特的性能。

Method: 利用量子比特的固有永久电偶极矩，使腔场在量子比特基态和激发态朝不同方向位移，减少波函数重叠，削弱横向的量子比特-腔交换作用。采用简化位移旋转波模型和完整横向模型进行分析，并使用 dressed 开放系统衰减率寻找工作点。

Result: 简化模型中交换作用在η=√2时完全消失；完整模型中该零点被抬升但仍存在强抑制。在固定指针分离条件下，归一化寿命从κT₁=11.1提升至47.3，单次读取错误率从0.21降至0.07。

Conclusion: 永久电偶极矩可以提供一种内部的、通道选择性的Purcell保护机制，有效抑制量子比特的谐振器耦合带来的衰减，同时保持纵向读取信号。

Abstract: Reading out a qubit often requires coupling it to a resonator, but that same resonator can also give the qubit an extra path to decay. Here, we study a way to reduce this loss using a built-in permanent electric dipole. The dipole shifts the cavity field in different directions for the qubit ground and excited states. This shift makes the relevant wave functions overlap less, which weakens the transverse qubit--cavity exchange that causes Purcell decay. In a simplified displaced rotating-wave model, this exchange vanishes at $η=\sqrt{2}$. In the full transverse model, this exact zero is lifted, but strong suppression remains at a larger dipole-induced displacement. Using dressed open-system decay rates, we find an operating point where the cavity-mediated decay is strongly reduced while the longitudinal readout signal remains finite. For the benchmark studied here, at fixed pointer separation, the normalized lifetime increases from $κT_1=11.1$ to $47.3$, and the estimated single-shot readout error drops from $0.21$ to $0.07$. These results show that permanent electric dipoles can provide an internal, channel-selective form of Purcell protection.

</details>


### [169] [All-optical Implementation of Generalized Quantum Teleportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22736)
*Takaya Hoshi,Akito Kawasaki,Xiruo Yan,Atsushi Sakaguchi,Takumi Suzuki,Tatsuki Sonoyama,Hironari Nagayoshi,Kosuke Fukui,Kan Takase,Warit Asavanant*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种全光学前馈(AOFF)架构用于广义量子隐形传态，通过消除光电转换来解决连续变量光量子计算中经典电子前馈电路导致的延迟和吞吐量瓶颈问题，在现实器件参数下实现了容错量子计算所需的噪声抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于测量的连续变量光量子计算受限于经典电子前馈电路的处理延迟和吞吐量瓶颈，严重制约了实际性能。

Method: 提出一种容错的全光学前馈(AOFF)架构，用于可执行任意线性操作的广义量子隐形传态，完全消除光电转换环节。

Result: 在现实器件参数下的定量噪声分析表明，该架构成功抑制了硬件引入的噪声本底，验证了其与容错量子计算的兼容性。

Conclusion: 该方案通过消除光电转换实现连续高吞吐量操作，大幅缩短电路运行时间，提供了一个兼具操作灵活性与光量子信息处理固有速度和带宽优势的噪声鲁棒平台。

Abstract: Measurement-based continuous-variable optical quantum computing inherently offers high-speed, large-scale operations, yet its practical performance remains constrained by the processing latencies and throughput bottlenecks imposed by classical electronic feedforward circuits. To overcome these limitations, we propose a loss-tolerant, all-optical feedforward (AOFF) architecture for generalized quantum teleportation capable of executing arbitrary linear operations. Quantitative noise analysis under realistic device parameters demonstrates that the architecture successfully suppresses hardware-induced noise floor, confirming its compatibility with fault-tolerant quantum computing requirements. By eliminating optoelectronic conversions, this scheme enables continuous high-throughput operations that drastically reduce circuit runtime. Ultimately, this approach delivers a noise-resilient platform that reconciles operational versatility with the intrinsic speed and bandwidth of optical quantum information processing.

</details>


### [170] [Wigner-Negative Magnon Steady States from Incoherent Qubit Pumping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22753)
*Yan Liu,Jiahua Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用非相干泵浦的量子比特实现级联耗散机制，稳定产生具有Wigner负性的磁振子稳态，单量子比特下可实现单磁振子占据接近1、强反聚束和显著Wigner负性，多量子比特扩展则可获得更高Fock分量的Wigner负态，且解析出生死模型与数值结果吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何利用非相干泵浦的量子比特作为可控耗散资源，产生非经典磁振子稳态，特别是具有Wigner负性的量子态。

Method: 将量子比特的非相干泵浦与色散磁振子数选择性相结合，构建级联耗散机制，通过单量子比特和多量子比特系统实现稳态Wigner负态，并建立解析生死模型进行验证。

Result: 单量子比特系统实现单磁振子占据接近1，强反聚束和显著Wigner负性；多量子比特系统获得由更高Fock分量主导的Wigner负稳态；解析生死模型与数值结果一致。

Conclusion: 非相干量子比特泵浦可作为可控耗散资源，在混合量子系统中产生非经典磁振子态，为量子信息处理提供新途径。

Abstract: We show that incoherently pumped qubits can realize a cascaded dissipative mechanism for stabilizing Wigner-negative magnon steady states. The mechanism combines qubit pumping with dispersive magnon-number selectivity to direct the steady-state population toward selected magnon Fock states. In the single-qubit case, the single-magnon population can approach unity, accompanied by strong antibunching and pronounced Wigner negativity. Extending the same principle to multiple qubits yields Wigner-negative steady states dominated by higher magnon Fock components. We further derive an analytical birth--death model that captures the mechanism and agrees with numerical results. These results establish incoherent qubit pumping as a controllable dissipative resource for generating nonclassical magnon states in hybrid quantum systems.

</details>


### [171] [Linear optical Bell state measurement for rotation-symmetric cat codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22832)
*Issa Oe,Suguru Endo,Rui Asaoka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对旋转对称猫（RS-cat）码的贝尔态测量（BSM）协议，仅使用半束分束器（HBS）和光子数分辨探测器（PNRD），通过利用RS-cat码的离散旋转对称性诱导的光子数结构，实现了光子数模和相位信息的联合提取。在理想无损耗条件下，该BSM协议对于任意对称阶数N和大振幅α是确定性的；在有光子损耗情况下，高阶RS-cat码在特定损耗区间具有优势，且后选择可提升成功概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长距离量子通信和融合型量子计算（FBQC）需要高效的贝尔态测量（BSM）作为关键原语，而现有基于玻色编码的BSM方案在实现高成功概率和抗光子损耗方面面临挑战。

Method: 利用RS-cat码的离散旋转对称性导致的光子数特殊结构，设计仅需半束分束器（HBS）和光子数分辨探测器（PNRD）的BSM方案，同时提取光子数模（模N信息）和相位信息来区分贝尔态。

Result: 理想无损耗条件下，对于任意对称阶数N，当振幅α足够大时BSM协议是确定性的；数值评估了光子损耗下的成功概率，发现高阶RS-cat码在特定损耗区间具有优势；后选择可进一步提升成功概率。

Conclusion: 提出的BSM协议为RS-cat码在长距离量子通信和FBQC中的应用提供了实用且高效的方案，特别是在中等光子损耗场景下高阶编码的优势明显。

Abstract: Rotation-symmetric cat (RS-cat) codes are a bosonic-code platform for quantum information processing, combining finite-energy realizability with robustness against photon loss through their discrete rotational symmetry. For applications in long-distance quantum communication and fusion-based quantum computation (FBQC), efficient Bell state measurement (BSM) is a key primitive. In this work, we consider a BSM protocol for RS-cat codes using only a half beam splitter (HBS) and photon-number-resolving detectors (PNRDs). By exploiting the characteristic photon-number structure induced by the discrete rotational symmetry of RS-cat codes, our protocol extracts both photon-number modulo and phase information for Bell-state discrimination. We show that, under ideal loss-free conditions, the proposed BSM protocol becomes deterministic for arbitrary symmetry order $N$ for sufficiently large amplitudes $α$. We further numerically evaluate the success probability under photon loss and identify the loss regime in which higher-order RS-cat codes provide an advantage. Finally, we show that post-selection can enhance the success probability.

</details>


### [172] [When the Learning With Errors Problem Meets the Coherent Ising Machine: A Penalty-Free Algorithm-Hardware Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22843)
*Shuxian Jiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出CIM-BDD，一种将LWE问题通过无惩罚映射转化为QUBO问题的混合有界距离解码求解器，采用CR-BNP编码和自适应混合进制编码器减少量子比特需求，并在相干伊辛机上端到端验证了40维LWE挑战实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LWE问题是后量子密码学的数学基础，对其的密码分析涉及经典格归约、机器学习和量子-经典混合方法。现有方法在NISQ设备上存在量子比特需求高、惩罚项引入额外复杂性的问题，需要更高效的量子-经典混合求解范式。

Method: 通过代数消元将秘密嵌入q-ary格，将LWE转化为最近向量问题（CVP）；采用无惩罚映射直接将平方误差范数作为QUBO能量；提出连续松弛Babai最近平面投影驱动的自适应混合进制编码器，减少量子比特数和QUBO系数范围；推导统计有界早停阈值作为单侧证书和Decision-LWE区分器。

Result: 在TU Darmstadt LWE挑战上，在相干伊辛机CPQC-550上端到端验证了40维Search-LWE和Decision-LWE实例。

Conclusion: 建立了量子-经典混合密码分析的新算法-硬件协同设计范式。

Abstract: The Learning With Errors (LWE) problem constitutes the mathematical foundation of modern Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC). Cryptanalysis of LWE ranges from classical lattice reduction to machine learning and quantum-classical hybrids. We propose CIM-BDD, a hybrid Bounded-Distance-Decoding solver that reduces LWE to a Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) problem through a strictly \emph{penalty-free} mapping. An algebraic elimination of the secret embeds LWE into a $q$-ary lattice, absorbing the modular arithmetic and recasting the problem as a Closest Vector Problem (CVP). The squared error norm is then used \emph{directly} as the QUBO energy, so the cryptographic noise is the objective to be minimized rather than a penalized constraint. To realize this general model on current Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) devices, we design a special encoding method: a Continuous Relaxed Babai's Nearest Plane (CR-BNP) projection drives an adaptive mixed-radix encoder that greatly reduces both the qubit count and the QUBO coefficient range, so that a single batched hardware submission suffices. We further derive a statistically bounded early-stopping threshold ($T_{\text{early}}$) that acts as a one-sided certificate and doubles as a Decision-LWE distinguisher. We validate the framework on the TU Darmstadt LWE Challenge, giving an end-to-end demonstration for both Search- and Decision-LWE of a $40$-dimensional instance on the Coherent Ising Machine CPQC-550. This work establishes a new algorithm-hardware co-design paradigm for quantum-classical hybrid cryptanalysis.

</details>


### [173] [Bunny Codes: Broadening Superconducting Quantum Error Correction Capability through Advanced Control Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22853)
*Runshi Zhou,Xingye Yuan,Linghang Kong,Fang Zhang,Kai Zhang,Zhaohui Yang,Jianxin Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用超导量子比特扩展的双量子比特门集合（CNOT和CXSWAP），发现了一类名为Bunny codes的qLDPC码。这些码在二维最近邻连接的超导硬件上，无需长程相互作用即可实现与标准CNOT实现等效的性能，并且在码率和逻辑错误率方面优于toric码和surface码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超导量子计算中，实现某些qLDPC码需要长程相互作用，硬件实现复杂。作者希望利用硬件层面可用的扩展本征门集（如CXSWAP），在不增加硬件复杂度的前提下实现高性能量子纠错。

Method: 利用超导量子比特中扩展的本征双量子比特门池（CNOT和CXSWAP），通过穷举搜索发现了一类名为Bunny codes的qLDPC码。这些码在二维最近邻量子比特连接拓扑上实现，无需长程耦合器。

Result: Bunny codes在周期性边界条件下，重量-6稳定子的码率约为toric code的3倍；转换为开放边界条件后，码率约为rotated surface code的2倍。电路级模拟显示，某些Bunny codes的逻辑错误率比码率相当的toric code低一个数量级。

Conclusion: 利用扩展的门集合而非长程耦合器，可以在显著降低硬件复杂度的同时实现高性能量子纠错。

Abstract: Drawing on advances in superconducting qubit control schemes that unlock enriched native gate sets at the hardware level, we systematically examine how harnessing this enlarged physical two-qubit gate pool -- specifically CNOT and CXSWAP -- streamlines syndrome extraction for certain qLDPC codes with nonlocal stabilizers. Through an exhaustive search, we discover a set of qLDPC codes with various stabilizer weights and distances that can be implemented on the two-dimensional nearest-neighbor qubit connectivity native to superconducting hardware while achieving performance equivalent to that of the direct CNOT implementation requiring long-range interactions. We refer to those codes as Bunny codes. Across all code distances we examine, the best Bunny codes with weight-6 stabilizers in periodic boundary conditions have a code rate approximately $3\times$ that of the toric code; when converted to open boundary conditions, they retain an approximately $2\times$ code rate advantage over the rotated surface code.
