In circuit-level simulation, we find that some Bunny codes exhibit logical error rates an order of magnitude lower than toric codes with comparable code rates. Our results demonstrate that high-performance quantum error correction can be achieved using an expanded gate set rather than long-range couplers, thereby significantly reducing hardware complexity.

</details>


### [174] [Adversarial Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Eavesdropping in BB84 Quantum Key Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22962)
*Ramon Jose C. Bagunu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究将经典强化学习（Q-Learning、SARSA、Double Q-Learning）应用于BB84量子密钥分发的自适应窃听。相比固定攻击率的非自适应基线，Q-Learning在1%信道噪声下将检测率从99.4%降至0.28%±0.27%，同时每回合提取约10.5个正确比特，实现355倍降低（p=0.020）。还发现了一种自发的"终局爆发"攻击模式，该模式可通过随机化检查点间隔消除，且不影响隐身性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BB84安全性评估假设Eve以固定、解析优化的速率攻击，忽略了攻击者可以自适应调整策略的可能性。本文旨在探索将Eve建模为学习智能体时，自适应窃听能否突破传统安全性假设。

Method: 将窃听问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），状态为量子比特误码率（QBER）反馈，动作为逐比特决定是否截获。使用三种表格型强化学习算法（Q-Learning、SARSA、Double Q-Learning），在三种信道噪声水平（1%、3%、5%）下训练，每个条件45次训练运行，每次10000回合，跨5个随机种子验证。

Result: 在1%信道噪声下，Q-Learning将检测率从99.4%降至0.28%±0.27%（355倍降低，统计显著p=0.020），每回合提取约10.5个正确比特。发现了自发的"终局爆发"行为：智能体在最终块集中提高攻击率。该漏洞可通过随机化检查点间隔消除，且不影响隐身性能。

Conclusion: 自适应攻击者能够显著削弱BB84的理论安全性保障，现有的固定攻击率基线不足以评估实际安全风险。建议将自适应对抗基线纳入量子密码安全性评估体系。

Abstract: BB84 quantum key distribution derives its security from a physical guarantee that any eavesdropper disturbs the channel in a statistically detectable way. Prior work evaluates this by assuming Eve attacks at a fixed, analytically optimized rate. We examine what happens when Eve is modeled instead as a learning agent. Classical reinforcement learning is used, specifically tabular Q-Learning, SARSA, and Double Q-Learning, to adaptive BB84 eavesdropping. This formulates the attacker's decision as a Markov Decision Process where the agent observes Quantum Bit Error Rate (QBER) feedback and decides, qubit by qubit, whether to intercept or pass. Experiments span three channel noise levels ($μ_{ch}\in\{1\%,3\%,5\%\}$) and are validated across five independent random seeds (45 training runs per condition, 10,000 episodes each). Against the best non-adaptive analytical baseline, Q-Learning reduces detection from $99.4\%$ to $0.28\%\pm0.27\%$ at $μ_{ch}=1\%$ while extracting approximately 10.5 correct bits per episode. This is a 355-fold reduction that is statistically significant ($p=0.020$, Mann-Whitney $U$ test). We also report the spontaneous emergence of an end-game burst, where agents independently learn to surge their attack rate at the final block. This exploit vanishes under randomized checkpoint intervals while stealth performance remains statistically indistinguishable. These results motivate the inclusion of adaptive adversary baselines in quantum cryptographic security evaluations.

</details>


### [175] [Satellite Mission Planning with Rydberg Atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23045)
*Michel Nowak,Benjamin Marchand,Yassine Naghmouchi,Serge Rainjonneau,Wesley Coelho,Louis Vignoli,Louis-Paul Henry*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用基于冷原子（里德伯原子）的量子处理单元（QPU）解决地球观测卫星任务规划问题，通过将问题转化为最大独立集（MIS）问题，并比较了图直接求解和QUBO框架两种方法，实验表明QUBO方法更为有效，论证了QPU在实际运营中解决该问题的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典计算难以处理涉及大量卫星和高密度观测目标的任务规划问题，而基于冷原子的量子计算机在模拟模式下有望处理经典上难以解决的构型数量，因此探索量子计算在该调度问题上的应用具有现实意义。

Method: 首先提出不考虑卫星敏捷性的朴素规划公式，再基于文献扩展至考虑敏捷性。将卫星任务规划问题表述为最大独立集（MIS）问题，然后在基于里德伯原子的QPU上求解。探索了两种求解MIS的途径：直接基于图的方法和基于二次无约束二元优化（QUBO）框架的方法，并对QUBO方法进行了深入的数值实验。

Result: QUBO方法比直接基于图的MIS求解方法更为相关有效；数值实验进一步验证了QUBO方法在解决该卫星任务规划问题上的适用性。

Conclusion: 使用QPU（基于冷原子）解决卫星任务规划问题在实际运营场景中具有潜在效用，尤其是QUBO框架提供了可行的求解路径。

Abstract: Quantum computers relying on cold atoms are being built and promise a high flexibility in the way information in encoded into the physical system. In particular, the analog mode is spiking interest in the field of optimization as a classically intractable number of configurations can be tackled. In this work, we investigate a problem that requires every-day scheduling of critical tasks involving a large number of actors. Namely, fixing the planning for a Earth Observation satellite fleet composed of several of units exposed to a high density of targets to be scanned. We explore numerical schemes that convert the formulated problem into a cold-atoms friendly setup. We begin by a naive formulation of the Satellite Mission Planning problem without taking account for the agility of the satellites. We then extend the problem to take it into account based on the literature. By formulating the planning problem as a Maximum Independent Set problem, we are able to solve the problem with a QPU based on Rydberg atoms. We explore two ways of solving the MIS problem on the QPU, one relying on the graphs and on the Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization Framework (QUBO). We show that the QUBO methodology is the most relevant and explore it more deeply with numerical experiments. We conclude on the potential utility of using a QPU to solve the Satellite Mission Planning problem in an operational context.

</details>


### [176] [An Efficient and Perfect Secret Sharing Scheme on a Class of Non-Maximal Quantum Access Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23082)
*Lei Li,Zhi Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对非最大量子接入结构下的量子秘密共享方案存在量子资源消耗高、安全与效率难以平衡的问题，重点研究了一类非最大QAS——三超边超星的判定准则与高效完美方案构造方法，通过定义代表元QAS并借助经典秘密共享方案，提出了一种通用、高效且完美的量子秘密共享方案，降低了量子态制备与分发难度，提升了安全性与资源利用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子秘密共享方案在非最大量子接入结构下存在量子资源消耗高、安全性与效率难以平衡的问题，亟需提出更优的构造方法。

Method: 首先刻画非最大QAS中的禁集和中间集，给出纯态编码可实现的非最大QAS的刻画条件；然后定义三超边超星的代表元QAS作为其子结构；最后利用经典秘密共享方案作为辅助，基于该超星QAS提出通用、高效且完美的量子秘密共享方案。

Result: 通过将轻量级量子资源部署到代表元接入结构，降低了量子态制备与分发难度，提升了安全性和资源利用效率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方案在非最大QAS下实现了高效且完美的量子秘密共享，为量子通信安全领域提供了新的技术支撑。

Abstract: Quantum secret sharing is one of the core security technologies in the field of quantum communication. Aiming at the problems of high quantum resource consumption and the difficulty in balancing security and efficiency existing in current quantum secret sharing schemes under non-maximal quantum access structure(QAS), this paper focuses on the judgment criteria of non-maximal QAS and the construction methods of efficient and perfect quantum secret sharing schemes on a kind of non-maximal QAS, i.e. hyperstar with three hyperedges. Firstly, we characterizes the forbidden sets and intermediate sets within this non-maximal QAS, and provides a characterization for non-maximal QAS realizable by pure-state encoding. Secondly, the representative element QAS of hyperstar with three hyperedges is defined, which serves as a substructure of this hyperstar QAS, and belongs to non-maximal QAS. Furthermore, we propose a universal, efficient, and perfect quantum secret sharing scheme based on this hyperstar QAS using corresponding classical secret sharing scheme as an auxiliary. By deploying lightweight quantum resources to representative element access structures, the proposed scheme reduces the difficulty in quantum state preparation and distribution, and enhances its security and resource utilization efficiency.

</details>


### [177] [Understanding Squeezed States of Light Through Wigner's Phase-Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23102)
*Sibel Baskal,Marilyn E. Noz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统阐述了从经典物理到量子力学的相空间转变，重点讨论了维格纳相空间分布函数在量子光学中的应用，包括压缩态、纠缠态生成、耦合谐振子以及退相干问题，并利用多种对称群（洛伦兹群、辛群、欧几里得群）进行理论构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相空间概念极大促进了从经典物理到量子力学的转变，但量子力学中的相空间表述（尤其是维格纳函数）在量子光学等快速发展的领域中已成为重要科学语言，因此需要系统梳理其理论基础与应用。

Method: 从密度矩阵定义维格纳相空间分布函数；通过相空间图像分析谐振子、相干态、单模/双模压缩态及压缩真空态；利用洛伦兹群、二维/四维辛群、欧几里得群等对称群进行理论构建；通过重构庞加莱球重新审视光场退相干问题。

Result: 阐明了压缩态天然产生双模间的纠缠特性；建立了耦合谐振子与维格纳相空间的联系；通过对称群方法为量子光学中的相空间表述提供了严格的数学框架；以庞加莱球重构展示了密度矩阵在退相干问题中的新视角。

Conclusion: 量子力学的相空间图像（特别是维格纳函数的最简形式）已成为量子光学领域不可或缺的科学语言，在压缩态、纠缠生成和退相干等前沿问题中具有广泛应用价值。

Abstract: This paper starts with the transition from classical physics to quantum mechanics which was greatly aided by the concept of phase space. The role of canonical transformations in quantum mechanics is addressed. The Wigner phase-space distribution function is then defined which arises from the formulation of the density matrix, followed by the harmonic oscillator in phase space. Coherent and one- and two-mode squeezed states of light as well as the squeezed vacuum are discussed in the phase-space picture. Attention is also drawn to the fact that squeezed states naturally generate entanglement between the two-modes. Coupled harmonic oscillators are also elucidated in connection with the Wigner phase space. It will be noted that the phase-space picture of quantum mechanics has become an important scientific language for the rapidly expanding field of quantum optics. Here, we mainly focus on the simplest form of the Wigner function, which finds application in many branches of quantum mechanics. We make use of several symmetry groups such as Lorentz groups, the symplectic group in two and four dimensions, and the Euclidean group. The decoherence problem of an optical field is examined through a reformulation of the Poincaré sphere as a further illustration of the density matrix.

</details>


### [178] [Entropic Uncertainty Relations for Mutually Unbiased Operator Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23185)
*Jesni Shamsul Shaari,Stefano Mancini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在希尔伯特-施密特算子空间框架下发展了算子框架形式的熵不确定关系，通过Riesz-Thorin插值导出一般连续索引算子框架的熵不确定关系，并基于恒模迹重叠定义了一类互无偏算子框架，利用双线性傅里叶变换得到更强的Hirschman-Beckner型熵不确定关系，以Weyl位移算子、Wigner核及位置动量笛卡尔二进框架为例进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将熵不确定关系从传统的测量结果层面推广到算子表示本身，建立算子框架形式下的熵不确定关系理论。

Method: 利用端点范数估计结合Riesz-Thorin插值方法导出熵不确定关系；通过恒模迹重叠定义互无偏算子框架；利用双线性傅里叶变换推导更强的Hirschman-Beckner型关系。

Result: 建立了连续索引算子框架的通用熵不确定关系，以及互无偏算子框架下的更强熵不确定关系，并通过Weyl位移算子、Wigner核和位置动量框架等实例恢复了经典的连续变量傅里叶对偶性。

Conclusion: 熵不确定关系可以自然地从测量结果扩展至算子表示层面，互无偏算子框架和双线性傅里叶变换是获得更强约束的关键结构。

Abstract: We develop an operator-frame formulation of entropic uncertainty relations in the Hilbert-Schmidt space of operators. For general continuous indexed operator frames, we derive an entropic uncertainty relation for the associated coefficient distributions by combining endpoint norm estimates with Riesz-Thorin interpolation. We then identify a distinguished class of mutually unbiased operator frames, defined through constant-modulus trace overlaps. Under suitable structural conditions, the corresponding coefficient amplitudes are related by a bilinear Fourier transform, leading to a stronger Hirschman-Beckner-type entropic uncertainty relation. As canonical realizations, we consider Weyl displacement operators and Wigner kernels, as well as Cartesian dyadic frames generated by position and momentum eigenstates. These examples recover familiar continuous-variable Fourier dualities while extending entropic uncertainty relations beyond measurement outcomes to operator representations themselves.

</details>


### [179] [Efficient Energy-Constrained Semi-Device-Independent QRNG with an Integrated Heterodyne Receiver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23231)
*Mattia Sabatini,Carles Roch i Carceller,Tommaso Bertapelle,Andrea Peri,Giulio Gualandi,Matías Rubén Bolaños,Yoann Piétri,Giacomo Corrielli,Paolo Villoresi,Giuseppe Vallone,Roberto Osellame,Armin Tavakoli,Marco Avesani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验演示了一种基于光子数约束的半设备无关量子随机数发生器(QRNG)，采用四态相干态星座和零差探测，在连续变量半设备无关QRNG中实现了每测量0.223比特的最高认证随机数产率，并结合熵累积方法实现了有限尺寸下的随机性认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半设备无关QRNG框架在强安全性保证和高随机数产率之间取得了良好平衡，且仅需少量实用的物理假设。作者旨在实验验证一种基于光子数约束的新方案，该方案特别适合光子学实现，并追求实现目前连续变量半设备无关QRNG中最高的可认证随机数产率。

Method: 采用四态相干态星座（对称分布在相空间中），通过零差探测进行测量。利用半定松弛技术直接计算可认证Shannon熵的下界，并结合熵累积方法实现有限尺寸下的随机性认证（无需假设独立同分布轮次）。实验采用低损耗集成光子学零差接收器与商用组件的简单发射器相结合。

Result: 实现了每测量0.223比特的可认证随机数产率，这是迄今为止连续变量半设备无关QRNG报道的最高值。

Conclusion: 该方案结合了强安全性保证和高随机数产率，且发射器简单、接收器采用集成光子学，提供了一种实用且高速的半设备无关随机数生成架构。

Abstract: Semi-device-independent QRNG frameworks represent a particularly attractive approach, combining strong security guarantees with high randomness generation rates while relying only on reduced and practical physical assumptions. A recently proposed approach based on photon-number constraints is particularly suited to photonic implementations, where these assumptions can be easily assessed experimentally. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a quantum random number generator within this framework, enabling the direct computation of lower bounds on the certifiable Shannon entropy via semidefinite relaxation techniques. When combined with entropy accumulation methods, this approach enables finite-size randomness certification without assuming independent and identically distributed rounds. We realize the protocol using a four-state coherent-state constellation symmetrically distributed in phase space and measured by heterodyne detection, certifying 0.223 bit per measurement, which is the highest value reported to date for a continuous-variable semi-device-independent QRNG. The implementation combines a low-loss integrated photonic heterodyne receiver with a simple transmitter assembled from commercial components, providing a practical and high-speed architecture for semi-device-independent randomness generation.

</details>


### [180] [Quantum Key Distribution Without Shared Reference Frame Under Unital Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23239)
*Junaid ur Rehman,Shehbaz Tariq,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在未知、静态、单量子比特幺正信道且无共享参考系（如卫星QKD场景）下的量子密钥分发问题，提出了两种无需共享参考系即可克服物理噪声的方法：一是通过构建泡利传输矩阵并识别奇异向量作为最优信号态，将相关性等效为泡利信道；二是顺序基匹配方法。两种方法在有效密钥交换率上等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际量子密钥分发中，信道未知且通信双方由于相对运动旋转无法建立全局共享参考系（如卫星QKD场景），需要设计能够在此条件下工作的QKD方案。

Method: 方法一：构建信道PTM描述（将缺乏共享参考系吸收进信道定义），识别PTM奇异向量作为最优信号态的Bloch向量，在优化局部基下相关性等效于泡利信道。方法二：顺序基匹配（SBM），顺序识别信道优化局部基以实现QKD。

Result: 在优化局部基下，所得相关性经输出重标记后等价于泡利信道，证明BB84和六态QKD协议在此条件下最优；两种方法在有效密钥交换率上等价。

Conclusion: 所提方法可在无共享参考系且信道未知的实用场景下实现安全有效的QKD，为卫星QKD等应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We consider a general and practical scenario of quantum key distribution (QKD) over an unknown, stationary, unital qubit channel. Furthermore, due to practical limitations, e.g., relative movement and rotation of communicating parties, a global shared reference frame cannot be established. This scenario can routinely appear in satellite QKD. We propose two methods to overcome the physical qubit noise and the lack of shared reference frame. The first proposed approach involves constructing the Pauli transfer matrix (PTM) description of the channel, which we achieve without requiring a shared reference frame, by absorbing the lack of shared reference frame in the channel definition. This is followed by the identification of singular vectors of PTM as the Bloch vectors for optimal signal states. In the optimized local bases, the resulting correlations are equivalent, up to outcome relabeling, to those of a Pauli channel, allowing us to show the optimality of the BB84 and six-state QKD protocols under these conditions. The second approach, called the sequential basis matching (SBM) involves sequentially identifying the channel-optimized local bases that enable QKD. We show that both of these approaches result in the same effective key exchange rate for QKD.

</details>


### [181] [Tuning Quantum MPS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23262)
*Anna Leonteva,Maxime Outteryck,Guido Masella*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段框架，用于自动选择量子MPS模拟的超参数：第一阶段通过CMA-ES离线优化构建电路-配置-性能数据库，第二阶段基于电路特征训练混合排序模型推荐配置。实验表明离线优化优于默认设置，但预测器性能受电路族、后端和规模影响，在基于规模的验证中表现优于基于电路族的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPS方法在量子电路经典模拟中效果显著，但实际性能对超参数高度敏感，默认设置通常不是最优的，因此需要自动化的超参数选择方法。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 离线阶段，使用CMA-ES在保真度约束下进行单目标优化，构建电路-配置-性能数据库；(2) 在线阶段，定义静态电路特征（捕捉MPS相关结构特性），训练电路感知的混合排序模型来推荐配置。

Result: 离线优化相比默认设置有所提升，但增益幅度取决于后端、电路族和电路规模。学习到的预测器能恢复部分离线优化增益，基于规模的验证优于基于电路族的迁移验证，但仍未达到离线最优水平。

Conclusion: 自动超参数选择对量子MPS模拟有实际价值，但跨电路族的迁移泛化仍具挑战性，预测器性能有待进一步提升。

Abstract: Matrix Product State (MPS) methods are among the most effective approaches for the classical simulation of quantum circuits, but their practical performance depends strongly on simulator hyperparameters, and default settings are often suboptimal. In this work, we propose a two-stage framework for automatic hyperparameter selection for quantum MPS simulation. In the first stage, we perform offline single-objective CMA-ES optimization under a fidelity constraint and construct a database of circuit--configuration--performance evaluations. In the second stage, we define a set of static circuit features designed to capture MPS-relevant structural properties and train a circuit-aware hybrid ranking model to recommend configurations for different quantum circuits. We evaluate the approach on multiple scalable circuit families using leave-one-family-out and size-based validation. The results show that offline optimization often improves over default settings, although the magnitude of the gain depends strongly on the backend, circuit family, and circuit scale. The learned predictor recovers a meaningful fraction of this gain, with better performance under size-based validation than under family-based transfer, but generally remains below the offline optimum.

</details>


### [182] [Fast quantum-state transfer in Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chains beyond the noninteracting regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23324)
*François Impens,David Guéry-Odelin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出在Su-Schrieffer-Heeger链中通过可调次近邻跳跃相位实现相互作用拓扑系统中的快速高保真边缘态转移，克服了非线性相位积累的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝热捷径在单粒子体系中实现了拓扑边缘态的快速转移，但将其推广到相互作用系统时受到非线性相位积累的阻碍，需要解决这一根本性问题。

Method: 在SSH链中引入相位可调的次近邻跳跃，利用一个跳跃正交分量将系统维持在瞬时暗态轨迹上，另一个正交分量抵消相互作用诱导的自相位调制。在平均场层次上得到精确非线性捷径，并进一步在多体希尔伯特空间中直接优化转移过程。

Result: 平均场方案是非微扰的，可有效抵消相互作用效应；将其应用于完整Bose-Hubbard动力学时，保真度低于1，揭示了超越乘积态图像的多体修正；通过多体空间优化，复相位可调次近邻跳跃可恢复近完美保真度。

Conclusion: 跳跃相位不仅是技术上的精炼，而是实现相互作用拓扑系统中快速高保真输运的关键控制资源。

Abstract: Shortcuts to adiabaticity have made topological edge-state transfer fast in the single-particle regime, but their extension to interacting systems is obstructed by nonlinear phase accumulation. We show that this obstruction can be removed in Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chains by making the next-nearest-neighbor shortcut hopping phase tunable. In the mean-field regime, this yields an exact nonlinear shortcut: one hopping quadrature keeps the state on the instantaneous dark-state trajectory, while the orthogonal quadrature cancels the interaction-induced self-phase modulation. The resulting protocol is nonperturbative in the mean-field interaction strength. When applied to the full Bose-Hubbard dynamics, the mean-field shortcut remains beneficial but saturates below unit fidelity, exposing genuinely many-body corrections beyond the product-state picture. We then optimize the transfer directly in the many-body Hilbert space and find that complex, phase-tunable next-nearest-neighbor hoppings recover near-perfect fidelity. Our results show that hopping phases are not merely a technical refinement, but a key control resource for fast and high-fidelity transport in interacting topological systems.

</details>


### [183] [Bounding Classical and Quantum Correlations in Bayesian Networks with Quasiprobabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23372)
*Paul Becsi,Matty J. Hoban*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了贝叶斯网络中的准概率模型，证明了在广泛网络类中准集合可恢复所有非信号关联，并推测其与嵌套马尔可夫模型等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝尔定理揭示了量子理论与经典因果推理（特别是局域因果性）之间的张力，这被理解为包含未观测节点和观测节点的贝叶斯网络中关联的非经典性。已有大量工作试图理解此类网络中量子关联和经典关联的界限，一种方法是考虑量子关联的外逼近。

Method: 考虑贝叶斯网络的准概率模型——将涉及未观测节点的概率分布替换为满足归一化但不满足正定性的准概率。将此类模型产生的关联集合定义为准集合，并利用与张量网络分解的联系进行分析。

Result: 证明了对于广泛类别的网络，准集合可产生所有非信号关联（推广了贝尔型非经典性研究中的已知结果），并据此提出猜想：准集合可恢复所谓的嵌套马尔可夫模型。

Conclusion: 准概率模型为理解贝叶斯网络中量子与经典关联的界限提供了新视角，与张量网络分解的联系可能具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: Bell's theorem reveals that quantum theory is in tension with classical causal reasoning and, in particular, the notion of local causality. This is now understood as a particular example of non-classicality in the study of correlations in (Bayesian) networks with both unobserved and observed nodes: the correlations are probability distributions over the observed nodes. There is a great deal of work aiming to understand the bounds on quantum and classical correlations in such networks and one approach is to consider outer approximations to the former. Along these lines, we consider quasiprobabilistic models for Bayesian networks, which can be seen as classical models but the probability distributions involving unobserved nodes are "replaced" with quasiprobabilities that respect normalisation but not positivity. We denote the set of correlations resulting from these models as the quasi set. Such models have a history in the study of Bell-type non-classicality where it has been shown that they can produce all non-signalling correlations. We show a generalisation of this result for a broad class of networks, which motivates a conjecture that the quasi set recovers the so called nested Markov model. Our work utilises a connection to tensor network decompositions, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [184] [On the cryptographic potential of single-qubit rotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23392)
*Alex B. Grilo,Lucas Hanouz,Anne Marin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出两种可组合安全构造，使得量子密码协议中原本需要可信量子比特制备或测量的参与方，可将这些任务委托给不可信提供方，仅需依赖可信单量子比特旋转设备，从而在Qline架构上支持多种单量子比特协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子通信中，密码协议通常要求用户拥有可信的量子比特源或探测器。本文旨在减少这些严格依赖，允许参与方仅通过可信的单量子比特旋转设备完成协议，将制备和测量任务委托给不可信方。

Method: 提出两种可组合安全构造：(1) 实现单量子比特测量，通用适用于任何上下文；(2) 处理量子比特制备，虽依赖特定假设，但这些假设被证明在大多数常见量子密码协议中天然满足。

Result: 两种构造在可组合安全框架下得到验证；第二构造所需的假设在主流量子密码协议中普遍成立；研究为Qline作为支持多种单量子比特协议的多功能架构提供了形式化验证。

Conclusion: 大多数量子密码协议中，原本需要可信量子比特制备或测量的参与方可将这些任务委托给不可信提供方，仅依赖可信单量子比特旋转设备；Qline被正式确认为一种支持广泛单量子比特协议的多功能架构。

Abstract: In the domain of quantum communication, cryptographic protocols often require users to have access to trusted qubit sources or detectors. Recently, it was shown that on an architecture called the Qline, several protocols can equivalently be performed by parties capable only of single-qubit rotations. In this work, we introduce two composably secure constructions that together show how in most quantum cryptographic protocols, parties traditionally required to perform trusted qubit preparation or measurement can delegate these tasks to an untrusted provider and instead rely on a trusted single-qubit rotation device. Our first construction implements single-qubit measurement and is universally applicable across any context. In contrast, our second construction, which addresses qubit preparation, relies on specific assumptions regarding the underlying protocol. We show, however, that these assumptions are inherently satisfied by the vast majority of common quantum cryptographic protocols. A notable consequence of our results is the formal validation of the Qline as a versatile architecture capable of supporting a wide range of single-qubit protocols.

</details>


### [185] [Quantum Convolutional Neural Networks for Groundwater Heat Plume Prediction: A Surrogate Modeling Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23411)
*Danyal Maheshwari,Julia Pelzer,Miriam Schulte*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探索了量子卷积神经网络（QCNN）作为地热热泵引起的地下水温度变化预测的替代模型，在理想模拟器、噪声模拟器、IBM 127量子比特Kyiv处理器及增强纠错硬件上进行了评估，结果显示QCNN在模拟器上表现稳健，在纠错硬件条件下性能显著提升，但预测精度仍低于经典神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子机器学习在复杂环境系统建模中的潜力日益受到关注，现有经典替代模型在处理高维地下水热羽流模拟数据时面临计算瓶颈，而当前量子硬件的可扩展性限制要求对原始高维输出进行降维压缩，因此需要探索一种能在近期量子设备上高效运行的量子替代模型。

Method: 提出QCNN架构，包含量子卷积层、量子池化层和全连接量子读出阶段；卷积与池化操作通过基于旋转门的参数化量子电路和测量驱动解码实现；采用哈密顿量启发的特征编码方案制备输入量子态；将原始高维模拟输出降维为一组紧凑的代表性参数作为训练目标；在理想状态向量模拟器、噪声模拟器、IBM 127量子比特Kyiv处理器以及配备先进纠错技术的硬件四种后端上评估模型。

Result: 经典神经网络仍达到最高预测精度；QCNN在模拟器上取得竞争性且一致的表现；在纠错增强的硬件条件下观察到QCNN性能的明显提升。

Conclusion: 量子增强替代建模是未来地下水温度预测的一个有前景的方向，随着量子硬件和纠错技术的持续成熟，QCNN有望在实际环境工程应用中发挥更大作用。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning methods are increasingly explored for modeling complex environmental systems, including groundwater heat plume dynamics. In this work, we explore a Quantum Convolutional Neural Network (QCNN) as a surrogate model for predicting temperature variations in groundwater induced by geothermal heat pumps in the city of Munich. To comply with the scalability constraints of current quantum hardware, the original high-dimensional simulation output is reduced to a compact set of representative parameters that serve as training targets for the surrogate. The proposed QCNN architecture consists of a quantum convolutional layer, a quantum pooling layer, and a fully connected quantum readout stage. Convolution and pooling operations are realized via parameterized quantum circuits based on rotational gates and measurement-driven decoding, while a Hamiltonian-inspired feature-encoding scheme is used to prepare informative input states on the quantum device. We evaluate the QCNN across multiple execution backends, including an ideal statevector simulator, a noisy simulator, IBM's 127-qubit Kyiv quantum processor, and the same hardware augmented with advanced error-mitigation techniques. Realistic noise models are employed to approximate device behavior and to assess the impact of mitigation strategies. Model performance is benchmarked using mean squared error (MSE) on both training and testing sets. The results show that, although classical neural networks still achieve the highest predictive accuracy, the QCNN attains competitive and consistent performance on simulators and exhibits noticeable improvement under error-mitigated hardware conditions. These findings indicate that quantum-enhanced surrogate modeling is a promising direction for future groundwater temperature prediction as quantum hardware and error-mitigation techniques continue to mature.

</details>


### [186] [Dissipative preparation of Laughlin-like states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23451)
*Tong Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于局域损耗和泵浦通道的纯耗散协议，用于制备填充因子为1/3的Laughlin-like态，并可推广至其他1/M填充态。研究表明，Laughlin-like态是Lindbladian的精确稳态，可从任意初始态到达，且Lindbladian间隙随系统尺寸增大有下界。进一步展示了通过缓慢调制泵浦通道实现Laughlin-like态的绝热泵浦。该工作为在近期量子模拟器上制备和操控分数量子霍尔态开辟了可行途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数量子霍尔态是强关联量子物质的核心范例和拓扑量子计算的关键平台，但实验制备和操控仍面临挑战，亟需可行的实现方案。

Method: 提出基于局域损耗和泵浦通道的纯耗散协议，利用Lindblad主方程框架，证明Laughlin-like态为精确稳态，并分析Lindbladian间隙的下界行为。

Result: Laughlin-like态是Lindbladian的精确稳态，可从任意初始态到达；Lindbladian间隙随系统尺寸增大有下界；通过慢调制泵浦通道可实现绝热泵浦。

Conclusion: 该耗散协议为在近期量子模拟器上制备和操控分数量子霍尔态提供了可行途径，有望推动拓扑量子计算的发展。

Abstract: Fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states are a central paradigm of strongly correlated quantum matter and a key platform for topological quantum computation. Here, we propose a purely dissipative protocol based on local loss and pump channels for preparing Laughlin-like states at filling $1/3$, with a possible extension to other $1/M$ filling states. We show that Laughlin-like states are the exact steady states of the Lindbladian and can be reached from arbitrary initial states. We find that the Lindbladian gap bounded from below with increasing system size. We further demonstrate adiabatic pumping of a Laughlin-like state through slow modulation of the pump channels during the evolution. Our work opens a feasible route to preparing and manipulating FQH states on near-term quantum simulators.

</details>


### [187] [Improved State Readout in NV Centers using Regression Models and Rabi Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23454)
*Fritz Haltenberger,Manpreet Singh Jattana*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于回归模型的数据驱动方法，利用NV色心在拉比振荡期间的荧光动力学信号来替代传统对比度校准方法，实现了更高保真度的固态量子寄存器状态读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于对比度的校准方法将NV色心的动态荧光行为简化为标量值，并独立计算每个可能状态的概率，导致读出保真度受限。需要一种能够充分利用荧光动力学信息的方法来提升读出精度。

Method: 使用回归模型（线性岭回归和非线性核模型）将实验测量的荧光信号映射到理想的模拟布居数。通过在诱导拉比振荡期间进行测量来增强荧光信号的信息含量。

Result: 包含动力学信号的荧光数据显著降低了多种模型的态读出误差。线性岭回归的表现几乎与非线性核模型相当，表明简单模型已能捕捉增强荧光信号与态布居数之间的映射关系。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动方法在实验设置中实现了比传统校准更高的保真度，为固态量子寄存器中的高保真度态读出提供了稳健的替代方案。

Abstract: Readout of state populations in nitrogen-vacancy centers from fluorescence measurements at room-temperature is routinely achieved via contrast-based calibration. The fidelities achieved by this conventional approach are limited by reducing the dynamical fluorescence behaviour of the NV center to a scalar value, and calculating the population of each possible state independently. To address these limitations, we use regression models trained on experimental data to map the fluorescence signals onto ideal simulated populations. Additionally, we enhance the informational content of the fluorescence signals by performing measurements during induced Rabi oscillations. Our results demonstrate that including these dynamical signals significantly reduces state readout errors across multiple tested models. Notably, linear ridge regression performs nearly on par with a non-linear kernel-based model, showing that simple models already capture the relevant mapping between the enhanced fluorescence signals and the underlying state populations. This data-driven approach provides a robust alternative that achieves higher fidelities than conventional calibration in our setting, paving the way for high-fidelity state readout in solid-state quantum registers.

</details>


### [188] [Prethermal rotating-frame solid echo in a dipolar nuclear-spin network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23467)
*Quentin Reynard-Feytis,William Beatrez,Leo Joon Il Moon,Emanuel Druga,Ashok Ajoy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在金刚石中偶极耦合¹³C核自旋网络驱动的脉冲自旋锁定下，实现了Floquet预热化旋转框架中的固体回波，其包络以拉伸指数衰减，特征时间约13 ms，归因于Floquet微运动在不同算子子空间间转移相干性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Floquet预热化如何赋予相互作用量子固体长寿命、近似守恒量，从而实现哈密顿工程和新动力学探测方法。

Method: 利用超极化偶极耦合¹³C核自旋网络（金刚石中），采用脉冲自旋锁定驱动，进入旋转框架预热化平台，测量准守恒横向磁化强度并实现周期分辨感应读出。

Result: 观察到旋转框架固体回波：延迟τ后信号在2τ时刻恢复，最大幅度在α≈π/2附近；回波包络以拉伸指数衰减，特征时间T₂'≈13 ms。

Conclusion: 分析表明回波源于Floquet微运动将相干性在不同算子子空间间转移，仅部分多体退相动力学被y脉冲反转。该工作将经典回波物理推广到预热化旋转框架，为高通量光谱学、哈密顿工程和长时量子传感提供了新平台。

Abstract: Floquet prethermalization can endow interacting quantum solids with long-lived, approximately conserved quantities, enabling Hamiltonian engineering and new dynamical probes. Using a hyperpolarized network of dipolar-coupled $^{13}$C nuclear spins in diamond driven by pulsed spin-locking, we access a rotating-frame prethermal plateau with quasi-conserved transverse magnetization and cycle-resolved inductive readout. Within this prethermal manifold we observe a robust \emph{rotating-frame solid echo}: after an apparent decay of the rotating-frame free-induction signal over a delay $τ$, the magnetization revives at time $2τ$ following a single $(α)_y$ pulse, with maximum amplitude near $α\simeqπ/2$. The echo envelope decays as a stretched exponential with characteristic time $T_2'\approx 13\,$ms. Analytical arguments and toy-model simulations attribute the revival to Floquet micromotion that transfers coherences between operator subspaces, so that only a subset of the many-body dephasing dynamics is inverted by the $y$ pulse. These results translate classic echo physics into the prethermal rotating frame and point to continuously interrogated prethermal spin ensembles as a versatile platform for high-throughput spectroscopy, Hamiltonian engineering, and long-duration quantum sensing.

</details>


### [189] [How Stark units enter SIC overlaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23535)
*Ingemar Bengtsson,Gary McConnell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了SIC-POVM中向量标量积的代数结构，发现重叠单元总是基域中最大整数环上射线类域的Stark单位平方根的整数幂乘积，非最小情况下涉及多个射线类域的晶格，每两个维度中部分重叠单元等于±1，且这些结果与Shintani-Faddeev模余循环直接计算结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示SIC-POVM中向量标量积的代数性质，特别是其与Stark单位和射线类域的深层联系，并验证与Shintani-Faddeev模余循环方法的一致性。

Method: 结合精确代数推导与数值实验，分析最小和非最小SIC-POVM中重叠单元的代数结构，研究射线类域上Stark单位平方根的整数幂乘积性质，并利用射线类域的特殊性质解释特定维度中重叠单元等于±1的现象。

Result: 发现重叠单元总是基域最大整数环上射线类域的Stark单位平方根的整数幂乘积；非最小情况涉及多个射线类域构成的晶格；每两个维度中（按特定计数方式）部分重叠单元等于±1，这源于射线类域的特殊性质。

Conclusion: SIC-POVM的重叠单元具有由Stark单位平方根幂乘积构成的代数结构，该结构与射线类域紧密相关，且与Shintani-Faddeev模余循环方法得到的结论互补一致。

Abstract: It has been observed that the mutual scalar products of the vectors in a SIC-POVM are given by algebraic units, and at least in some cases by square roots of Stark units. The full picture is somewhat more complicated, especially if non-minimal SIC-POVMs are considered. We present a mixture of exact and numerical evidence suggesting that the overlap units are always products of integral powers of square roots of Stark units from ray class fields all of which are attached to the maximal ring of integers in the base field. In the non-minimal case a lattice of such ray class fields is involved. In every second dimension (counted in a certain way) some of the overlap units equal $\pm 1$, and we show that this follows from a special property of the ray class fields. Our observations are complementary to but consistent with the claim that the overlap units can be calculated directly from the Shintani--Faddeev modular cocycle.

</details>


### [190] [Structure and information measures of few-electron systems under a spherically symmetric Gaussian potential within a density functional approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23538)
*Raveena Arya,Santanu Mondal,Amlan K. Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于密度泛函理论，采用类高斯势下的工作函数交换势、Wigner/LYP相关泛函及广义伪谱方法，系统研究了H、He-like（Z=2-18）、Li、Be离子的能量、信息论测度（Shannon熵、Fisher信息）及相关效应，发现势阱宽度减小导致能量升高、Shannon熵出现最小值、Fisher信息出现最大值，Fisher-Shannon平面显示电子密度局域化和相对电子关联效应减弱，Collins猜想呈现非线性环状特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在球对称高斯势（量子点模型）下，通过密度泛函方法精确计算多电子离子体系的能量与信息论特征，揭示势阱参数（宽度、深度）对电子结构及关联效应的调控机制，为量子点中电子性质的操控提供理论依据。

Method: 采用密度泛函形式，求解径向Kohn-Sham方程；使用基于工作函数的交换势；引入两种电子关联泛函（局域参数化Wigner泛函和非线性梯度-拉普拉斯依赖的LYP泛函）；采用广义伪谱方法（GPSM）实现非均匀最优空间离散化并满足Dirichlet边界条件；计算Shannon熵（位置空间S_r、动量空间S_p）和Fisher信息（位置空间I_r）等信息论测度。

Result: 势阱宽度减小导致能量升高；S_r出现最小值，S_p和I_r出现最大值；势阱深度增加进一步放大上述效应；Fisher-Shannon平面显示电子密度逐步局域化与压缩，相对电子关联效应减弱；Collins猜想呈现非线性环状特征；同时报告了He原子低能激发态1s2s（¹³S）的结果。

Conclusion: 通过调节高斯势的宽度和深度可以有效操控体系的能量和电子密度分布；信息论测度（Shannon熵、Fisher信息）能敏锐反映电子局域化与关联效应的变化；大部分结果首次报道，为量子点电子性质调控提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Energies of H, He-like ($Z=2-18$) ions, Li, and Be are investigated under a spherically symmetric Gaussian potential through a density functional formalism. The radial Kohn-Sham equation has been solved by invoking a work function-based exchange potential. The effect of electron correlation is analyzed by incorporating two functionals: a local parameterized Wigner functional and a non-linear gradient- and Laplacian-dependent Lee-Yang-Parr (LYP) functional. The generalized pseudospectral method is employed to provide accurate numerical eigenfunctions and eigenvalues. This allows nonuniform, optimal spatial discretization fulfilling the Dirichlet boundary conditions. This work demonstrates a possible manipulation of energy by controlling dot parameters. Apart from ground states, exploratory results are also reported for low-lying excited state $1s2s$ ($^{1,3}S$) of He atom. Companion calculations are also performed for various information-theoretic measures, such as Shannon entropy in position ($S_{r}$), momentum ($S_{p}$) spaces, and Fisher information in position space ($I_{r}$). The behavior of correlation functionals in presence of Gaussian potential is examined critically. We find that energy increases, $S_{r}$ exhibits minima, while $S_{p}$, $I_{r}$ attain maxima for a decrease in the width of potential, whereas an increase in potential depth further amplifies these effects across all properties. The Fisher-Shannon plane reveals a progressive localization as well as the compression of electronic density, and thereby indicates a weakening of relative electron-correlation effects. In the Collin's conjecture, it gives rise to a non-linear loop-like feature. Much of the results are presented here for the first time.

</details>


### [191] [Structure-Aware Variance Reduction for Unbiased Randomized Hamiltonian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23544)
*Joshua W. Dai,Fredrik Hasselgren,Chusei Kiumi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出连续时间演化概率角度插值（continuous TE-PAI）方法，消除了Trotter离散化误差，并通过经典方差缩减技术将随机化哈密顿量模拟的采样开销降低约91%-96%，同时不引入额外偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机化哈密顿量模拟方法常在系统偏差与采样开销之间存在权衡。现有Trotter化方法仅在无限深度极限下消除离散化误差，且会导致张量网络模拟中键维度的非物理指数增长。需要一种无偏且高效的随机化模拟方案。

Method: 提出连续TE-PAI协议，一种拟概率随机电路方法；将方差分解为经典计数分量和量子排序分量；针对经典计数分量应用经典方差缩减技术；对可观测量和估计量定制粗粒度统计量。

Result: 连续TE-PAI在有限深度随机电路下消除Trotter离散化误差；避免了张量网络模拟中键维度的指数增长；在小系统中实现约70%误差降低；在n=30自旋链的张量网络模拟中实现约80%误差降低，对应约91%和约96%的采样成本降低。

Conclusion: 经典方差缩减技术可有效应用于随机化哈密顿量模拟而不引入额外偏差，主导模拟开销来自哈密顿量动力学的非对易部分，针对性地缩减经典计数分量能显著降低采样成本。

Abstract: Randomized Hamiltonian simulation methods are often governed by a trade-off between systematic bias and sampling overhead. We study how classical variance-reduction techniques can be applied to such methods without changing their mean channel, and therefore without introducing additional bias. As a motivating unbiased estimator, we formulate continuous time-evolution probabilistic angle interpolation (continuous TE-PAI), a quasiprobabilistic random-circuit protocol whose remaining Monte Carlo error is purely statistical. Continuous TE-PAI removes Trotter discretization error with finite-depth random circuits, whereas deterministic Trotterization does so only in the infinite-depth limit. Further, in tensor-network simulations, we demonstrate that discretization error can cause an unphysical exponential growth in the bond dimension required for Trotterized simulations, whereas comparable-depth continuous TE-PAI circuits avoid this growth. We then show that the variance of randomized product-formula-based estimators admits a canonical decomposition into a classical counting component and a quantum ordering component such that the dominant simulation overhead results from the non-commutative parts of the Hamiltonian dynamics. Motivated by this decomposition, we achieve an $\approx70\%$ error-reduction using the counting-component for small systems whereas our tensor-network simulations of $n=30$ spin-chain dynamics use coarser statistics tailored to the observable and estimator attaining a negligible bias and a reduction of $\approx 80\%$ leading to $\approx91\%$ and $\approx96\%$ sampling-cost reductions, respectively.

</details>


### [192] [Genuine Global Kochen-Specker Contextuality as Classical Coordination Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23577)
*Ming Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了第三种量子关联机制——真正的全局Kochen-Specker（KS）语境性，其中局部子系统是非语境的、多体块是广义贝尔局域的，但整体经验模型无法用全局非语境隐变量解释。作者提出了一个协调代价框架，将通信、记忆和局部计算视为经典模拟器从局部可用信息维护全局经典解释的不同方式，并引入了协调比特、全局语境覆盖数、任务族标度律以及抽象提升定理，通过极化路径Hardy阻塞和后选择KCBS类型任务作为实例分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟量子关联可能失败的原因除了低通信局域隐变量模型不存在或单一非语境隐态无法解释所有兼容测量语境之外，还存在第三种机制——真正的全局KS语境性。现有研究对前两种机制理解较深，但对第三种机制缺乏系统的理论框架和量化工具。

Method: 提出协调代价框架，将通信、记忆和局部计算统一为经典模拟器维持全局经典解释的资源成本；定义协调比特、全局语境覆盖数、任务族标度律等量化指标；建立抽象提升定理，将KS语境种子族的经典模拟下界迁移到真正的全局KS模型；通过极化路径Hardy阻塞和后选择KCBS任务进行实例验证。

Result: 建立了全局KS语境性的形式化理论框架，包括协调比特等量化指标和提升定理，并通过具体任务验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 真正的全局KS语境性代表了量子关联超越经典模拟的第三种机制，协调代价框架为量化这种资源需求提供了系统方法。

Abstract: Classical simulations of quantum correlations can fail because no low-communication local hidden-variable model exists, or because no single noncontextual hidden state can explain all compatible measurement contexts. This manuscript studies a third regime: genuine global Kochen-Specker contextuality, where local subsystems are noncontextual and the tested multipartite blocks are generalized-Bell-local, but the whole empirical model admits no global noncontextual hidden-variable explanation. We propose a coordination-cost framework in which communication, memory, and local computation are treated as different ways for a classical simulator to maintain a global classical explanation from locally available information. We introduce coordination bits, global contextual covering numbers, scaling laws for task families, and an abstract lifting theorem showing how classical simulation lower bounds for KS-contextual seed families can be transferred to genuinely global-KS models. As worked examples, we analyze a polarization-path Hardy obstruction and postselected KCBS-type tasks.

</details>


### [193] [Random dimension reduction and learning symmetric properties of quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23592)
*Angus Lowe,Xinyu Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为“随机降维”的通用过程，可同时降低多个量子态的维度，同时保持等距张量幂作用下的不变属性。该过程为学习对称属性（包括依赖多个输入态的情况）提供了将样本复杂度中的维度替换为最大秩的黑盒方法。研究表明，降维后结合完全态断层扫描可改进状态对间的距离、保真度和相对熵估计的上界。通过Choi-Jamiolkowski同构实现该过程，揭示了其与Tang、Wright和Zhandry近期提出的随机纯化通道的密切联系，并完成了样本最优断层扫描的端到端分析。最后，证明不存在能同时纯化多个不同输入态的随机纯化通道，表明随机降维与随机纯化相关但本质不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态层析的样本复杂度通常依赖于Hilbert空间的维度，但许多对称属性（如保真度、相对熵）的实际信息复杂度仅取决于态的秩。现有方法无法有效利用这一差异。本文旨在设计一种通用降维框架，使样本复杂度从依赖维度降低为依赖最大秩，同时保持对称属性的不变性，从而改进量子态学习任务的理论界。

Method: 提出随机降维过程：通过等距张量幂作用将多个量子态同时投影到低维子空间，保持对称属性不变。利用Schur变换实现高效量子电路。通过Choi-Jamiolkowski同构将过程映射为量子通道，揭示与随机纯化通道的联系。建立基于该过程的样本最优断层扫描分析方法，避免使用Schur变换或Schur多项式。

Result: 1) 降维后结合完全态断层扫描改进了状态对间距离、保真度和相对熵估计的上界。2) 揭示了随机降维与随机纯化通道的深层联系。3) 完成了样本最优断层扫描的端到端分析。4) 证明不存在能同时纯化多个不同输入态的随机纯化通道，明确区分了两种方法。

Conclusion: 随机降维是一种通用且有效的工具，可将对称量子属性学习的样本复杂度从维度依赖降为秩依赖。该过程与随机纯化相关但不等价，填补了现有方法的空白，为量子态层析和属性估计提供了理论最优的框架。

Abstract: We introduce a procedure called random dimension reduction that simultaneously reduces the dimensions of many, potentially distinct quantum states while preserving properties invariant under the tensor power action of an isometry. This provides a black-box method to replace the dimension with the maximum rank in the sample complexity of learning symmetric properties, even those depending on multiple input states. We show that dimension reduction followed by full state tomography yields improved upper bounds for estimating distances, fidelities, and relative entropies between pairs of states. We also give an efficient quantum circuit implementation of the procedure using the Schur transform.
  Expressing the action of our procedure through the Choi-Jamiolkowski isomorphism reveals an intimate connection with the recently introduced random purification channel by Tang, Wright, and Zhandry. This perspective also completes an end-to-end analysis of sample-optimal tomography without requiring a reference to the Schur transform or Schur polynomials. Finally, we prove that there does not exist a random purification channel that simultaneously purifies copies of multiple, potentially different input states. Hence, random dimension reduction is related to, but distinct from, random purification.

</details>


### [194] [A quantum algorithm for one-shot signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23612)
*Gopikrishnan Muraleedharan,Minh Thuy Truc Pham,Vir Pathak,Thomas Gardner,Chuanqi Zhang,QPerfect,Gavin K. Brennen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种预混淆电路级别的单次签名方案，基于随机仿射陪集叠加和可穿刺伪随机函数，实现了经典公钥/量子密钥对生成与经典签名输出，并给出了详细的量子比特和门复杂度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为单次签名方案提供一种高效的电路级实现，该方案可应用于委托签名、安全令牌转移和公开可验证随机性等场景。

Method: 方案分为两个阶段：密钥生成阶段生成经典公钥/量子密钥对；签名阶段用量子密钥处理消息字符串生成经典签名。核心方法是通过可穿刺伪随机函数的输出确定随机仿射陪集，制备该陪集元素的叠加态，并结合陪集成员测试电路。安全性依赖于预混淆（obfuscation）以抵抗经典和量子多项式时间攻击。

Result: 逻辑量子比特数为Θ(κ log(r) + n + l)，门复杂度为Θ(n³ + nl)，其中r为公钥大小，n+l为签名大小，l为消息大小，κ=Ω(n)为密码安全参数。提供了不同n下的显式量子比特和门计数，并标识了需要预混淆的电路组件。

Conclusion: 该构造无算法错误，经典验证者可高效验证签名结果，为实现安全单次签名提供了可量化的电路级实现路径。

Abstract: We provide a pre-obfuscation circuit-level implementation of an efficient one shot signature scheme, which has known applications to delegated signatures, secured token transfer, and publicly verifiable randomness. The algorithm consists of two stages: a key generation stage where a classical public key/quantum secret key pair is produced, and a signing stage where the quantum secret key is processed with a message string to produce a classical signature. There is no algorithmic error in the construction and the signed message can be efficiently checked by a classical verifier. Our scheme works by preparing a superposition over elements of a random affine coset determined by the output of a puncturable pseudorandom function, together with a circuit that tests coset membership. The logical qubit number scales like $Θ( κ\log(r) + n + l)$ and the gate complexity scales like $Θ(n^3 + nl)$, where $r$ is the public key size, $n+l$ is the signature size, $l$ is the message size, and $κ= Ω(n)$ is the cryptographic security parameter. We provide explicit qubit and gate counts for varying $n$ and identify the circuit components where obfuscation would be required for security against classical and quantum polynomial time attacks.

</details>


### [195] [Log-concavity and tunneling: adiabatic quantum optimization for convex functions (with a spike)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23614)
*Arthur Braida,Elie Bermot,Simon Apers*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了绝热量子优化中量子隧穿效应的理论基础，将“汉明重量带尖峰”（HWS）问题的分析推广到更一般的势能函数。作者建立了离散薛定谔算子的基态对数凹性作为关键结构性质，并利用该性质将HWS的微扰分析扩展到具有对数凹基态的更大势能家族，证明了隧穿效应对凸势能（含尖峰）的广泛适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子隧穿被认为能为量子计算提供加速，绝热量子优化旨在利用这一效应。然而，除了一些学术概念验证（如HWS问题）外，隧穿效应的算法增益仍未被充分探索。本文旨在将HWS问题的分析推广到更一般的势能函数，以揭示隧穿效应的更广泛适用性。

Method: 首先，建立离散一维薛定谔算子基态的对数凹性作为关键结构性质，提出一个框架适用于包含凸势能及某些具有局部极小值的势能家族。在凸情形下，该结果提供了Brascamp和Lieb（'76）连续结果的离散版本。其次，利用基态对数凹性结果，将Reichardt（'04）关于HWS问题的微扰分析扩展到具有对数凹基态的更大势能家族。具体而言，将HWS分析从线性势（精确可解）推广到二次势（不可精确求解）。

Result: 建立了离散对数凹性的框架，获得了新的谱隙界限，超越了Jarret和Jordan（'14）关于凸势能的结果。成功将HWS微扰分析从线性势扩展到二次势，表明隧穿效应对凸势能（含尖峰）具有更广泛的适用性。

Conclusion: 基态对数凹性是理解绝热量子优化中隧穿效应的关键结构性质。本文结果强烈表明，隧穿效应可以更广泛地应用于带有尖峰的凸势能问题，为绝热量子优化算法提供了理论支持。

Abstract: Quantum tunneling is expected to provide a computational speedup in quantum computing, a phenomenon that Adiabatic Quantum Optimization (AQO) aims to leverage. While some academic proofs of concept have been studied, such as the "Hamming weight with a spike" (HWS) problem, the algorithmic gains of this effect remain underexplored. In this work we extend the analysis underlying HWS to more general potentials.
  In the first half of the work, we establish (discrete) log-concavity of the ground state as a key structural property in this context. We devise a framework for establishing log-concavity of the ground state for a large family of discrete, 1-dimensional Schrödinger operators. The family includes convex potentials, but also certain potentials with local minima. In the convex case, this provides a discrete version of a continuous result by Brascamp and Lieb ('76). We demonstrate the utility of our result by establishing new spectral gap bounds, going beyond related results by Jarret and Jordan ('14) for convex potentials.
  In the second half of the work, we use our results on log-concavity to extend the perturbative analysis of HWS by Reichardt ('04) to the larger family of potentials with log-concave ground state. As a concrete instantiation, we use our result to extend the HWS analysis from a linear potential (which is exactly solvable) to a quadratic potential (which is no longer solvable). Our result strongly suggests the broader applicability of tunneling to convex potentials with spikes

</details>


### [196] [Genuine certification of incompatible quantum instruments through sequential communication tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23647)
*Arindam Mitra,Satyaki Manna,Debashis Saha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子仪器不兼容性的操作意义，通过经典输入输出的三方通信任务实现了半设备无关的量子仪器不兼容性认证，并证明该认证是量子优势在通信中的体现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子仪器是量子动力学的通用描述，其不兼容性是非经典性的基本体现。然而，这种不兼容性的操作意义尚未被充分揭示，需要建立其与具体通信任务之间的定量联系。

Method: 提出一类三方通信任务（发送方-中继方-接收方），推导出所有兼容仪器可实现性能的紧上界，该上界与同等维度约束下经典通信的最优性能一致，突破该上界即证明仪器不兼容。

Result: 该认证协议是"真正的"（genuine），能够认证一对仪器的不可兼容性，即使这些仪器诱导的测量和通道分别两两兼容；识别了能实现不兼容性认证的最简通信场景实例。

Conclusion: 量子仪器不兼容性的认证是量子优势在通信中的体现，且该认证不依赖于仪器诱导的测量和通道的不兼容性，是一种更基础的量子非经典性表现。

Abstract: Quantum instruments constitute the general description of quantum dynamics, encompassing both quantum measurements and quantum channels as special cases. Consequently, the incompatibility of quantum instruments represents a fundamental manifestation of nonclassicality in quantum theory. Here, we establish the operational significance of this notion by demonstrating communication tasks with classical inputs and outputs that enable the semi-device-independent certification of incompatible quantum instruments. We introduce a class of three-party communication tasks involving a sender, a relayer, and a receiver, and derive the tight upper bound of the figure of merits of these tasks achievable by all compatible instruments implemented by the relayer and this bound coincides with the optimal performance attainable in a classical communication subject to the same dimensional constraints. Violation of this bound certifies the incompatibility of the pair of quantum instruments implemented by the relayer. This identifies certification of incompatible instruments as a manifestation of quantum advantage in communication. This certification protocol is genuine as it is able to certify the incompatibility of a pair of instruments where the measurements and channels induced by the instruments are pairwise compatible and, therefore it does not depend on the incompability of measurements and channels induced by the instruments. Finally, we identify the simplest instances of our communication scenario that enable the certification of incompatible quantum instruments.

</details>


### [197] [Robust Structure Learning of $k$-local Lindbladians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23652)
*Tim Möbus,Thiago Bergamaschi,Daniel Stilck França,Cambyse Rouzé*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效协议，用于学习n量子比特上未知的k-局域Lindblad生成器。该协议仅使用乘积态制备、短时演化和单量子比特Pauli测量，无需先验知识即可估计所有哈密顿量和耗散Pauli-GKSL系数。对于固定k和有界加权相互作用强度，协议使用$\widetilde{\mathcal O}_k(\varepsilon^{-2}n^{2k}\log(1/\delta))$样本和对数多个演化时间即可达到精度$\varepsilon$。通过半定投影可将其转化为有效k-局域Lindblad生成器。在满足稳定稀疏性条件下，样本复杂度可从多项式改进为对数级别。本文还提供了鲁棒结构学习过程、模型误设保证及互补的样本复杂度下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子动力学习方法通常需要大量实验控制（如完整的量子态层析或长时演化），或仅适用于无耗散的幺正动力学。对于一般k-局域耗散量子动力学（由Lindblad主方程描述），尚缺乏在有限实验控制（仅乘积态制备、短时演化和单比特Pauli测量）下的高效学习保证。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 协议分为两步：(1) 通过短时乘积态演化后的单比特Pauli测量，估计所有Pauli-GKSL系数（哈密顿量与耗散部分）到逐项精度$\varepsilon$；(2) 通过半定规划投影将这些估计值转化为满足完全正定迹保持条件的有效k-局域Lindblad生成器。对于稀疏结构，利用稳定稀疏性条件进一步降低样本复杂度。此外提供鲁棒结构学习算法处理模型误设情况。

Result: 对于一般k-局域情形，样本复杂度为$\widetilde{\mathcal O}_k(\varepsilon^{-2}n^{2k}\log(1/\delta))$（逐项精度）和$\widetilde{\mathcal O}_k(\varepsilon^{-2}n^{4k}\log(1/\delta))$（金刚范数精度），经典后处理为多项式时间。在满足稳定稀疏性条件下，样本复杂度可降至$\widetilde{\mathcal O}_k(\varepsilon^{-2}\log(n/\delta))$（有界交度精确支撑）或对长程相互作用有类似改进。还给出了匹配的样本复杂度下界。

Conclusion: 这是首个在仅使用乘积态制备、短时演化和单比特Pauli测量这种有限实验控制下，为一般k-局域耗散量子动力学提供高效学习保证的工作。结果涵盖了非稀疏和稀疏场景，并考虑了模型误设的鲁棒性，为量子动力学层析的开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We present an efficient protocol for learning an unknown $k$-local Lindblad generator on $n$ qubits using only product-state preparations, short-time evolution, and single-qubit Pauli measurements, without prior knowledge of the interaction structure. For fixed $k$ and bounded weighted interaction strength, the protocol estimates all Hamiltonian and dissipative Pauli--GKSL coefficients to entrywise accuracy $\varepsilon$ with probability at least $1-δ$ using $\widetilde{\mathcal O}_k(\varepsilon^{-2}n^{2k}\log(1/δ))$ samples and polylogarithmically many evolution times. A semidefinite projection converts these estimates into a valid $k$-local Lindblad generator with diamond-norm error at most $\varepsilon$ using $\widetilde{\mathcal O}_k(\varepsilon^{-2}n^{4k}\log(1/δ))$ samples and polynomial-time classical postprocessing. If a suitable set of influential coefficients is supplied and satisfies a stable sparsity condition, the dependence on $n$ can improve from polynomial to logarithmic; in particular, exact supports of bounded intersection degree require only $\widetilde{\mathcal O}_k(\varepsilon^{-2}\log(n/δ))$ samples, with analogous reductions in system-size dependence for sufficiently decaying long-range interactions. We also provide a robust structure-learning procedure, extend the guarantees to model misspecification, and prove complementary sample-complexity lower bounds. To our knowledge, these are the first efficient learning guarantees for general $k$-local dissipative quantum dynamics under such limited experimental control.

</details>
